# FlagonQuest Site Export — Techniques

Raw text pulled from an old, self-hosted Google Sites export of the FlagonQuest rules — the designer's working site from several versions back, before the current single-file `index.html`/`rulebook.html` site existed. Minimal formatting: page navigation stripped, split label/value pairs and stray inline fragments rejoined, blank lines added for readability — otherwise left close to verbatim, not rewritten. Much of this predates and has since been reworked, renamed, or replaced by the current `scripts/techniques.csv`/`rulebook.md` — treat this as historical reference, not current rules. One section per source page.

---

## Agility
*Source: `agility.html` (Part 2)*

Agility

**Disciple of the Flowing Hand (Level 2)**

You have trained in the style of unarmored fighting, granting you increased mobility as you flow like water around your weighed-down opponents.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you are not wearing any armor you gain a +1 bonus to Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 3

**Silent Steps of Shadow (Level 2)**

When you don't wish to be seen you move like a phantom through the shadows; in fact, staying hidden is in your nature so much that you can easily cover for the missteps of your more clumsy allies.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever you and any number of allies would make a Stealth flip together, instead you make your Stealth flip before any allies. For each Extra Success you get on the flip you may give an ally Good Luck on their Stealth flip, although no more than once per ally per flip.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 3

**Quick Draw (Level 2)**

You have an uncanny knack for both always having just what you need right at hand and having quick fingers readying your equipment.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: It takes you 0 AP to retrieve or store an item, regardless of where the item is located.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

**Step of the Wind (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Your balance and agility are without peer, allowing you to move across terrain in ways that lesser people would think defies gravity.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: Until the end of your turn you gain a +2 bonus to Speed, and you may move across vertical surfaces or on top of surfaces such as water at your full Speed, as long as you begin and end all of your movement on otherwise stable footing.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 4

**Vanish (Level 4) [Encounter Technique]**

With a smoke bomb, great wave of a cloak, or some other obscuring act, you vanish from sight and disappear entirely from view.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Any onlookers who can see you.

**Effects**: Make a Stealth flip against an Awareness flip from each target. Each target you win against is Unaware of you for 1 round or until you declare an attack.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 5

**Lightning Stride (Level 5)**

Your athletic prowess and near-perfect coordination have given you unparalleled speed, dancing around lesser people

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your Speed is increased by 2. You have Good Luck on any flips where running speed is a factor, such as chasing or outrunning anyone.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 5, Athletics 5, Agility 5

## Awareness & Insight
*Source: `awareness-insight.html` (Part 2)*

Awareness & Insight

**Analyze Stance (Level 2)**

"Based on how far down the handle he's holding his sword and the fact his left foot is forward but at a slight angle, he's likely a practitioner of the Shiawasena Tanoshī Jikan (幸せな楽しい時間) style of swordplay. Keep your guard up and look for the downward slash; that's your opening to strike."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Any time a combatant you can see is in a Stance,, begins to enter a Stance, or begins to use a Martial Technique, you automatically recognize it and know exactly what it will do as long as its level is no greater than [half your Academics Skill Total] . If a combatant you can see applies any of their Soaks against an attack, you automatically know what those Soaks are.

As a Move action you may make an Insight attack against the Mental Defense of a combatant within [twice your Awareness Skill Total] meters. If the attack hits you learn the target's Dodge Defense, as well as their Parry Defense and relevant Skill Total with any weapons they are holding. If you flipped a Spade on the attack and it hits, you also gain a +2 bonus to Dodge and Parry Defense against any attack from the target for 1 round. You may only make such an attack against any specific target once per scene.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3, Academics 2

**Blindsight (Level 2)**

All of your senses work in perfect concert, granting you a more comprehensive perception of your surroundings.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can sense your surroundings out to [twice your Awareness Skill Total] meters, including through physical barriers. This gives you an understanding of everything within this range that is as thorough as if you could see all of it, although you do not need to rely on any specific sense to be able to perceive it.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 2, Cunning 2, Insight 1

**Distraction (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Trained in advanced infiltration methodology, you have a trick up your sleeve for every situation. When you skulk into a guarded area, you know just how to draw their attention in a way that allows you to handily pass them.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Choose a point within 20 meters; this Technique targets all creatures within 5 meters of that point.

**Effects**: Make an Insight attack against the Mental Defense of each target, and if you flip a Spade ~. Each target hit by the attack is distracted for 2 rounds, during which they have Disadvantage on any flips to notice anything other than the distraction.

A creature stops being distracted if their attention is drawn away from the distraction by something or someone else. If a creature is specifically paying attention to something else when the Insight attack is made it is immune to the effects of this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 2, Insight 2, Stealth 3

**Instantaneous Observation (Level 3) [Encounter Technique]**

With a moment of extreme concentration you can perfectly survey the room you're in, taking in the scene with a glimpse.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: Everything within [Awareness Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Awareness flip, for which if you flip a Spade you gain a +1 bonus, and you learn about the target area as though you had thoroughly searched it with your result. For closed containers and other objects that cannot be feasibly searched, you don't gain exact knowledge about their contents but instead more general knowledge about the object and its possible contents - what it is most likely to contain, whether or not a door or drawer is locked, and so on.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 4

## Battle Features
*Source: `battle-features.html` (Part 2)*

Battle Features

Battle Features

For Battle Strike you may choose from the following features (some may apply additional prerequisites to the Technique):

**Precision**: The Technique's Power is increased by 1.

**Outmaneuver**: If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the Technique's Power is increased by 1.

**Wind Slash**: For the attack the chosen weapon's range is increased by

[Power] × [Strength]

.

**Disengage**: After the attack is resolved, Shift [Power] meters.

**Lodestone**: If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Victorious Blow**: If the attack Downs the target, draw a card.

**Forceful Assault**: If a target Parries or is hit by the attack, they are Harried.

**Battering Blow**: If a target Parries the attack they gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Dazing**: If the attack hits the target is Dazed [Power] times for 1 round.

**Hamstring**: If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [Power] times for 1 round.

**Compelling**: If the attack hits, the target is Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Magebane**: If the attack hits, the target is Enervated [Power] times for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 1

**Dark Crescent**: If the attack hits, the target is Vulnerable for [Power] rounds.

**Elemental**: When you choose this feature, choose one of the following elemental damage types: Fire, Frost, or Shadow. The attack has up to

2 + [Power]

Physical damage converted into the chosen damage type.

**Guarded**: You gain a + [Power] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

**Invigorate**: You gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Opportunist**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action when a creature moves from a space within your melee range to a space out of your melee range.

**Prereqs**: Cunning 2

**Riposte**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action when you Parry an attack while wielding no more than a single one-handed melee weapon. If you do, the attack targets the creature that made the Parried attack if it is in range of your one-handed melee weapon.

**Prereqs**: Cunning 2

Fighter's Features

For Fighter's Strike you may choose from the list of Battle Features above, as well as the following features (some may apply additional prerequisites to the Technique):

**Unbalance**: If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone.

**Revenging**: If the attack hits, the target gains [your stacks of Wounded] stacks of Bleeding.

**Viper's Sting**: You may use the Technique as a Move instead of an Action, and if you do your Strength is halved for this attack.

**Pinpoint Flash**: Before making the attack you Teleport up to

2

× [Power] meters. You may consider your position directly between the origin and destination of the Teleport for the purpose of determining the target of the attack. If there are no such targets then the attack is not made.

**Prereqs**: (Athletics, Acrobatics, or Elementalism) 3

**Counterstrike**: You may use the Technique as a Reaction when you Dodge or Parry an attack while your attacker could be targeted by the Technique, and if you do your Strength is halved for this attack.

**Hemorrhage**: The attack ignores Armor Soak. If the attack would cause the target to lose Health, instead they gain half that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2

**Shockwave**: If the attack hits, you may make an attack using the chosen weapon against the Dodge Defense of another creature adjacent to the target. Your Strength is halved for the attack, and all features that apply to the first attack apply to this attack as well.

**Whirlwind**: If your chosen weapon is a melee weapon, the attack instead targets any number of creatures in range of your weapon, you may not gamble on it, and your Strength is halved for this attack.

**Scattershot**: If your chosen weapon is a ranged weapon, choose a space within range of your weapon and the attack instead targets all creatures in that space and a 2 meter cone behind that space, you may not gamble on it, and your Strength is halved for this attack.

Warlord's Features

For Warlord's Strike you may choose from the lists of Battle and Fighter's Features above, as well as the following features (some may apply additional prerequisites to the Technique):

**Inescapable Claw**: If the attack misses you may make a second attack using the chosen weapon against the target, and all features that apply to the first attack apply to this attack as well.

**Stagger**: If the attack hits, the target loses a Move on each of their turns for [Power] rounds.

**Execute**: If the attack hits, after the attack is resolved the target gains a number of Bleeds equal to half of the total amount of Health they are missing.

**Battlemaster**: The Technique's Power is increased by 2.

**Fighting Spirit**: You may spend an extra 1 Willpower to use the Technique. If you do, if your Strength would be halved for the attack instead it is not.

**Ten Ton Hammer**: You may spend an extra 1 Willpower to use the Technique. If you do, the bonus damage from gambling is doubled for the attack.

**Cloak and Dagger**: You may spend an extra 1 Willpower to use the Technique. If you do, before making the attack, make a Legerdemain flip opposed by the Awareness of each target. Any targets who lose the opposed flip may not Dodge or Parry the attack, and if you flipped a Spade those targets may not take any Reaction to the attack.

## Battle Magic
*Source: `battle-magic.html` (Part 2)*

Battle magic is a blanket term for a wide variety of elemental magic used for combat and war. It is primarily used to lay waste to foes with fire, ice, and shadow, although some variants exist that work this magic in more subtle ways to cripple and disarm foes. Battle magic is the art of elementalism in its purest form: destructive, terrifying, and effective.

You may apply multiple copies of a feature, unless stated otherwise

Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique

**Apprentice's Blast (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A basic form of standard battle magic, one of the first "real" spells a budding apprentice learns. This method allows the caster to channel primal elemental energies, although without the fine control that more advanced practitioners are capable of.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose Dodge or Bodily Defense, as well as one basic battle magic feature to modify the spell attack.

Make a Elementalism spell attack against the target's Defense that you chose. If the attack hits, it deals [your Mind] Fire damage.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2

**Elementalist's Blast (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As long as mortalkind has known how to work magic, they have used it to kill their enemies, including each other. You carry on this proud tradition, employing Elementalism magic to blast foes with fire, lightning, cold, or shadow energy. These forms are so fundamental to the school of magic that rather than being called by a specific ritual magic users learn to shape them in a variety of ways, depending on the tastes of the caster. Most who learn the art of Elementalism learn this magic in some form as part of their training, although many who pursue a more peaceful life only really use it to light hearthfires or chill drinks.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose Dodge, or Bodily Defense, as well as two basic battle magic features to modify the spell attack.

Make a Elementalism spell attack against the target's Defense that you chose. If the attack hits, it deals [your Mind] Fire damage.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Tormenting Curse (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variation on the standard battle magic that elementalists traditionally learn, by manipulating the energy in a manner that is subtle to most you can cause it to affect the victim differently. Rather than simply trying to cause harm, practitioners of this magic instead cripple their enemies by freezing their joints or sapping the strength from their bodies.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose Dodge or Bodily Defense, as well as four basic battle magic features to modify the spell attack.

Make a Elementalism spell attack against the target's Defense that you chose.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Warmage's Standard-Issue (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As students of elemental magic grow in ability, they improve on staple incantations. These require much more power to realize, but a magic user capable of unleashing such destruction will give virtually any foe pause in combat. Including such classic incarnations as rings of blistering frost, funnels of fire, and spears of shadow that seem to steal the vitality of their victims, there are as many flavors of war magic as there are upstart mercenaries foolish enough to challenge seasoned battlemages.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it and then choose one basic battle magic feature. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Blast Line (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage packs the magical energy into a dense payload that sears through multiple foes.

**Target**: All creatures in a line [half your Elementalism Skill Total] meters long, from your space.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Channel (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage establishes a magical link to a foe and channels a stream of elemental energies to destroy them.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique. Each time the spell attack hits, on your next turn you may spend 2 AP to make an identical spell attack against the target. You may not do this if the target is farther than [Elementalism Skill Total] meters past this Spell's range.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Funnel (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage spreads out the force of the blast to bathe everyone in front of them in destructive magic.

**Target**: All creatures within a 2-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Nova (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage weaves the energy of the spell in a circle around them, sending a wave of destruction outward.

**Target**: All adjacent creatures.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Elementalist's Artillery (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of elementalist magic are capable of unleashing overwhelming amounts of energy before their unlucky enemies can react. Annihilating entire squadrons of soldiers and laying waste to chunks of battlefield, spellcasters who can leverage this caliber of power are worth their weight in gold on any elite military force.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 3 Battle Magic Technique and one expert battle magic feature. This Spell functions the same as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

Basic Battle Magic Features

**Destructive**: The spell attack deals an extra 1 damage.

**Arcane Chant**: You may spend an extra 1 AP to cast the spell. If you do, choose another one of the spell's basic battle magic features and apply it to the spell twice.

**Lance**: The spell attack is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters. If the spell attack affects an area, you may choose any point within the spell attack's range to be your location for the purposes of determining that area. If the spell attack is already ranged, instead its range is increased by [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Abjuring**: After the spell attack is resolved, you gain 1 stack of [attack's damage type] Ward.

**Rebuking**: If the spell attack hits, the target is Pushed up to 2 meters in a direction of your choice.

**Sanguine**: The spell attack deals an extra 3 damage. If it would cause the target to lose Health, instead they gain that many stacks of Bleeding. You may only apply one copy of this feature.

**Barraging**: If the attack hits, or if the target Dodges the attack, they are Harried once.

**Frigid**: If the attack hits the target is Slowed once. When you choose this feature, you may change the attack's damage type to Frost.

**Glacier**: After the attack, every space occupied by or adjacent to any targeted creature - or, for Spells that target an area, every space in or adjacent to that area - gains 1 level of Difficult Terrain which lasts for the encounter. When you choose this feature, you may change the attack's damage type to Frost.

**Corrupting**: If the attack hits the target gains 1 stack of Vulnerable. When you choose this feature, you may change the attack's damage type to Shadow.

**Necrotic**: If the attack hits, before the attack deals any damage, the target gains 2 stacks of Necrotic. When you choose this feature, you may change the attack's damage type to Shadow.

**Draw Power**: If the attack Downs the target, they are instantly killed and you draw a card. When you choose this feature, you may change the attack's damage type to Shadow.

Expert Battle Magic Features

**Mastery of Fundamentals**: Choose two basic battle magic features to modify the spell attack.

**Convection**: You have Good Luck on the spell attack.

**Swath**: You determine targets for the spell attack as though you were casting it two times, aimed separately, except you may only target any particular creature once.

## Battle Magic
*Source: `battle-magic_1.html` (Part 1)*

Battle magic is a blanket term for a wide variety of elemental magic used for combat and war. It is primarily used to lay waste to foes with fire, ice, and shadow, although some variants exist that work this magic in more subtle ways to cripple and disarm foes. Battle magic is the art of elementalism in its purest form: destructive, terrifying, and effective.

Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once. Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique. Each time you learn one of these Techniques you learn a separate copy, based on the features chosen.

Battle Magic Techniques

**Apprentice's Blast (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A basic form of standard battle magic, one of the first "real" spells a budding apprentice learns. This method allows the caster to channel primal elemental energies, although without the fine control that more advanced practitioners are capable of.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack the Spell's Power is increased by 1. If the attack hits, it deals [your Mind] Fire damage.

When you learn this Technique you choose a

basic battle magic feature

to modify the spell attack.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2

**Elementalist's Blast (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As long as mortalkind has known how to work magic, they have used it to kill their enemies, including each other. You carry on this proud tradition, employing Elementalism magic to blast foes with fire, lightning, cold, or shadow energy. These forms are so fundamental to the school of magic that rather than being called by a specific ritual magic users learn to shape them in a variety of ways, depending on the tastes of the caster. Most who learn the art of Elementalism learn this magic in some form as part of their training, although many who pursue a more peaceful life only really use it to light hearthfires or chill drinks.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack the Spell's Power is increased by 1. If the attack hits, it deals [your Mind] Fire damage.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

basic battle magic features

to modify the spell attack.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Tormenting Curse (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variation on the standard battle magic that elementalists traditionally learn, by manipulating the energy in a manner that is subtle to most you can cause it to affect the victim differently. Rather than simply trying to cause harm, practitioners of this magic instead cripple their enemies by freezing their joints or sapping the strength from their bodies.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent creature.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack the Spell's Power is increased by 1. When you learn this Technique you choose five

basic battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, but it cannot deal any damage.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Warmage's Standard-Issue (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As students of elemental magic grow in ability, they improve on staple incantations. These require much more power to realize, but a magic user capable of unleashing such destruction will give virtually any foe pause in combat. Including such classic incarnations as rings of blistering frost, funnels of fire, and spears of shadow that seem to steal the vitality of their victims, there are as many flavors of war magic as there are upstart mercenaries foolish enough to challenge seasoned battlemages.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it and then choose one

basic battle magic feature

. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Blast Line (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage packs the magical energy into a dense payload that sears through multiple foes.

**Target**: All creatures in a line [half your Elementalism Skill Total] meters long, from your space.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it and then choose one

basic battle magic feature

. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it and with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Channel (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage establishes a magical link to a foe and channels a constant stream of elemental energies to destroy them.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it and with this Technique's specified target(s). If the spell attack hits, on your next turn you may spend 2 AP to make an identical spell attack against the target.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Funnel (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage spreads out the force of the blast to bathe everyone in front of them in destructive magic.

**Target**: All creatures within a 2-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it and with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Warmage's Nova (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage weaves the energy of the spell in a circle around them, sending a wave of destruction outward.

**Target**: All adjacent creatures.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 2 Battle Magic Technique as though you were learning it. This Spell functions as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it and with this Technique's specified target(s).

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Elementalist's Artillery (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of elementalist magic are capable of unleashing overwhelming amounts of energy before their unlucky enemies can react. Annihilating entire squadrons of soldiers and laying waste to chunks of battlefield, spellcasters who can leverage this caliber of power are worth their weight in gold on any elite military force.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 3 Battle Magic Technique and one

expert battle magic feature

. This Spell functions the same as the chosen Technique, except with the chosen feature applied to it.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

Battle Magic Features

Basic Features

Modifications

**Focused**: The spell attack deals an extra 1 damage. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Gather Energy**: You may spend an extra 1 AP to cast the Spell. If you do, choose either to increase the spell attack's damage or the Spell's Power by 1.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 2

**Amplified**: The Spell's Power is increased by 1. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Lance**: The spell attack is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters. If the spell attack affects an area, you may choose any point within the spell attack's range to be your location for the purposes of determining that area. You may choose this feature multiple times. If the spell attack is already ranged, instead its range is increased by [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Miasma**: The spell attack is against Bodily Defense instead of Dodge Defense.

**Ethereal**: The spell attack is against Mental Defense instead of Dodge Defense.

Bolstering

**Abjuring**: After the spell attack is resolved, you gain [Power] Resist of the type of damage the attack dealt, which lasts for 1 round.

**Draw Power**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack Downs the target they are instantly killed and you draw a card.

Debuffing

**Rebuking**: If the spell attack hits, the target is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Sanguine**: The spell attack deals an extra 3 damage. If it would cause the target to lose Health, instead they gain that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Frigid**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and if the attack hits the target is Slowed [Power] times.

**Barraging**: If the attack hits or if the target Dodges the attack, they are Harried [Power] times.

**Glacier**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and after the attack every space occupied by or adjacent to any targeted creature gains [Power] levels of Difficult Terrain which lasts for the scene.

**Corrupting**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack hits the target gains [Power] stacks of Vulnerable.

**Necrotic**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack hits the target gains [Power] stacks of Necrotic which last for the scene.

Expert Features

**Mastery of Fundamentals**: Choose two

basic battle magic features

to modify the spell attack.

**Gainsay**: You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell as an Interrupt (an attack is declared against you, where the attacker could be targeted by this Spell). If you do, the spell attack must target the attacker.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3

**Swath**: When you determine targets for the spell attack you may do so as though you were casting the Spell two separate times, and the spell attack targets all creatures those two copies would have targeted.

**Elemental Storm**: You may spend an extra 1 Focus when you cast the Spell. If you do, after the attack is resolved a storm begins to appear in a 2-meter-radius circle centered on one of the targets of the spell attack; this is a very obvious effect. At the start of your next turn the storm appears in that area and lasts until the end of the scene, and whenever a creature starts their turn within, moves into, or is Pushed into the storm, you may make another spell attack against them.

**Cyclical Energy**: After you cast the Spell, you may regain the use of a level 1 or level 2 Spell Encounter Technique.

**Convection**: If the attack does not hit, the target takes

1 + [Mind]

Fire damage.

**Rime**: After you cast the Spell you gain a + [Power] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round. If the attack hits, the target is Unbalanced for 1 round.

**Dark Servitude**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage. If the attack Downs the target they are instantly killed, you heal 1 Health, and the target's body is Summoned as a

reanimated corpse as per the Creature Statistics page.

You may give this Reanimated Corpse a turn in combat with 1 Ap, but if you do you may not use its Shambling Horde ability. The corpse remains reanimated for the scene.

## Battle Magic
*Source: `battle-magic_2.html` (Part 1)*

Battle magic is

Unless otherwise stated, you may only select a feature once.

**Apprentice's Blast (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A basic form of standard battle magic, one of the first "real" spells a budding apprentice learns. This method allows the caster to channel primal elemental energies, although without the fine control that more advanced practitioners are capable of.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within close-range.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense. The spell attack deals [Will] Fire damage. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 2.

When you learn this Technique you choose one

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**Battle Magic (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As long as mortalkind has known how to work magic, they have used it to kill their enemies, including each other. You carry on this proud tradition, employing Elementalism magic to blast foes with fire, lightning, cold, or shadow energy. These forms are so fundamental to the school of magic that rather than being called by a specific ritual magic users learn to shape them in a variety of ways, depending on the tastes of the caster. Most who learn the art of Elementalism learn this magic in some form as part of their training, although many who pursue a more peaceful life only really use it to light hearthfires or chill drinks.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within close-range.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 2. The spell attack deals

1 + [Will]

Fire damage.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**War Magic - Standard Issue (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As students of elemental magic grow in ability, they improve on staple incantations. These require much more power to realize, but a magic user capable of unleashing such destruction will give virtually any foe pause in combat. Including such classic incarnations as rings of blistering frost, funnels of fire, and spears of shadow that seem to steal the vitality of their victims, there are as many flavors of war magic as there are upstart mercenaries foolish enough to challenge seasoned battlemages.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within close-range.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 2. The spell attack deals

1 + [Will]

Fire damage.

When you learn this Technique you choose three

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**War Magic - Blast Line (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage packs the magical energy into a dense payload that sears through multiple foes.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: All creatures in a line [half your Elementalism Skill Total] meters long, from your space.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details).

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against each target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 2. The spell attack deals [Will] Fire damage.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**War Magic - Channel (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A variant of the standard war magic, the battlemage establishes a magical link to a foe and channels a constant stream of elemental energies to destroy them.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 2. The spell attack deals [Will] Fire damage, and if it hits you may choose to establish a link to the target. Each turn you may either end the link or spend a Main action to make another spell attack against the target. At the start of your turn if you are unable to target the target creature, the link ends.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

(this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack, and the resulting spell attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

.

**Elementalist's Artillery (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of elementalist magic are capable of unleashing overwhelming amounts of energy before their unlucky enemies can react. Annihilating entire squadrons of soldiers and laying waste to chunks of battlefield, spellcasters who can leverage this caliber of power are worth their weight in gold on any elite military force.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Level 3 War Magic spell and one

elementalist's artillery feature

. This spell functions the same as the chosen War Magic spell, except with the chosen feature applied to it.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell

Battle Magic Features

**Focused**: The spell attack deals an extra 1 damage. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Amplified**: The Spell's Power is increased by 2.

**Lance**: The spell attack is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters. If the spell attack affects an area, you may choose any point within the spell attack's range to be your location for the purposes of determining that area. You may choose this feature multiple times. If the spell attack is already ranged, instead its range is increased by [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Gainsay**: You may cast the spell as a Counter - you are hit by an attack where the attacker could be targeted by this Spell. If you do, the spell must target the attacker, and it resolves at the same time as the triggering attack.

**Abjuring**: After the spell attack is resolved, you may gain [Power] Elemental Soak of the type of damage the attack dealt.

**Ethereal**: When you cast the spell, you may choose either Mental Defense or Bodily Defense. If you do, the spell attack is against the chosen defense instead of Dodge Defense.

**Sanguine**: The spell attack deals an extra 4 damage. If it would cause the target to lose Health, instead they gain half that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Barraging**: The attack is instead against the target's Dodge or Parry Defense, and if they apply either Defense against the attack they are Harried an extra [Power] times.

**Brainfreeze**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and if the attack hits the target is Dazed for [Power] rounds.

**Frigid**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and if the attack hits the target is Slowed [Power] times for 1 round.

**Glacier**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and after the attack every space occupied by or adjacent to a targeted creature gains [Power] levels of Difficult Terrain.

**Necrotic**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack hits the target gains [Power] stacks of Necrotic for 2 rounds.

**Corrupting**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack hits the target gains [Power] stacks of Vulnerable for 2 rounds.

**Draw Power**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage, and if the attack Downs the target they are instantly killed and you draw a card.

**Rebuking**: If the spell attack hits, the target is Pushed back [twice the spell's Power] meters.

Elementalist's Artillery Features

**Mastery of Fundamentals**: Choose two

battle magic features

to modify the spell attack.

**Swath**: When you determine targets for the spell you may do so twice, as though you had cast the spell twice, although a creature cannot be targeted more than once by the spell.

**Cyclical Energy**: When you cast the spell, regain the use of a level 1 or level 2 Spell Encounter Technique.

**Tendrils of Power**: The spell's Power is increased by 2.

**Convection**: If the attack does not hit, the target takes

1 + [Will]

Fire damage.

**Concussive**: If the attack hits, you may choose to either knock the target Prone or knock them Off-Balance for 1 round.

**Rime**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Frost damage, and if the attack hits the target is Snared for [half the spell's Power] rounds.

**Elemental Storm**: You may spend 1 Soul to cast the spell. If you do, after the attack is resolved a storm begins to appear in a 2-meter-radius circle centered on one of the targets of the spell, which is obvious to anyone who can see the area. At the start of your next turn the storm appears in that area and lasts until the end of the scene, and whenever a creature starts their turn within, moves into, or is Pushed into the storm, as a Non-action you may make another spell attack against them.

**Dark Servitude**: You may have the spell attack deal its damage as Shadow damage. You may spend 1 Soul to cast the spell. If you do, if the attack Downs the target they are instantly killed, you heal 1 Health, and the target's body is Summoned as a

reanimated corpse as per the Creature Statistics page.

You may give this Reanimated Corpse a turn in combat as an Extra action, but if you do you may not use its Shambling Horde ability. The corpse remains reanimated until destroyed or until the next sunrise.

## Blessing Magic
*Source: `blessing-magic.html` (Part 1)*

Blessing Magic lets you choose from different boons and enhancements to give yourself or one of your companions. Each feature represents a different blessing you can provide, or an improvement to a blessing, and with higher level spells you can choose a great number of options to combine into an amazing supportive spell.

Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once. Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique. Each time you learn one of these Techniques you learn a separate copy, based on the features chosen.

Blessing Magic Techniques

**Blessing Magic (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the cornerstones of theurgist magical theory, by way of beckoning spirits and infusing essence you can bolster a comrade to superhuman(oid) heights.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Special**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose five features from

basic blessing magic features

to modify the Spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Infusion Magic (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Further augmenting an ally's abilities with spiritual energy, your expertise allows for a greater range of blessings.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Special**: This Spell has a Power equal to [half your Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose six features from

advanced blessing magic features

and/or

basic blessing magic features

to modify the Spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4

**Transcendent Blessing (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A conduit for theurgy magicks, you can call spirits and lay mantles of magic on allies that lift them up and push them to unbelievable achievements.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Special**: This Spell has a Power equal to [half your Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose one feature from

expert blessing magic features

and five features from

advanced blessing magic features

and/or

basic blessing magic features

to modify the Spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5

Blessing Magic Features

Basic Features

Modifications

**Fortified**: The Spell's Power is increased by 1. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Enduring**: The duration of all effects of the Spell are increased by 1 round. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Reach**: The Spell is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Quickened**: You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell.

**Coordinated**: You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell as an Interrupt (the target's turn starts).

**In Time**: You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell as an Interrupt (an attack hits the target).

Defensive

**Shield**: The target gains [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round or until an attack or other effect causes the target to lose Health, whichever happens first.

**Elemental Ward**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. For 1 round your target gains +1 Resist against the chosen damage type; if the spell's Power is at least 3 this increases to +2 Resist, and if the spell's Power is at least 5 this increases to +3 Resist.

**Step of Air**: The target gains a + [Power] bonus to Speed for 1 round.

**Invigoration**: The target ignores up to [Power] stacks of Wounded for 1 round.

**Freedom**: Remove up to [Power] total stacks of your choice of Slowed and/or Snared from the target. If the target is Prone they may stand up immediately.

**Sharpen Instincts**: Remove up to [Power] total stacks of your choice of Crippled and/or Weakened from the target.

Offensive

**Retribution**: For 1 round, if an attack causes the target to lose Health, the attacker gains a stack of Bleeding.

**River's Might**: For 1 round, when the target hits a creature with a weapon attack that creature is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Guided Hand**: For 1 round, the target may re-flip any cards 4 or lower on attack flips. Once the target has re-flipped [Power] cards this way the effect ends.

Advanced Features

**Steal Fate**: For 1 round, if the target Downs a creature then the target draws a card. Once the target has drawn [Power] cards this way the effect ends.

**Inspired Focus**: For 1 round, if the target spends Focus then they draw cards equal to the amount of Focus spent. Once the target has drawn [Power] cards this way the effect ends.

**Elemental Enhancement**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. For 1 round, the next [Power] weapon attacks the target hits with are empowered. These attacks deals their damage as damage of the chosen type, and if they would cause a creature to lose Health instead the creature gains that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Balance**: Remove any stacks of Unbalanced and Harried from the target.

Expert Features

**Simplicity**: When you choose this feature, choose two features from

basic blessing magic features

to modify the Spell.

**Mass Blessing**: The spell may target an additional target.

**Power Overwhelming**: For 1 round, any Techniques or other abilities the target uses have their Power increased by 1. Once this has successfully applied to [Power] abilities, this effect ends.

Blessing Magic lets you choose from different boons and enhancements to give yourself or one of your companions. Each feature represents a different blessing you can provide, or an improvement to a blessing, and with higher level spells you can choose a great number of options to combine into an amazing supportive spell. Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once.

**Blessing Magic (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the cornerstones of theurgist magical theory, by way of beckoning spirits and infusing essence you can bolster a comrade to superhuman heights.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Special**: This Spell has a Power of

[half your Theurgy Skill Total] (this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

Blessing Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn. Your target gains benefits based on the features chosen.

[half your Theurgy Skill Total] (this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

and

Blessing Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn. Your target gains benefits based on the features chosen.

**Transcendent Blessing (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A conduit for theurgy magicks, you can call spirits and lay mantles of magic on allies that lift them up and push them to unbelievable achievements.

**Action**: Main.

[half your Theurgy Skill Total] (this affects some of its features, see features for details)

.

Transcendence Features

and three features from the lists of

Infusion Features

and

Blessing Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn. Your target gains benefits based on the features chosen.

Blessing Features

**Quickening**: The spell may be cast either as an Extra action, as a Counter - the target's turn starts, or as a Counter - the target is hit by an attack.

**Reach**: The spell is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Fortified**: The duration of one of the chosen features is increased by 1 round. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Shield**: The target gains [Power] temporary Health for 1 round. This temporary Health goes away after an attack or other effect causes the target to lose Health.

**Elemental Ward**: Choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. For 1 round your target gains + [Power] Elemental Soak against the chosen damage type.

**Elemental Enhancement**: Choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. For 1 round, if your target hits with a weapon attack it deals up to [Power] of its damage as damage of the chosen type.

**Retribution**: For 1 round, if an attack causes the target to lose Health the attacker takes

2 + [Power]

Spirit damage.

**Invigoration**: The target ignores up to [Power] stacks of Wounded and up to [Power] stacks of Weakened for 1 round.

**River's Might**: For 1 round, when the target hits with a weapon attack the target of their attack is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Step of Air**: The target gains a + [Power] bonus to Speed for 1 round.

**Surefoot**: The target ignores [Power] levels of Difficult Terrain for 1 round.

Infusion Features

**Resurgent Lifeforce**: Whenever the target loses any non-temporary Health they gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round; this effect lasts for 1 round.

**Steal Fate**: For 1 round, if the target Downs a creature then the target draws a card.

**Balance**: The target re-flips any cards 4 or lower for 1 round.

**Guidance**: For 1 round, the target may gain Good Luck on a flip of their choice; once they do, this effect ends.

Transcendence Features

**Mass Blessing**: The spell may target an additional target.

**Enduring Blessing**: The duration of each chosen feature is increased by 1 round.

**Overbearing Blessing**: The spell's Power is instead [your Theurgy Skill Total] .

## Blessing Magic
*Source: `blessing-magic_2.html` (Part 1)*

Blessing Magic lets you choose from different boons and enhancements to give yourself or one of your companions. Each feature represents a different blessing you can provide, or an improvement to a blessing, and with higher level spells you can choose a great number of options to combine into an amazing supportive spell.

You may apply multiple copies of a feature, unless stated otherwise

Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique

**Blessing Magic (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the cornerstones of theurgist magical theory, by way of beckoning spirits and infusing essence you can bolster a comrade to superhuman(oid) heights.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose four basic blessing features. These determine what the Spell does when cast.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Infusion Magic (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Further augmenting an ally's abilities with spiritual energy, your expertise allows for a greater range of blessings.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose five advanced and/or basic blessing features. These determine what the Spell does when cast.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4

**Transcendent Blessing (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A conduit for theurgy magicks, you can call spirits and lay mantles of magic on allies that lift them up and push them to unbelievable achievements.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose one expert blessing feature and four advanced and/or basic blessing features. These determine what the Spell does when cast.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5

Basic Blessing Features

**Reach**: The Spell is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Shielding**: The target gains 1 stack of Protected.

**Turn Elements**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. After you make the attack, you gain 1 stack of [chosen Element] Ward.

**Step of Air**: The target is Hasted once.

**Invigoration**: The target is Bolstered once.

**Retribution**: For 2 rounds, if an attack causes the target to lose Health, the attacker gains a stack of Bleeding.

**River's Might**: For 2 rounds, when the target hits a creature with a weapon attack that creature is Pushed back 2 meters.

**Steady**: Remove a stack of either Slowed or Crippled from the target.

**Embolden**: Remove a stack of either Taunted or Frightened from the target.

**Elemental Enhancement**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. For 2 rounds, the next weapon attack the target hits with deals it damage as that type instead, but deals -1 damage. This can apply to one attack for each copy of this feature.

Advanced Blessing Features

**Quickened**: The Spell only takes 1 AP to cast.

**Alacrity**: The target may adjust their position in turn order by 1.

**Steal Fate**: For 2 rounds, when the target Downs a creature, they draw a card. They may only draw 1 card for each copy of this feature.

**Inspired Focus**: For 2 rounds, when the target spends any Focus, they draw that many cards, but only up to 1 card for each copy of this feature.

Expert Blessing Features

**Simplicity**: When you choose this feature, choose two advanced and/or basic blessing magic features to modify the Spell.

**Mass Blessing**: The spell may target an additional target.

**Guided Hand**: For 2 rounds, the target has Good Luck on the next attack they make.
