## Brawl & Meditation
*Source: `brawl-meditation.html` (Part 1)*

Brawl & Meditation

**Placeholder's Eldritch Enlightenment (Level 1) [Discipline]**

"So it was that was that the arcanist Placeholder dealt a final, decisive strike to the Master of the School of the Fifth Dragon Fist, completing the trial that had been laid before him. The School's council gathered and summarily declared Placeholder worthy of learning the secrets of the Fifth Dragon, notably the final chakra that was the missing piece of one of Placeholder's great theses of power. With it unlocked, and his thesis complete, Placeholder had mastered the pathways which allowed him to freely manipulate energy between his corporeal being and his environment, forging a unified practice of the arcane and meditative arts."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. IV

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may treat Disciplines you know as though they were Spells for any use of Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana.

**Prereqs**: Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana, Meditation 1

**Shugen Style - Brilliant Bolt (Level 1) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Concentrating a small fragment your ki into a hand or other limb, you launch a salvo of energy at an enemy.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Parry or Dodge Defense. The attack deals

1 + [Mind]

Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 2

**Snake Style - Chainbreaker (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"One of the most historically esteemed practitioners of the martial tradition of the Snake, Master Raha, is said to have been imprisoned by a local warring nation when he was a young man. Though he was strong in the art of the Snake, he was not a Master then, and he toiled in labor camps for a few long years under their oppressive regime. Every night and morning he meditated, looking for guidance and a way to free himself and others. One day he grew strong enough to break his shackles, and using them as a weapon he defeated scores of guards and freed the entire camp. Once he returned to his monastery he taught of what he had learned, how one's limbs flow outward like chains to strike many places at once, and the art of the Snake flourished for his wisdom."

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 2, Acrobatics 1

**Bear Style - Shattering Slam (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Throwing the weight of your entire body into some poor soul, you slam into them with enough force to rattle their bones.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack is Parried the target gains a stack of Bleeding, and if the attack hits it deals no damage and instead the target gains [half your Might Skill Total] stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Might 2

**Demon Style - Ripjaw Gambit (Level 2) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

By using your own spirit's energy to fuel your attacks, you devastate your victim but leave yourself open to counterattack.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack this Technique's Power is increased by 1. If the attack hits, choose a number up to [Power] ; the attack deals its damage as Shadow damage, deals an extra [chosen number] damage, and for 1 round if an effect would deal damage to you it deals an extra [chosen number] damage.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Might 1, Meditation 1

**Great Old Oak Style - Tree Withstands the Storm (Level 2) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

The basic movement of the style, it teaches its practitioners to stand strong, resist all that comes, then defeat their enemies once they tire.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Effects**: For [Power] rounds, ignore up to 1 stack of Harried on yourself.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Athletics 1, Meditation 1

**Ignore Pain (Level 2) [Discipline]**

Sometimes there are things that need doing, and even if your leg is broken it isn't a good enough reason to stop.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Remove all stacks of Crippled, Slowed, or Weakened from yourself. For the scene you ignore any stacks of Wounded you have.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Ki Style - Spirit Seeker (Level 2) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

Grasping the ephemeral threads of energy that flow around another's spirit you pull yourself towards them, seeming to flash and blink through the air as you do so.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within [Power] meters.

**Effects**: Teleport to any unoccupied space adjacent to the target, then make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Meditation 2

**Shugen Style - Disintegrate Vitality (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Forming your energy into a torrent of barbs and unleashing them upon an enemy, you shred their ki and cause their life force to drain from them.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target gains [Power] stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Shugen Style - Iron Skin (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

As an attack weaves its way past your guard, you concentrate your energy into your skin to deflect the force of the blow.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you are hit by an attack, before the attack is resolved).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts until the attack is resolved.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Shugen Style - Unbreakable Spirit Meditation (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Engaging your foe in a battle of wills, you send them reeling with your iron resolve.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Frightened of you.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Snake Style - Crashing Leg Sweep (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You take advantage of an opponent's momentary distraction to trip them, ending their attempts to flee in a tangle of limbs.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a creature within range of a close-range Brawl weapon you are wielding begins to move or be Pushed to a space outside of that range)

**Target**: The creature moving or being Pushed.

**Effects**: Make an attack with a close-range Brawl weapon against the target. If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone, and if they attempted to move their movement is cancelled and they are treated as having moved their full Speed for the current action.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Acrobatics  2

**Bear Style - Boulder Toss (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You grab someone and launch them away from you, often into a wall or one of their friends.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you successfully grapple a creature).

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You end the grapple and throw the target up to [half your Might Skill Total] meters in a straight line, then the target falls Prone in the space in which they land. If the target would be thrown through a space occupied by a creature, make a Might attack against that creature's Dodge Defense, and if it hits then the target strikes the creature. If the target is thrown into an object, if the object is sturdy enough to stop the target then the target strikes the object, otherwise the object is knocked aside, damaged, and otherwise affected as is appropriate, and the target continues onward.

If the target strikes a creature or object, the attack deals

2 + [your Body]

Physical damage to both the target and that creature or object, and the target falls Prone in a space adjacent to that creature or object.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Might 3

**Corrupted Blood (Level 3) [Discipline]**

One belief that many civilizations across the land share is that, for a variety of reasons, they consider blood to be important, and perhaps even sacred. This is for good reason - blood sustains living things and is in many ways a conduit for the power a person can wield, and for this very reason that blood magic is so feared. It is also for this reason that some monks perfected this technique, training control over their body's energy to the point where they can corrupt their own blood, turning it to poison on par with that of the most venomous of beasts.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 2 non-temporary Health.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation flip against difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose either

Deadly Poison

,

Graven Poison

, or

Wasting Poison

, and you draw enough of your blood to create a fresh dose of the chosen poison. For each Extra Success on the flip you may choose to either increase the poison's Difficulty by 2 or increase its Potency by 1.

You may spend 1 AP to use this Technique, and instead you draw the blood with a weapon or piece of ammunition and the poison then coats that weapon instead of collecting into a container.

You are immune to poison created with your blood through this Technique. 24 hours after the poison's creation it loses its potency, becoming a mundane vial of blood.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Demon Style - Hand of Defilement (Level 3) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

A corruption of traditional hand-to-hand

ki

maneuvers, by twisting your body's energy before unleashing it on your victim you pollute their vitality and drive them farther from salvation.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack this Technique's Power is increased by 1. If the attack hits, the target gains [Power] stacks of Necrotic which last for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Meditation 2, Might 1

**Great Old Oak Style - Branch in the Wind (Level 3) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

After the style had been developed and passed down for a handful of generations, one group of disciples took it upon themselves to improve the practice. To contrast against the steady, predictable nature of the style they learned to manifest its defensive power by means of a quick counterattack that provided much-needed variation in strategy. Some historians argue that this maneuver made the style robust enough that its monks survived the great

Shui Zhao

conflict, preserving the school into the current age.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense hits you, and it was made by a creature within range of your Unarmed weapon).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits, you gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts until the target's attack is resolved.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Meditation 2, Athletics 1

**Ki Style - Prana Disintegration Methodology (Level 3) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

Once you have mastered your body and its energy, you can make attacks that deliver devastating blows of energy, using your ki to destroy your enemies from the inside.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Condition**: You have hit a creature with an Unarmed weapon attack this turn.

**Target**: The creature you hit with your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Bodily Defense. This attack deals

2 + [Will]

Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Meditation 3

**Shugen Style - Empowered Blood (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

When an opponent draws your blood, you can send your energy through it and cause it to  to sear them in a gruesome counter-attack.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack from an adjacent creature causes you to lose Health, after you lose the Health).

**Target**: The attacker.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Dodge Defense. This attack deals

2 + [Mind]

Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Shugen Style - Pull Through the Void (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Just as water rushed to fill an empty vessel, by swiftly altering your energy you can move through space and pull another creature to the space in which you stood only a moment earlier.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, you and the target Teleport into each other's spaces.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Shugen Style - Slip Through Reality (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

By attuning your energies to those of the world around you, you can seemingly step through thin air as a volley of arrows fills the place you were standing just a moment ago.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Dodge Defense, before the attack flip is made).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Effects**: Teleport up to [Power] meters. The attack no longer targets you and you suffer no effects from it, as though it were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are affected normally.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Snake Style - Turn the Tables (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a certain limb-lock you can twist a creature you are grappling in the way of oncoming blows, allowing you to deflect some attacks by using your victim as a human(oid) shield.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you successfully grapple a creature).

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You use the target as a shield, lasting until the grapple is ended. While using the target as a shield you may Parry with them as a Brawl weapon with a Defense of 0. If you successfully Parry an attack with the target, the attack instead targets their Parry Defense and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Acrobatics 3

**Bear Style - The Grizzly Awakens (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With true mastery over the style of the Bear, you shatter the earth with the force of several bears and send a shockwave forward to violently destroy all in its path.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Target**: All creatures and large, unattended objects in a 3-meter-long cone. The area of effect passes through any affected objects and is not blocked by them.

**Effects**: Make a Brawl attack against the Dodge Defense of each target creature. The attack deals

3 + [Body]

Physical damage, and if the attack hits the target is knocked Prone.

Any target object that is large enough to be affected is treated as though you had tried to knock it over or batter it down, based on your Might Skill Total.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Might 4

**Demon Style - Wasting Claw (Level 4) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

"Abasi the Devil was known as a master of shadow magicks but also one of the pioneers of the Demon style of supernatural arts. Unlike his contemporaries, however, he studied not in a monastery but instead from the corpses of hundreds of foes, torturing each to their last breath as he ripped their life from them. In doing so he perfected one of the zenith practices of the form, his so-called Wasting Claw that renders its victim so broken that many monks refuse to acknowledge it as a meditative practice at all, referring to it simply as base and terrible necromancy."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. III

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you hit with an attack with your Unarmed weapon, after the attack is resolved).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: The creature you hit.

**Effects**: Make a Brawl attack against the creature's Bodily Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack this Technique's Power is increased by 1. If the attack hits, choose either Vulnerable or Bleeding, and the target gains [Power] stacks of the chosen debuff.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Might 2, Meditation 2

**Great Old Oak Style - Bark Over Flesh (Level 4) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

An ancient martial form that was the original cornerstone of the style, it is said to have been learned by a master who studied the energy of a great oak tree for an entire moon cycle. He taught his students to suffuse their bodies in power as they fought, helping to close their wounds and dramatically improve their endurance.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within range of a Brawl weapon you are wielding.

**Effects**: Make a Brawl weapon attack against the target. For [Power] rounds, when you lose Health you gain 1 temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Athletics 2, Meditation 2

**Harmonious Mending (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

You can concentrate toxins and other impurities within your body into a small portion of your body's energy and then expel it from yourself, removing foreign influences and restoring harmony to your body as well as re-balancing your energies.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation flip against difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose one of the following:

Remove up to 2 stacks of Bleeding from yourself.

If you are suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed.

If you are infected with any diseases, you may make a Resilience flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty, ending the disease's effects on a success.

You heal 1 Health.

For each Extra Success on the flip, you may choose an extra option from the list. You may choose the same option more than once.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Ki Style - Chakral Overload (Level 4) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

A mark of mastery over the Ki style, you have learned how to focus substantial energy into your fists and aim for their spiritual center, striking their metaphysical self instead of their physical body.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits, the target is Weakened [Power] times.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Meditation 4

**Shugen Style - Fist of the Third Dragon (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Dragons are creatures who hold great amounts of power, and due to this are naturally skilled in its use. By studying the wisdom and technique of the dragons, acolytes have learned how to channel an enormous amount of energy in impressive displays of power.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures in a 1-meter-radius circle centered on a space within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against each target's Bodily Defense. The attack deals

1 + [Mind]

Brilliant damage, and if the attack hits the target is Snared once.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Shugen Style - Radiant Rebirth of the Pheonix (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Calling on a mere spark of the pheonix's glorious fire, otherwise life-threatening wounds are mended as your body glows in a brilliant light.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you would lose enough Health to become Downed).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round. Make a Meditation attack against the Mental Defense of anyone who can see you; any target hit by the attack is Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Shugen Style - Whirlwind of the Ethereal Emperor (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Sending a wave of energy crashing from your body, you drive back your foes while leaving your allies untouched.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Target**: Any number of creatures within 2 meters of you.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the Bodily Defense of each target. The attack deals [Will] Spirit damage, and if the attack hits the target is Pushed back [Power] meters. Any target not hit by the attack is Pushed back 2 meters.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Snake Style - Serpentine Redirection (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly shifting and swaying in the middle of pitched combat, you can set yourself up to strike an enemy's arm, leg, or other pivotal point in order to misdirect the force of an attack to harm them instead.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Parry Defense).

**Power**: When you use this Technique make a Brawl weapon attack, but instead of being a normal attack this Technique's Power is equal to the result, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack its Power is increased by 1.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Your Parry Defense against the attack is instead [Power] , and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack. If you Parry the attack then it instead targets your attacker, is treated as having a flip result of [Power] , and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Acrobatics 4

## Centering + Martial Arts
*Source: `centering-martial-arts.html` (Part 2)*

Centering + Martial Arts

**Snake Style - Chainbreaker (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"One of the most historically esteemed practitioners of the martial tradition of the Snake, Master Raha, is said to have been imprisoned by a local warring nation when he was a young man. Though he was strong in the art of the Snake, he was not a Master then, and he toiled in labor camps for a few long years under their oppressive regime. Every night and morning he meditated, looking for guidance and a way to free himself and others. One day he grew strong enough to break his shackles, and using them as a weapon he defeated scores of guards and freed the entire camp. Once he returned to his monastery he taught of what he had learned, how one's limbs flow outward like chains to strike many places at once, and the art of the Snake flourished for his wisdom."

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 2, Cunning 1

**Bear Style - Boulder Toss (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You grab someone and launch them away from you, often into a wall or one of their friends.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Action**: Non-action - you successfully grapple a creature.

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You throw the target up to [your Might Skill Total] meters in a straight line, and the target falls Prone in the space in which they land.

If the target would be thrown through a creature's square make a Might attack against that creature's Dodge Defense, and if the attack hits the creature is knocked Prone and the target lands in an empty space adjacent to the creature.

If the target is thrown into an object, if the object is sturdy enough to stop the target then the target takes

3 + [your Strength]

Physical damage and falls Prone in the space before the object, and if the object is not sturdy enough to stop the target the object is knocked aside, damaged, or otherwise affected as is appropriate.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 3, Might 1

**Corrupted Blood (Level 2)**

One thing that is constant across many civilizations is that, for a variety of reasons, they consider blood to be important and in some cases even sacred. This is for good reason; blood sustains living things and is in many ways a conduit for the power a person can wield. It is for this very reason that blood magic is so feared. It is also for this reason that people first sought to perfect this technique, training control over their body's energy to the point where they can corrupt their own blood, turning it to poison on par with that of the most venomous of beasts.

**Cost**: Lose 2 non-temporary Health to use this Technique.

**Action**: Main

(or Extra, see below)

.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You draw some of your blood into some container, which becomes a dose of freshly-crafted

Basic Poison

of your choice. Make a Centering flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7 - the strength of the poison depends on your result:

On a result of at least 11 you may create the poison with -2 Difficulty

On a result of at least 13 you may create the poison at its listed statistics

On a result of at least 15 you may create the poison with +2 Difficulty and +1 Potency

On a result of 17 or higher you may create the poison with +4 Difficulty and +2 Potency

You may also use this Technique as an Extra action by drawing the blood with a weapon or piece of ammunition; if you do, it coats that weapon instead of collecting into a container.

You are immune to poison created with your blood through this Technique. 24 hours after the poison's creation it loses its potency, becoming a mundane vial of blood.

**Prereqs**: Centering 3, Resistance 2

**Demon Style - Spirit-Rending Claw (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By temporarily sacrificing a portion of your own fortitude you can corrupt your victim's spirit, weakening their very life force.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed Strike weapon attack against your target. If the attack hits, both you and your target gain [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of Necrotic which last for the scene.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, you may have it deal its damage as Shadow damage.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 2, Centering 2

**Great Old Oak Style - Tree Withstands the Storm (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

The ancient style of hand-to-hand combat teaches one to stand strong, resist all that comes at you, then defeat your enemies once they are worn down.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. You gain a + [half your Centering Skill Total] bonus to Dodge and Parry Defense for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 2, Centering 2

**Ignore Pain (Level 2)**

Sometimes there are things that need doing, and even if your leg is broken it isn't a good enough reason to stop.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Make a Centering flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 you ignore any stacks of Crippled, Slowed, Weakened, or Wounded you have for 1 round. For every 2 points your result is above 9, you may have the effect last an extra 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Centering 3, Resistance 2

**Ki Style - Disrupt Chakra (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By striking a target's chakras with your ki you can disrupt the flow of their energy, interrupting their focus.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack. If the attack hits the target is Dazed and gains [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of Weakened, each lasting for 1 round.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, you may have it deal its damage as Spirit damage.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 2, Centering 2

**Shugen Style - Iron Skin (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

As an attack weaves its way past your guard, you concentrate your energy into your skin to deflect the force of the blow.

**Action**: Counter - you are hit by an attack.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain [half your Centering Skill Total] temporary Health which lasts until the attack is resolved.

**Shugen Style - Spiritual Shurikens (Level 2) [Martial]**

Concentrating a small fragment your ki into a hand or other limb, you launch an endless salvo of energy at an enemy.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Centering Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Parry or Dodge Defense. The attack deals

3 + [Will]

Physical damage.

**Prereqs**: Centering 3

**Shugen Style - Unbreakable Spirit Meditation (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Engaging your foe in a battle of wills, you send them reeling with your iron resolve.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: A creature within [Centering Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Crippled, Enervated, and Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Centering 3

**Snake Style - Crashing Leg Sweep (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You take advantage of an opponent's momentary distraction to trip them, ending their attempts to flee in a tangle of limbs.

**Action**: Counter - when a creature within range of your Martial Arts weapon moves or is Pushed to a space outside of that range.

**Target**: That creature.

**Effects**: Make an attack with a Martial Arts weapon against the target. If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone and Snared for 1 round.

**Bear Style - Shattering Slam (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Rearing back with both arms raised high, you slam into some unfortunate soul with the entire weight of your body, sending the force of your blow through their bones.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. The attack ignores Armor Soak, and if the attack hits the target is Snared and Dazed for 1 round each.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 4, Might 3

**Demon Style - Corrupted Fist (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Twisting your body's energy, you lash out with a blow that, given a path to your foe, creates a vicious explosion of energy that leaves both you and your enemy weakened.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against your target. If the attack hits, after the attack is resolved choose either Vulnerable or Weakened - your target gains [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of the chosen debuff and you gain [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of the other debuff, each lasting for 2 rounds.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, you may have it deal its damage as Shadow damage.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 3, Centering 3

**Expel Befoulment (Level 3) [Encounter]**

You can concentrate toxins and other impurities within your body into a small portion of your body's energy and then expel it from yourself, removing foreign influences and restoring harmony to your body as well as re-balancing your energies.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Make a Centering flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 you may choose one of the following:

Remove up to 2 Bleeds from yourself.

If you are suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed.

If you are infected with any diseases you make a Resistance flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty, ending the disease's effects on a success.

You are no longer Snared, any penalties to your Movement Speed end, and if you are Prone you may stand up immediately as a Non-action.

For every 2 points your result is above 9, you may choose an extra option from the list. You may choose the same option more than once.

**Prereqs**: Centering 4, Resistance 3

**Great Old Oak Style - Bark Over Flesh (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

An ancient martial form, a staple of the style, punches are thrown in a series of movements that strengthens the body and its energies, allowing you to shrug off a foe's counterattack.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack. You gain [half your Centering Skill Total] temporary Health which lasts for 2 rounds.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 3, Centering 3

**Ki Style - Whelming Wave (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A cornerstone of the ki style, you strike your opponent with a burst of your energy to overwhelm their senses and render them defenseless.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack; use Centering in place of the weapon's relevant Skill, and the attack is against the target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone and Off-Balance, each lasting for 1 round.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, you may have it deal its damage as Spirit damage.

When an opponent lands a blow you are capable of energizing your ki, causing you blood to sear them in a gruesome counter-attack.

**Action**: Counter - an adjacent creature's attack causes you to lose Health

**Target**: The adjacent creature that caused you to lose Health.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Dodge Defense. This attack deals

3 + [Will]

Spirit damage.

**Prereqs**: Centering 4

**Shugen Style - Metaphysical Chains (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You tune your energies to resonate with your foe's and send out waves of your energy to encompass them, binding both their physical and mental energies.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Centering Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Bodily Defense. The attack deals

1 + [Will]

Spirit damage and if the attack hits the target is Dazed and Snared, each lasting for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Centering 4

**Shugen Style - Slip Through Reality (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By attuning your energies to those of the world around you, you can seemingly step through thin air as a volley of arrows fills the place you were standing just a moment ago.

**Action**: Counter - you are targeted by an attack.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: The attack automatically misses you and you suffer no effects from it, as though it were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are affected normally. Then you Teleport up to [half your Centering Skill Total] meters.

**Prereqs**: Centering 4

**Snake Style - Turn the Tables (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a certain limb-lock you can twist a creature you are grappling in the way of oncoming blows, allowing you to deflect some attacks by using your victim as a human(oid) shield.

**Action**: Non-action - you successfully grapple a creature.

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You use the target as a shield, lasting until the grapple is ended. While using the target as a shield you may Parry with them as a Martial Arts weapon with a Defense of 0. If you successfully Parry an attack with the target, the attack instead targets their Parry Defense and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 4, Insight 2

**Bear Style - Grizzly De-Fangs the Tiger (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With the strength of a great bear, you block an attack and bat your enemy's weapon out of their hands.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Action**: Counter - a creature within range of your Unarmed weapon makes a weapon attack against your Parry Defense.

**Target**: The creature that made the attack.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon and substitute the result for your Parry Defense against the attack. You ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack. If you Parry the attack make an opposed Might flip against the creature, and if you win you knock the weapon the creature used to make the attack out of their hand and [how much you won] meters away from you.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 5, Might 4

**Demon Style - Ripjaw Gambit (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A brutal attack that blatantly disregards your own safety, this maneuver has the potential to rip an opponent into pieces. If the martial artist fails to properly execute it, however, the forces channeled harm them instead.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against your target. If the attack hits the target gains [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of Bleeding, and if the attack misses you gain [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of Bleeding.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, you may have it deal its damage as Shadow damage.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 4, Centering 4

**Great Old Oak Style - River Stone Deflection (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

From a defensive stance you are capable of briefly strengthening your stance, deflecting an attack with the strength of a coursing river and sending your would-be assailant reeling.

**Action**: Counter - a creature within range of your Unarmed weapon targets you with an attack against your Parry Defense.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: You gain a

+[half your Centering Skill Total]

bonus to Parry Defense against the attack. If you Parry that attack, the attacker is Harried [half your Centering Skill Total] times.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 4, Centering 4

**Ki Style - Prana Disintegration Methodology (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Once you have mastered your body and its energy, you can make attacks that deliver devastating blows of energy, using your ki to destroy your enemies from the inside.

**Action**: Non-action - you hit a creature with an Unarmed weapon attack.

**Target**: The creature hit with the attack.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Bodily Defense. The attack deals

2 + [Will]

Spirit damage.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 4, Centering 4

**Shugen Style - Disintegrate Spirit (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Forming your energy into a torrent of barbs and unleashing them upon an enemy, you shred their ki and cause their life force to drain from them.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Centering Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits the target gains [half your Centering Skill Total] stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Centering 5

**Shugen Style - Radiant Rebirth of the Pheonix (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Calling on a mere spark of the pheonix's glorious fire, otherwise life-threatening wounds are mended as your body glows in a brilliant light.

**Action**: Counter - you would lose Health and be Downed as a result.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain [half your Centering Skill Total] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round. Anyone who can see you is Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Centering 5

**Shugen Style - Whirlwind of the Ethereal Emperor (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Sending a wave of energy crashing from your body, you drive back your foes while leaving your allies untouched.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within 2 meters.

**Effects**: Make a Centering attack against the Bodily Defense of each target. The attack deals

1 + [Will]

Spirit damage, and if the attack hits the target is Pushed back [Centering Skill Total] meters. Any target not hit by the attack is Pushed back 2 meters.

**Prereqs**: Centering 5

**Snake Style - Serpentine Redirection (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly shifting and swaying in the middle of pitched combat, you can set yourself up to strike an enemy's arm, leg, or other pivotal point in order to misdirect the force of an attack to harm them instead.

**Action**: This Technique is used as a Reaction to a creature making an attack against your Parry Defense, before the attack is made.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Make a Martial Arts weapon attack and substitute the result for your Parry Defense against the attack. You ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack. If you Parry the attack then it instead targets your attacker, using your Martial Arts weapon attack result in place of the original attack result.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 5, Insight 3

**Wholeness of Body (Level 4) [Encounter]**

Using your inner energy to fuel your body's regeneration, you are capable of quickening the mending of wounds to the point where they close before your eyes.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Make a Centering flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 you heal 1 Health. For every 2 points your result is above 9, you may heal an extra 1 Health.

**Prereqs**: Centering 5, Resistance 4

**Bear Style - The Grizzly Awakens (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With true mastery over the style of the Bear, you shatter the earth with the force of several bears and send a shockwave forward to violently destroy all in its path.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: All creatures and large objects in a [Might Skill Total] -meter-long cone. The area of effect passes through any affected objects and is not blocked by them.

**Effects**: Make a Martial Arts attack against the Dodge Defense of each target creature. The attack deals

3 + [Strength]

Physical damage which ignores Armor Soak. If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone.

Any target object that is large enough to be affected is treated as though you had tried to knock it over or batter it down, based on your Might Skill Total.

**Prereqs**: Martial Arts 5, Might 5, Strength 5

---

## Craft & Mixology
*Source: `craft-mixology.html` (Part 2)*

Craft & Mixology

**Immaculate Saucepan Methodology (Level 1)**

With access to enough spices and resources you can adjust the flavor of just about any meal, salvaging any mistakes and satisfying even the most picky eaters.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: A culinary dish adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Mixology flip to change the flavor of the target dish any way you please, and if you flip a Spade then you gain a +1 bonus to the flip. The result of your flip is modified as follows:

+2 if you are keeping close to the dish's flavor profile - changing a sugary meat sauce to taste of a certain fruit, etc.

-2 to dramatically change the flavor, or change it to something dissimilar from the original dish - making a garden salad taste like a steak, for example

-2 to mask a strong taste the dish has - compensating for a burnt cut of meat, or removing all heat from a ghost pepper

The difficulty of this flip will vary depending on how complicated the seasoning is, and more intricate dishes (ones with a higher Difficulty to create in the first place) will be harder to successfully alter in this way.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 2, Cunning 1

**Stately Tailoring (Level 1)**

Well-versed in multiple eras of high-society fashion, you have a keen eye for the ways that different styles of clothing can differ from each other - and how similar they might be.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: A garment adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Craft flip to alter the garment any way you please, and if you flip a Spade then you gain a +1 bonus to the flip. If you are only making a minor alteration to the garment's color or form then you have Good Luck on the flip. If you are dramatically change the garment's color or form you have Bad Luck on the flip.

**Prereqs**: Craft 2, Cunning 1

**Deploy Mounted Launcher (Level 2) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

The cunning tinker is never caught off-guard, and always carries an assortment of parts that can be assembled into a defensive contraption at a moment's notice.

**Cost**: 1 Gold worth of Craft materials.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An empty space adjacent to you.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Mounted Launcher

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you use this Technique you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Mounted Launcher lasts as long as you keep this Technique committed.

Prereqs: Craft 3, Academics 2

**Salvage (Level 2)**

While many craftsmen can work with prepared materials you have a more pragmatic approach and are a deft hand at rendering finished products into serviceable materials for re-use.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can use existing items as raw materials for item creation, for which purpose the items count as materials worth half of what their value would be if they were new.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 3

**Scraptrops (Level 2) [Encounter Technique]**

By keeping spare pieces of metal and wood from your various tinkerings, you always have a source of caltrops to scatter at your foes' feet.

**Cost**: 1 Gold worth of Craft materials.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Each space within a 2-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: You scatter caltrops in the target spaces. Each space with caltrops in it gains [half your Craft Skill Total] levels of Difficult Terrain; a creature may choose to ignore this Difficult Terrain, but if they do they gain [half your Craft Skill Total] stacks of Bleeding.

You may spend 1 minute gathering the caltrops. If you do, they are removed and you regain this Technique's Cost.

**Prereqs**: Craft 3

**Essence Admixture (Level 3)**

Each mixture you brew up has precise amounts of various reagents added in,

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A Potion or Grenade, that you personally crafted, which you are holding.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following options, which then applies to the target item for the scene.

If the target is a Potion, one of the following applies when drunk:

The imbiber heals 3 Health.

Choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. The imbiber gains [half your Mixology Skill Total] Soak of the chosen type for 3 rounds.

The imbiber gains a + [your Mixology Skill Total] bonus to Speed for 3 rounds.

The imbiber gains Good Luck on Athletics and Might flips for 3 rounds.

If the target is a Grenade:

The Grenade's effects apply to all spaces adjacent to the initial space, targeting any creatures in those spaces if it involves an attack.

If the Grenade is used for an attack that deals damage, it deals an extra

[your Intelligence

] damage of a type it would deal.

When you use this Technique you may choose to commit the Soul spent, and if you do then after the scene ends the effects of the Technique last as long as you keep the Soul committed.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 4

**Inspirited Armaments (Level 3)**

The armaments that you craft hold secrets and potential that you can unlock for your allies.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A weapon or piece of armor, that you personally crafted, which you are touching.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following options, which then applies to the target item for the scene. The available options depend on whether the item is a weapon or a piece of armor.

If the target is a weapon:

If the weapon is used to Parry an attack, if the attacker is within the weapon's reach they gain a stack of Bleeding.

Choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow - when the weapon is used to make a damaging attack, it deals up to [half your Craft Skill Total] of its damage as damage of the chosen type.

If the weapon is used to make an attack that causes a creature to lose Health, that creature gains a stack of Bleeding.

If the weapon is ranged, you gain a +1 bonus to Strength for the purpose of determining the weapon's range.

If the weapon is close-range, its range is increased by 1, although it is still considered to be a close-range weapon.

If the target is armor:

Choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow - the wearer gains +2 Elemental Soak of the chosen type.

The chosen armor's Dexterity Limit is increased by 1.

The wearer gains a +1 bonus to any one type of Soak that the armor provides against attacks for which a Heart was flipped.

Any opposed flip to grapple the wearer has Bad Luck.

When you use this Technique you may choose to commit the Soul spent, and if you do then after the scene ends the effects of the Technique last as long as you keep the Soul committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4

**Reactant Brewing (Level 3)**

Your diverse study of alchemical ingredients and their interdependent effects has given you the ability to adjust formulae on the fly, turning potions into mimics of others.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Target**: An

alchemical good

which you are holding.

**Effects**: You permanently turn the alchemical good into another alchemical good of your choice. The resulting good must be one that you know how to make, and it may not have a higher cost or a higher Minimum Skill Total than the original good.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 4, Academics 3

**Saboteur (Level 3)**

A skilled infiltrator can pick a lock or disable a trap. A truly gifted infiltrator can reach deeper into a given contraption, rigging it to turn an enemy's stronghold into their own domain.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you successfully disable a mechanical device, such as a trap or similar contraption, you may sabotage the device to function as you choose. This can be relatively simple, such as resetting a trap to trigger for the next person who passes, or more complicated such as configuring a device to trigger after a certain amount of time. Trigger conditions that are particularly complicated or hard to estimate may require a successful Academics flip to set correctly. You may also modify the function of the device with a successful Craft flip, although this may also require crafting materials.

For either the initial flip to disable or sabotage the device, or the Academics flip to set a complicated trigger, if you flip a Spade then you may treat the value of any card flipped as 7.

**Prereqs**: Legerdemain 3, Academics 2, Craft 2

**Scrapcraft (Level 3)**

You are in the habit of keeping a variety of half-completed spare parts in your pack, and in the practice of making common items from various odds and ends. After some time doing both of these, you can pull together anything from a sword to a lantern in a moment's notice.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself.

Effects: You create an item, which must have Craft as its relevant Skill and for which you meet the requirements to create, as long as the materials required for the item are within your easy reach. The item lasts until the end of the scene, at which time it falls apart into the materials used to make it.

When you use this Technique you may choose to commit 1 Soul to the item you create. If you do then after the scene ends the created item lasts as long as you keep the Soul committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4, Legerdemain 2, Cunning 2

**Vessel of Belief (Level 3)**

In darkest hour your companions know they can rely on the arms and elixirs you create to see them through their troubles, drawing strength from your belief in them.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: An item you created, such as with Craft or Mixology, which you are holding.

**Effects**: You commit any amount of Soul, which you place within the target item. If the target item is consumed when used, such as a potion, when the item is consumed any Soul you had placed within it is uncommitted and the person who consumed the item gains temporary Soul equal to that which was placed in the item, which lasts for the scene. If the item is a non-consumable item, such as a weapon, while that item is being worn or used the wielder may choose to un-commit any amount of Soul you had placed in it and gain that much temporary Soul, which lasts for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 4, Will 3

**Artisan's Eye (Level 4)**

Your discerning eye sees all when it comes to your craft. A moment's glance is enough to tell you exactly how an item was crafted and how it is designed to function.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: A created item, or schematic/recipe to create an item, within [Awareness Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You learn the item's statistics, including what materials it is made from, if it differs from the standard version of that weapon, and so on. Depending on the situation it might be impossible to gain all of this information, or rare cases where the item is too foreign to properly analyze, but you will know that there is something you cannot analyze about the item.

In addition, if you meet the item's Minimum Skill Total in its Required Skill, you learn how to create the item, permanently learning it as a recipe for item creation.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 5, Academics 4

---

## Disguise, Legerdemain, & Stealth
*Source: `disguise-legerdemain-stealth.html` (Part 1)*

Disguise, Legerdemain, & Stealth

**Air of Mystery (Level 2)**

You have an easily forgettable face, and people usually need to "meet" you more than once to recognize you. Anyone who only gets a glimpse of you won't be able to make out any notable features, and official records of you tend to be rather light on details.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Anyone attempting to research your involvement in anything, gather rumors on you, or the like has Bad Luck on any flips for that purpose. You have Good Luck on any Disguise flips to conceal your identity, as long as you are not trying to look like a specific person.

**Prereqs**: (Stealth or Savvy) 3, Cunning 2

**Footsteps of Shadow (Level 2) [Encounter]**

When you don't wish to be seen you move like a phantom through the shadows; in fact, staying hidden is your nature to the point where you easily cover for the missteps of your more clumsy allies.

**Action**: Non-action - you make a Stealth flip.

**Target**: Any number of willing creatures making a Stealth flip with you.

**Effects**: You may also use the result of your Stealth flip as though it were a flip to give Backup to each target for their Stealth flips. If you do so, and a target's Stealth result would exceed your Stealth result, instead their result is treated as being the same as your result.

Prereqs: Stealth 3, Cunning 2

**Ventriloquy (Level 2)**

Your practice with impersonating others has given you the ability to speak in a variety of voices and even make it seem as though you are not the one speaking.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A direction, creature, or object within [twice your Disguise Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You throw your voice, making it seem as though your speech is coming from the target direction, creature, or object. Make a Disguise flip opposed by Awareness flips of any onlookers - any onlookers against whom you win are convinced that the speech is coming from the target. If you flip a Spade, any onlookers who win the opposed flip will not know where the speech came from.

**Prereqs**: Disguise 3, Cunning 2

**One of Many Faces (Level 3)**

You have mastered the art of infiltration and imitation. You can move between personas in the space of a breath, and even if someone manages to see through one of your masks

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: As long as you are not being observed, you may put on a Disguise as a Full action rather than the several minutes it would normally take.

If someone successfully sees through your Disguise, as long as your disguise is not removed they will not be able to tell your actual identity, merely that you are some impostor.

**Prereqs**: Disguise 4, Legerdemain 3

**Vanish (Level 4) [Encounter Technique]**

With a smoke bomb, great wave of a cloak, or some other obscuring act, you vanish from sight and disappear entirely from view.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Any onlookers who can see you.

**Effects**: Make a Stealth flip which is opposed by an Awareness flip from each target, and each target against whom you win cannot see you for 1 round.

If you flip a Spade and the card's value is under 8, you gain Advantage on the flip.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 5

---

## Elementalism Spells
*Source: `elementalism-spells.html` (Part 1)*

Elementalism Spells

**Elemental Empowerment (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You've practiced with your elemental magic enough to be able to give a spell a bit of extra kick when needed.

**Action**: Non-action - you cast an Elementalism Spell.

**Target**: The Elementalism Spell you are casting.

**Effects**: The target spell has its Power increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2, Cunning 1

**Bonewrack (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Digging into an enemy's bones with shadow magic, you cause their joints to lock up and their muscles to seize temporarily.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against your target's Resistance Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [Power] times for 2 rounds and is Harried [Power] times.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Medicine 1

**Force Barrier (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As an enemy's attack comes towards you a curved wall of force appears at your fingertips, deflecting the blow.

**Action**: Counter - a creature makes an attack against your Parry Defense.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: The attack is instead against your Dodge Defense, and you may substitute a Defense of

(10 + [Elementalism Skill Total])

instead of your Dodge Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Cunning 2

**Force Missiles (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Even while focusing most of your attention on other matters, you can quickly conjure a barrage of force to blast your foes.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The spell attack deals

4 + [Will]

Physical damage.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Shadow Jaunt (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Your form flickers into absolute darkness, and in the blink of an eye you fade from view and reappear somewhere else.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You Teleport up to [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Acrobatics 1

**Sheets of Ice (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You coat the ground in slick sheets of ice, making it incredibly difficult for your enemies to pass through an area.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Up to [Power] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of [half your Elementalism Skill Total] .

**Effects**: The target spaces each gain [Power] levels of Difficult Terrain.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Wall of Shadows (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a wave of swirling, opaque shadows, obscuring sight in the area.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of swirling shadows, roughly occupying the target area for the scene. The wall is not solid and creatures and objects can pass through it, but the wall fully blocks any sort of sight both through it for anything within it.

If a creature passes through or begins its turn in the wall, make an Elementalism spell attack against that creature's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the creature is Weakened for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3

**Bindings of Force (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure chains of magical force and wrap them around an enemy, concentrating your magic to bind them tight.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism flip opposed by your target's Martial Arts, Acrobatics, or Might. If you win you begin grappling the target at range, which works as a normal grapple but with the following differences:

When you attempt to drag the target you make an Elementalism flip opposed by your target's Might, and if you win you Push the target up to [how much you won] meters.

Each turn you must either spend a Main action to maintain the grapple or the grapple ends.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4, Martial Arts 2

**Corrupt Chakras (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Following principles of meditation and monastic arts, you can recognize the flow of energy around your foes' chakras. Rather than manipulating them using your

ki

, as is tradition, you instead leverage elemental magics to overwhelm them with destructive shadow.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of [your Will] .

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against your target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target suffers [Power] stacks of Necrotic and is Dazed, each lasting for 2 rounds.

If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the spell's Power is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4, Centering 2

**Dazzling Sweep (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You wave your arm and send forth a crashing wave of brilliant, multi-colored light to blind and disorient everyone caught in its wake.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: All creatures within a 2 meter long cone.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Any targets hit by the attack have are Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Awareness 1

**Summon Elemental (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Twisting elemental magic into solid form, you bring an elemental into being - a creature of energy and magic that you can command to destroy your enemies.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An empty space within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Elemental

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Elemental lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Wall of Ice (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

With a wave of your arms you raise a cliff of solid ice,

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of ice that lasts for the scene. When the wall is created, any creature that would be in a space the wall occupies is moved to the nearest unoccupied space, including on top of the wall.

The wall has [your Will] Soak against all damage types except for Fire, each space of the wall has [your Will] Health, and when it loses all of its Health the wall is destroyed in that space.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Phantasmal Clones (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Illusory copies that look identical to you begin to drift from your form and meld back in, making it extremely difficult to track your exact movements.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You create illusions that mask your movements, lasting for 3 rounds. Whenever a creature makes an attack that targets you and is not an area of effect, before the attack is made, make an Elementalism spell attack against that creature's Mental Defense. If the attack hits that creature has Bad Luck on the attack against you, and if the attack misses then that creature ignores the effects of this Spell for the rest of its duration.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5, Insight 3

**Wall of Fire (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a curtain of burning fire, threatening to incinerate anyone who passes through it.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of fire in the target area which lasts for the scene. Any creature or object that passes through the wall or starts its turn within the wall takes

2 + [your Will]

Fire damage, although a creature cannot be damaged by the wall more than once per round.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

---

## Elementalism
*Source: `elementalism.html` (Part 2)*

**Concentrate Arcana (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell]**

Pushing your magical abilities to their limits, you fold incredible amounts of force into an incantation.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you begin casting an Elementalism Spell, once per Spell).

**Effects**: Choose one:

If the Spell includes a damaging spell attack, that attack deals an extra 3 damage.

The Spell's Power is increased by 3.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2, Resilience 1

**Force Barrier (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As an enemy's attack comes towards you a curved wall of force appears at your fingertips, deflecting the blow.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Parry Defense).

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism flip, with a bonus of + [Power] . The attack is instead against your Dodge Defense, and for this your Dodge Defense is equal to the result of your flip and you ignore any stacks of Harried.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Insight 2

**Force Missiles (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Even while focusing most of your attention on other matters, you can quickly conjure a barrage of force to blast your foes.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The spell attack deals 2

+ [Mind]

Physical damage.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Might 2

**Shadow Jaunt (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Your form flickers into absolute darkness, and in the blink of an eye you fade from view and reappear somewhere else.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Spell has a power of [Elementalism Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You Teleport up to [Power] meters.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Acrobatics 2

**Wall of Shadows (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a wave of swirling, opaque shadows, obscuring sight in the area.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of swirling shadows, roughly occupying the target area for the scene. The wall is not solid and creatures and objects can pass through it, but the wall fully blocks any sort of sight both through it for anything within it.

If a creature passes through or begins its turn in the wall, make an Elementalism spell attack against that creature's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the creature is Weakened once.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Resilience 2

**Bindings of Force (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure chains of magical force and wrap them around an enemy, concentrating your magic to bind them tight.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the attack hits, you begin grappling the target at range, which works as a normal grapple but with the following differences:

When you attempt to drag the target you make an Elementalism flip opposed by your target's Might, and if you win you Push the target up to [how much you won] meters.

Each turn you must either spend 2 AP to maintain the grapple or the grapple ends.

For flips to end the grapple or keep another creature from ending the grapple, you may use Elementalism instead of Brawl.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4, Brawl 2, Might 2

**Summon Elemental (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Twisting elemental magic into solid form, you bring an elemental into being - a creature of energy and magic that you can command to destroy your enemies.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Elemental

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Elemental lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Wall of Ice (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

With a wave of your arms you raise a cliff of solid ice,

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each target space must be next to another target space, and they are 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of ice that lasts for the scene. When the wall is created, any creature that would be in a space the wall occupies is moved to the nearest unoccupied space, including on top of the wall.

The wall has [Mind] Soak against all damage types except for Fire, each space of the wall has [Mind] Health, and when it loses all of its Health the wall is destroyed in that space.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Wall of Fire (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a curtain of burning fire, threatening to incinerate anyone who passes through it.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of fire in the target area which lasts for the scene. Any creature or object that passes through the wall or starts its turn within the wall takes

2 + [Mind]

Fire damage, although a creature cannot be damaged by the wall more than once per round.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

**Reign of Blood (Level 5) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As mighty warriors battle on they accumulate bodily stress, the result of trading blows and pushing their bodies to their limits. Usually at the end of a battle they catch their breath and stave off the worst of the trauma, but by striking them in a vulnerable moment you overwhelm their bodies with lethal shadow energy, forcing them to suffer the sum of their injuries.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against the target's Resistance Defense. If the attack hits, all stacks of Bleeding on the target Expire.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5, Medicine 5, Spirit 5
