## Elementalism
*Source: `elementalism_1.html` (Part 1)*

Elementalism magic produces straightforward results, generally geared towards neutralizing and destroying enemies.

Battle Magic

Battle Magic is a collection of Spells that an Elementalist can customize to fit their style of magic use. They come in a range of Levels, with a variety of features to specialize them.

See details on the Battle Magic section.

**Elemental Storm (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

With a surge of magical power you cause your magic to destabilize nearby elemental energies, creating a dangerous storm.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (You declare a spell attack for an Elementalism Spell).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Note the spell attack you made. After the attack is resolved, a storm begins to appear in a 1-meter-radius circle centered on one of the targets of the spell attack; this is a very obvious effect. At the start of your next turn the storm appears in that area, and lasting until the end of the scene.

If a creature is in the storm when it appears, moves or is Pushed into the storm, or ends their turn in the storm, make the noted spell attack against that creature. For this attack, ignore Crippled, Good Luck, and Bad Luck.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2, Resilience 1

**Gainsay (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

"Here we are at last, Conflagrator. We shall see whether my blade or your magic shall prove more nimble!"

"Listen, Ikami, I really don't have time for this. How's this sound: you don't get that stupid toothpick of a sword anywhere near me, and I don't turn you into a fine, pink mist. Fair?"

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a weapon attack is declared against you, and the attacker could be targeted by one of your Spells).

**Target**: The creature attacking you.

**Effects**: Cast an Elementalism Spell against the target. The Spell cannot take more than 2 AP to use.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2, Insight 1

**Force Missiles (Level 1) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Even while focusing most of your attention on other matters, you can quickly conjure a barrage of force to blast your foes.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against your target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The spell attack deals 2

+ [Mind]

Physical damage. If the spell attack would deal its damage as any type other than Physical, it deals -2 damage.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2, Might 1

**Elemental Blend (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Stance]**

Many styles of warmage training focus on overwhelming power and raw eldritch offense over all else, and each of those that do have built their practices with this stance as a foundation. With a series of motions that leverage your entire body to gather magical energy and intricate double-handed gestures to keep control over it, you wring every last measure of potency from your incantations.

**Effects**: When you make a damaging spell attack, choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow, and the attack deals its damage as that type. The action for the spell attack requires an extra 1 AP to make.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Resilience 2

**Force Barrier (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As an enemy's attack comes towards you a curved wall of force appears at your fingertips, deflecting the blow.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Parry Defense).

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism flip, with a bonus of +2. The attack is instead against your Dodge Defense, for this your Dodge Defense is equal to the result of your flip, and you ignore any stacks of Harried.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Insight 2

**Overchanneling (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Stance]**

Many styles of warmage training focus on overwhelming power and raw eldritch offense over all else, and each of those that do have built their practices with this stance as a foundation. With a series of motions that leverage your entire body to gather magical energy and intricate double-handed gestures to keep control over it, you wring every last measure of potency from your incantations.

**Effects**: Your damaging spell attacks deal 1 more damage. You may not Parry attacks.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Might 2

**Shadow Jaunt (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Your form flickers into absolute darkness, and in the blink of an eye you fade from view and reappear somewhere else.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You Teleport up to [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Acrobatics 2

**Wall of Shadows (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a wave of swirling, opaque shadows, obscuring sight in the area.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces, each within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters. Each space is 3 meters tall, and all spaces must be connected.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of swirling shadows, roughly occupying the target area for the scene. Creatures cannot see through the wall, or anything within it, but it is not solid. Any attacks into or through the wall treat it as Heavy Cover.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Resilience 2

**Warmage's Reserves (Level 2) [Elementalism Spell]**

At the Warmage's Garrison in the cosmopolitan City of Five Ports, multidisciplinary study of elemental magic has led to impressive advancements. The first and most important of these, now a requirement for any graduate warmage, teaches an elementalist to develop a deep well of magical resources and flexibility.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Regain the use of an Elementalism Spell Encounter Technique.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 3, Academics 2

**Bindings of Force (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure chains of magical force and wrap them around an enemy, concentrating your magic to bind them tight.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the attack hits, you begin grappling the target at range, which works as a normal grapple but with the following differences:

When you attempt to drag the target you make an Elementalism flip opposed by your target's Might, and if you win you Push the target up to [how much you won] meters.

Each turn you must either spend 2 AP to maintain the grapple or the grapple ends.

For flips to end the grapple or keep another creature from ending the grapple, you use Elementalism instead of Brawl.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4, Brawl 2, Might 1

**Summon Elemental (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

Twisting elemental magic into solid form, you bring an elemental into being - a creature of energy and magic that you can command to destroy your enemies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: An empty space w

ithin [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Elemental

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Elemental lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Wall of Ice (Level 3) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

With a wave of your arms you raise a cliff of solid ice, splitting a skirmish in two or boxing in an unfortunate enemy.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each target space must be next to another target space, and they are 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of ice that lasts for the scene. When the wall is created, any creature that would be in a space the wall occupies is moved to the nearest unoccupied space, including on top of the wall.

The wall has [Mind] Soak against all damage types except for Fire, each space of the wall has [Mind] Health, and when it loses all of its Health the wall is destroyed in that space.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 4

**Wall of Fire (Level 4) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

You conjure a curtain of burning fire, threatening to incinerate anyone who passes through it.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [Elementalism Skill Total] spaces within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters; each space must be next to at least one other space and is 3 meters tall.

**Effects**: You summon a wall of fire in the target area which lasts for the scene. Any creature or object in the area when the Spell begins, moves or is Pushed into the area, or ends their turn in the area takes

2 + [Mind]

Fire damage, although a creature cannot be damaged by the wall more than once per round.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5

**Reign of Blood (Level 5) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

As mighty warriors battle on they accumulate bodily stress, the result of trading blows and pushing their bodies to their limits. Usually at the end of a battle they catch their breath and stave off the worst of the trauma, but by striking them in a vulnerable moment you overwhelm their bodies with lethal shadow energy, forcing them to suffer the sum of their injuries.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism spell attack against the target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, remove all stacks of Bleeding from the target, and they lose that much Health.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5, Medicine 5, Spirit 5

**Shadowfrost Bolt (Level 5) [Elementalism Spell] [Encounter]**

A true master of elemental magic is capable of hybridizing the prime forms of elemental energies, ripping apart anything but the most complete of defenses.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Target**: A creature within 10 meters.

**Effects**: Make a Elementalism spell attack against the lower of your target's Dodge or Bodily Defense. The spell attack deals 10 damage as either Fire, Frost, or Shadow - whichever is the target's lowest Resist. If the attack hits, before it deals its damage, the target is Slowed and gains 2 stacks of Necrotic.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 5, Spirit 5, Mind 5

## Expert
*Source: `expert.html` (Part 2)*

**All Juiced Up (Level 1)**

Whether through many long nights of sampling your own brews or through a freakish acclimation to certain reagents, potions and elixirs have a tendency to keep you feeling sharp and awake.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: While you are affected by a Potion's effects, you have Good Luck on Reflex flips.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 2, Resilience 1

**Animal Companion: Trick (Level 1) [Re-Learning]**

"You know, I really wish your wolf would stop slobbering into our mugs."

"Well boy, you got two choices. You can either put up with a little drool in your mug, or you can pour your own damn beer."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your companion learns one of the following tricks:

**Civilized**: Inexplicably, your companion can carry and manipulate objects with about the skill of a small child, and without damaging them.

**Determination**: The maximum of your companion's 'Bloodied (-2)' and 'Winded (-0)' Health level are each increased by 1.

**Trained**: Your companion's Awareness, Might, or Stealth Skill Total is increased by 2. You may choose this ability multiple times, but only once for each Skill.

**Quick**: Your companion's Speed increases by 2. You may only choose this ability once.

**Martial Training**: Your companion learns a

Squire's Strike

; it may ignore Prereqs.

**Beast Feast [Encounter]**: Your companion eats the heart of an enemy that was Downed by you, your companion, or one of your allies, then heals 2 Health.

These improvements apply to whichever animal is your companion at the time. If you lose a companion and gain a new one, the new one automatically benefits from this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Animal Companion

**Bedside Manner (Level 1)**

You are a practiced physician, sawbones, or battle medic, with enough practice to tend to groups of patients while they rest.

**Action**: 15 minutes.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: Any allies that are resting for the night.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you get an Extra Success. If you succeed, one target heals 1 Health when they next get a full night's rest, so long as they do so directly after you use this Technique. For each Extra Success you may choose an additional target.

A creature can only benefit from this Technique once per rest.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2

**Hidden Meaning (Level 1)**

Above and beyond common parlance used by thieves and information brokers, you know a wealth of double-meanings that you can weave into conversation with similarly practiced accomplices in order to conduct covert business in broad daylight.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your conversations and written documents can hold hidden meanings. If anyone who hears your conversations or reads your letters tries to understand them, make a Streetwise flip against that person's Insight. Unless they win, they think the topic of discussion is different, normal, and can gain no useful information from you. They might still suspect that you are conducting other business, but cannot find any evidence in your speech or word.

You may spend 1 hour and coach any number of other people in the doublespeak you will use for a certain topic. If you do, whenever you use this Technique to discuss that topic, those people may understand your meaning perfectly.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 2, Academics 1

**Wisdom of the Woods (Level 1)**

"Tryin' to make the trek north to Rivera? That's a tricky one, after the rains this spring the roads aren't worn in the ground anymore up there, easy to get lost. Watch for broken branches, make sure you keep where you can see other people been passin' through. You're leavin' pretty late in the day too, you'll be at it part of the night at least, so make sure you got plenty of torches. What's that? No, your magic light-makin' won't help here, unless it's an actual fire it won't keep the wolves from gettin' curious enough to take a nibble at you. Oh, and make sure to pack some cinnamon too; that's not for the wolves or anything, you just want to keep your liver in good shape."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: As a student of nature and traveling lore, you have the following benefits:

You have Good Luck on flips to forage for food, water, or other supplies.

You automatically succeed when giving Backup on Survival flips.

Once per day you may make a Survival flip to recall an interesting fact about a relevant subject. It may not be particularly useful, but will provide some amount of wisdom on the topic.

**Prereqs**: Survival 2

**Air of Mystery (Level 2)**

People find it hard to remember exactly who you were or what you were doing just a bit ago. Even when some enterprising soul figures out it's all an act, they still can't quite see past your illusions.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Anyone attempting to investigate anything about you while you were disguised has Bad Luck on any related flips. If someone successfully sees through your disguise and notices that you are an impostor, as long as you wear the disguise they will not be able to determine your actual identity.

**Prereqs**: Masquerade 3

**Analyze Energy (Level 2)**

"What about the one in the dress?"

"That's not a dress, it's the traditional raiment of the Flame Dragon warmage school... three-point contact with the ground, one arm high, power forming in an arc around his other hand... that one's going full offense, avoid those blasts at all costs."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever a creature you can see enters a Spell or Discipline Stance or declares the use of a Spell or Discipline ability, if its Level is no higher than [half your Academics Skill Total] then you instantly know what its effects will be.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 1, Meditation 1

**Analyze Stance (Level 2)**

"Based on how far down the handle he's holding his sword and the fact his left foot is forward but at a slight angle, he's likely a practitioner of the Shiawasena Tanoshī Jikan (幸せな楽しい時間) style of swordplay. Keep your guard up and look for the downward slash; that's your opening to strike."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever a creature you can see enters a Martial Stance or declares the use of a Martial ability, if its Level is no higher than [half your Academics Skill Total] then you instantly know what its effects will be.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3, (Martial Arts or Melee) 1, (Acrobatics or Archery) 1

**Animal Companion (Level 2)**

"This old girl? Found her hurt in the woods a few seasons back, nursed her back to health. Turns out she's pretty vicious in a scrap, and she's had my back ever since."

"You... tied a whiskey flask to your pet wolf?"

"First off, she's not my 'pet'. Second... yeah, she picked up a couple of my bad habits. Nature o' the game."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You train an animal, making it your

Animal Companion

and giving it those statistics. The animal must be friendly enough towards you, which requires that wild animals first be tamed or otherwise made friendly, and you must properly train it, which usually takes a full day (unless the GM states otherwise). Once it is officially your companion, it behaves as though Summoned. You can only have one animal companion at a time, and training a new animal companion will break the bond between you and your old companion, although they will keep the same attitude they had toward you (at first).

**Prereqs**: Survival 3, Presence 2

**Assert Dominance (Level 2)**

"How did I make a wolf into a pet? Well, the first step is you gotta teach the animal who the real alpha is. If it's a wolf, like this little guy here, you gotta look him in the eyes and snarl at him, raise your lip a bit and show some teeth, like this. Scary, right? Now if it's something bigger, like a mother bear, you gotta puff yourself up, widen your stance, just look as big as possible. You do it right, she might not play fetch with you, but she'll figure you're not worth the trouble."

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Target**: An animal within 3 meters.

**Effects**: Make a Survival attack against the target's Mental Defense, and if you flip a Spade you get an Extra Success. If the attack hits, and if the target has no more than [1 + Extra Successes] Health remaining, you cause the animal to submit until the end of the scene. While doing so, it will avoid direct confrontation, will not make any attacks, and will flee from any hostile action against it.

**Special**: At all times, you have Good Luck on flips to calm or handle animals.

**Prereqs**: Survival 3

**Deploy Mounted Launcher (Level 2) [Encounter]**

The cunning tinker is never caught off-guard, and always carries an assortment of parts that can be assembled into a defensive contraption at a moment's notice.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 4 raw materials - may be Metal and/or Wood, with at least 1 of each - within reach

**Target**: An empty space adjacent to you.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Mounted Launcher

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene, after which it is automatically destroyed. When you use this Technique you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Mounted Launcher lasts as long as you keep this Technique committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 3, Academics 2

**First Aid (Level 2)**

With the right supplies and a quick moment of focus you have the medical training to patch up some light wounds.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: A humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the target heals 1 Health, and if you get at least two Extra Successes the target heals an extra 1 Health.

Regardless of result, an individual can only have First Aid used on them once per day.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

**Philosopher's Metallurgy (Level 2)**

Having studied the similarities between all manner of objects and substances, you have begun to grasp some fundamental truths of all matter. By means of subtle manipulations, you can produce surprising products from unlikely resources.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may convert 3 raw materials of any single Type and Level into 1 raw material of that Type with a Level 1 higher. You may convert 2 raw materials of any single Type and Level into 3 raw materials of that Type with a Level 1 lower.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 3, Academics 2

**Re-purpose Materials (Level 2)**

While many craftsmen can work with prepared materials you have a more pragmatic approach and are a deft hand at rendering finished products into serviceable materials for re-use.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may break down existing items into raw materials. This produces the number of materials that the item took to create, split evenly between the material types of the item, and they have the same Level as the item.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 3

**Silent Steps of Shadow (Level 2)**

When you don't wish to be seen you move like a phantom through the shadows; in fact, staying hidden is in your nature so much that you can easily cover for the missteps of your more clumsy allies.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever you and any number of allies would make a Stealth flip together, instead you make your Stealth flip before any allies. For each Extra Success you get on the flip you may give an ally Good Luck on their Stealth flip, although no more than once per ally per flip.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 3

**"Impressive" Reputation (Level 2)**

Your reputation precedes you, although you had to go through the trouble of greasing a few palms to get the stories out into the world. They have a life of their own now, however, and you can invoke them to give pause to people who might doubt you.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: The first time each scene that you try to leverage your reputation, you have Good Luck on a related social flip. This only applies when interacting with someone you have not met before, and only if you use your reputation in a way that matches the social flip.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 3, Performance 1

**Supportive Treatment (Level 2)**

By securing a comfortable, compressing bandage or applying medicinal salve to certain muscle groups, you keep the patient's body operating at full strength under certain kinds of stress.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: A humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the next time the target would gain a stack of Crippled or Slowed instead they don't, and for each Extra Success this prevents an additional stack. This effect is removed if the target gets a full night's rest, or if someone uses this Technique on them again.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

**Animal Companion: Combat Training (Level 3)**

"Okay, you've traveled with Ben for a while. Does he really plan to hunt the bandits by sending a wolf after them? I feel like he's just going to need a new wolf if the-"

"Shh! If he hears you talking like that, he'll make you fight the wolf to prove his point. Listen, all you need to know is the wolf somehow learned Bear Style martial arts, and yes I know that doesn't make any sense. Just... don't ask, please. My arm was sore for a week."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your companion gains the following benefits:

Its Dodge, Bodily, and Mental Defenses each increase by 2.

Its Claw attack's Accuracy increases by 2.

Its War Training ability now lets it wear Medium armor.

Its Claw attack's Damage increases by 1.

These improvements apply to whichever animal is your companion at the time. If you lose a companion and gain a new one, the new one automatically benefits from this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Animal Companion, Survival 4, Presence 3

**Artisan's Eye (Level 3)**

Your discerning eye sees all when it comes to your craft. A moment's glance is enough to tell you exactly how an item was crafted and how it is designed to function.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever making a flip to identify a created item or read a schematic/recipe for such an item, instead of flipping you may act as though you had flipped a 13. When you do so you learn the item's statistics, including what materials it is made from. There may be some situations where certain information is impossible to tell, but if so you will know why.

If you meet the item's Minimum Skill Total to create for its Required Skill, you learn how to create the item and permanently learn it as a recipe for item creation.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 4, Academics 3

**Bespoke Masterpieces (Level 3)**

Where other smiths create obvious, unoriginal armaments, you can tease your creations into strange and unique shapes.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may add a Masterwork enhancement to a Masterwork item that you personally created. The item is used in place of the normal base item for the enhancement.

This increases the item's Level by that of the new enhancement, but you may not raise an item's Level past 6. You cannot add the same Masterwork enhancement to an item a second time.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4

**Catalytic Activation (Level 3)**

Each mixture you brew up has precise amounts of various reagents added in, allowing you to add a little spice to any situation you might find yourself in.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: A Potion or Grenade, that you personally crafted, which you are holding.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following options, which then applies to the target item for the scene.

If the target is a Potion, one of the following applies when drunk:

The imbiber heals 1 Health at the start of their next 3 turns.

Choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. The imbiber gains +1 Soak of the chosen type for 3 rounds.

The imbiber is Hasted 3 times.

If the target is a Grenade:

The Grenade's attack targets anyone adjacent to the initial target. If it already targeted an area, instead that area is increased by 1 meter.

If the Grenade's attack deals damage, it deals 2 extra damage.

If the Grenade's attack hits, the target is Harried 3 times.

When you use this Technique you may choose to commit the Focus spent, and if you do then after the scene ends the effects of the Technique last as long as you keep the Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 4

**Gather the Evidence (Level 3)**

"We're clearly missing something here. We've spoken to those townsfolk at the tavern, checked with a few merchants in the market, and no sign of... hold on a moment, those merchants had some runemetal athames. Judging from what that one woman said earlier, ever since goblins killed the mayor everyone's purses have been a bit light. In a town of this size, unless your economy is doing well you're not going to regularly stock runemetal, you'd go out of business... much less every merchant in town. They said that stock came in recently, too... why did they buy them? And from whom...?

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Choose a subject, then make an Academics flip with Good Luck. The difficulty of the flip depends on the subject and possibly the situation surrounding it.

If you succeed, you learn a useful fact or insight about the situation at hand. This may also take the form of an answer to a question about the situation. If there is an obstacle keeping you from gaining information or insight on the subject, such as some knowledge you are missing, you learn what it is. While you cannot substitute use of this Technique for another Skill, you will learn exactly what Skill can give you that information.

**Prereqs**: Academics 4, Streetwise 1, Insight 1, Survival 1

**Hand of the Chirurgeon (Level 3)**

With a great amount of practice and knowledge you have learned how to perform a variety of life-saving acts of medicine, keeping all manner of illness at bay.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: Yourself or a willing humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: When you use this, choose Bleed or Poison. If used on another patient, they must spend 1 AP as well. Then make a Medicine flip with a difficulty based on what you chose, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success.

Bleed: The difficulty of the flip is 11. If you succeed you remove 1 stack of Bleeding from the target, and for each Extra Success you remove another stack of Bleeding.

Poison: Choose a poison the target is affected by - the difficulty of the flip is the poison's Difficulty. If you succeed then the poison's effects are reduced as though an hour had passed, plus an extra hour for each Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 4

**Hunter's Mark (Level 3)**

"You know, greenhorn, I have a saying - you can tell a lot about someone by their boots. Once you get a good look at someone's footprints, how they walk on the ground, the kind of boots they wear... why, you can learn an awful lot. Boots are how someone talks to the earth, how they go everywhere, how they live their life. The way you walk around tells me where I can find you... and if things come down to a fight, it also tells me how you carry your weight, and how I can throw you off your game. If you got the guts to wear your boots around me, for your sake they'd better be your very best boots."

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature, set of tracks, or some sign of a creature's passing within [Survival Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target creature or creature who left the tracks/sign becomes your mark, lasting until you mark another creature or choose to end the mark. While the creature is marked you have Good Luck on any flip to track them, inquire about them, or otherwise gather information about them. If you hit the marked creature with a weapon attack, they are Slowed once.

**Prereqs**: Survival 4

**Preparation (Level 3)**

The old saying goes "an ounce of prevention is worth a pound of cure", and likewise your boundless intellect and strategy frequently saves the day when you just so happen to have prepared the perfect solution to a problem.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You reveal that you have made preparations for a situation you are currently in. These preparations can include stashing supplies ahead of time in the location you happen to be in, sending a letter or otherwise corresponding with someone about something, having conveniently researched a topic beforehand which has just become relevant, or simply having brought a certain item with you which is now particularly useful. In order to make any preparation which would have cost you money, such as "gaining" a new item by revealing you had it all along, you must pay all costs at market value.

While you can reveal a wide variety of very unlikely preparations with this Technique, there are some that are impossible to have made ahead of time. If a certain good was not available to you when you would have made a particular preparation, or you would not have had knowledge of something to research it ahead of time, or you would have had absolutely no reason to send a letter to someone "just in case something happened", then the GM can rule a preparation is invalid, although this does not necessarily rule out similar preparations.

**Prereqs**: (Insight, Streetwise, or Survival) 4

**Rumormonger (Level 3) [Social] [Encounter]**

You know all the ways a rumor can spread, and are adept at starting rumors then fanning them into wildfires.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: The general social crowd present while in a larger social situation such as a formal party, moderately crowded tavern, or town hall.

**Effects**: You attempt to start a rumor in a larger social situation such as a formal party, moderately crowded tavern, or town hall. Make a Streetwise flip to determine how widely spread and believed this rumor is over the course of the following few days or weeks; the difficulty of the flip will vary based on the circumstances surrounding the subject of the rumor and how believable it is.

Once you have successfully spread a rumor, you may take advantage of it during your turn in a social contest. This gives you Good Luck on one social statement, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. The rumor must somehow be relevant to the matter at hand in order to take advantage of it, and you may only do so once per social contest.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 4

**Ventriloquy (Level 3)**

The art of disguise is less about how you look as it is about how others see you. This goes doubly for what others hear, and with a handful of clever trick you can throw your voice to misdirect a crowd.

**Action**: Varies.

**Target**: An area, creature, or object within [your Masquerade Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a short speech, roughly up to a sentence, then make a Masquerade flip opposed by Awareness flips from anyone who can hear you. Anyone you beat believes that the speech came from the target, although they might eventually be convinced otherwise. At minimum, they will be uncertain and confused for the rest of the scene. Anyone else will not be deceived, but will not necessarily know the source of the speech.

**Prereqs**: Masquerade 4

**Animal Companion: War Training (Level 4)**

"Listen... I'm glad to have your party guarding us on the long road down to Spire. I've heard the stories, I know you're all good for it. But I have a question: what's with the little wooden cart full of bones?"

"Oh, you hear the tale about when we helped the militia in Hayward fight off those barbarian tribes? Yeah, Jen's wolf mauled like a dozen of their warriors and made a nest out of their bones. I told Jen we'd have to get rid of it when it got bugs, but honestly the wolf's pretty tidy about it."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Each time you learn this, choose whether to improve your companion or teach it a new trick.

If you choose to improve your companion, it gains the following benefits:

Its Dodge, Bodily, and Mental Defenses each increase by 2.

Its Claw attack's Accuracy increases by 2.

Its Natural Resist increases by 1.

Its Claw attack's Damage increases by 1.

These improvements apply to whichever animal is your companion at the time. If you lose a companion and gain a new one, the new one automatically benefits from this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Animal Companion, Survival 5, Presence 4

**Battle Calculus (Level 4)**

"If that blow had landed a bit to the left, she wouldn't have been able to block it; looks like that's about the limit of her quickness. And that arrow didn't slow down at all when it struck him, those leathers must be about as thin as they look. Well, looks like a couple well-placed slashes should take the fight out of him, not like she'll be able to jump to his side... time to get to work."

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (varies).

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below. The Interrupt action for this Technique depends on the option chosen:

Interrupt (whenever a combatant you can see applies any of their Resists against an attack)

-  you automatically know the value of that Resist.

Interrupt (whenever a combatant you can see applies one of their Defenses against an attack)

- make an Academics flip against a difficulty of that Defense's current value. If you succeed, you learn exactly what the Defense's current and normal values are.

Interrupt (whenever a combatant you can see makes an attack flip)

- make an Academics flip against a difficulty of that flip's result. If you succeed, you learn exactly what flip's result is, as well as that combatant's total bonus to the attack.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3, Insight 2, (Martial Arts or Melee) 1, (Acrobatics or Archery) 1, Resilience 1, Integrity 1

**Bottomless Bottles (Level 4)**

Each week your fingers are stained a different color from different alchemical reagents. Each time one of your friends gets hurt, or needs a pick-me-up to help them finish writing an important letter, or wants a bottle of acid to clean something with, you have just the thing they need. As a resourceful and cunning mixologist, you always seem to have a little bit of something you can mix together.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Cost**: Any amount of Focus you choose.

**Effects**: You create items with a total Gold cost up to 3 times the Focus spent, and you do not require any materials to create them. These items must have Mixology as their Relevant Skill, and you must meet all requirements to create the item. These items last until you get a full night's rest, at which point they lose their potency and fade away.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 3, Resilience 3, (Streetwise, Academics, or Medicine) 3

**Inspirited Armaments (Level 4)**

The armaments that you craft hold secrets and potential that you can unlock for your allies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: A non-Masterwork item that you personally crafted, which you are touching.

**Effects**: The target item gains a

Masterwork Enhancement

of your choice, lasting until you get a full night's rest. It must be a suitable base item for the enhancement, and you must meet the skill and recipe requirements to apply the enhancement. When you use this Technique you may choose to commit the Focus spent, and instead the item remains Masterwork as long as you keep the Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 3, Resilience 3, (Academics, Integrity, or Meditation) 3

**One of Many Faces (Level 4)**

You have mastered the art of infiltration and imitation. You can move between personas in the space of a breath, blend into a crowd, and adopt a personality so convincingly that you can sway others any way you choose.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You gain the following benefits:

As long as you are not being observed by anyone, you may assume a disguise with Masquerade by spending 4 AP rather than the several minutes it would normally take.

Whenever you disguise yourself as an ordinary or nondescript person, you have Good Luck on any Masquerade flips to remain in disguise or avoid notice.

While you are disguised, you may make Maneuvering social statements with Masquerade as the relevant Skill.

**Prereqs**: Masquerade 5

**Pressure Point Revitalization (Level 4)**

Thanks to your almost encyclopedic knowledge of the human(oid) body, you can tell at a glance where someone's life force pools in their body. By delivering precise pressure to those areas, you can stoke their energy and use it to mend their wounds.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A willing humanoid patient adjacent to you, who must have at least 1 Focus remaining.

**Effects**: The target loses 1 Focus. Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the target heals 1 Health, and for each Extra Success they heal an extra 1 Health.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 5

**Vanish (Level 4) [Encounter]**

With a smoke bomb, great wave of a cloak, or some other obscuring act, you vanish from sight and disappear entirely from view.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Any number of onlookers who can see you.

**Effects**: Make a Stealth attack against the Mental Defense of each target. If it hits, the target cannot see you for 1 round or until you declare an attack against them.

**Special**: You may spend 1 Focus at any time to regain the use of this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 5

---

## General Martial
*Source: `general-martial.html` (Part 1)*

General Martial

**Brace (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You draw a breath, steeling yourself for the struggle ahead.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain 1 temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Might 2, Resistance 1

**Commander's Opening (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

The skilled commander keeps an eye on the flow of battle, directing their allies when they spy an opening.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: A willing ally with whom you can clearly communicate.

**Effects**: You may adjust the target's position in turn order by 1 position.

**Prereqs**: Perform 2, Awareness 1

**Feint (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You quickly fake an attack against a foe, throwing them off-balance.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: A creature within range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Make a Legerdemain flip opposed by the target's Insight. If you win, the target is Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Legerdemain 2, (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 1

**Flashing Steel (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You step into an offensive stance for a moment, proactively countering your foe's assault.

**Action**: Counter - an attack targets your Parry Defense.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack and substitute the result for your Parry Defense against the attack. You ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 2, Cunning 1

**Intense Focus (Level 1) [Martial]**

With a moment of brief concentration, you line up a brilliant attack.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Non-action - you make a weapon attack.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain Good Luck twice on the weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 2, Centering 1

**Lucky Dodge (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You twist out of the path of an attack at the last second, though you probably burn through a bit of your luck in the process.

**Cost**: Discard a card.

**Action**: Counter - an attack targets your Dodge Defense.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Your Dodge Defense becomes [discarded card's value] + [Acrobatics or Martial Arts Skill Total] + [any other bonuses to Dodge Defense]

against the attack. You ignore any stacks of Harried for your Dodge Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 2, Cunning 1

**Adaptive Style (Level 2) [Martial]**

Practicing each maneuver tirelessly and seeing how they flow seamlessly into another, the expert swordsman can adapt to almost any situation in a heartbeat and abandon strategies in favor of seizing opportunity when it arises.

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Martial Encounter Techniques.

**Action**: Non-action.

**Effects**: You regain the use of a Martial Encounter Technique with a level of

[expended Techniques' combined level] / 2

or lower, or until the end of your turn you may use such a Technique without expending the use of it.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3, (Cunning or Intelligence) 2

**Call to Arms (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

As combat breaks your allies spring into action on your command, acting as a well-oiled machine under your expert guidance.

**Action**: Non-action - as combat starts, before Reflex flips are made.

**Target**: Up to [half your Diplomacy Skill Total] allies with whom you can clearly communicate.

**Effects**: The target allies have Good Luck on their Reflex flips, and you have Bad Luck on your Reflex flip.

**Prereqs**: Perform 3, Academics 2

**Challenge (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You bellow out a challenge to an enemy, drawing their attention away from your allies and forcing them to face you.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: A hostile creature within [twice your Intimidate Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for 3 rounds.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 3, Integrity 2

**Cry of Victory (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

As you strike down an enemy you bellow out

**Action**: Counter - you Down a hostile creature.

**Target**: Any number of hostile creatures within [twice your Intimidate Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the Mental Defense of each hostile target. Each target hit is Crippled for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 3, Might 2

**Evasive Drift (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your reflexes are honed to near perfection, allowing you to slide away from an attack as it connects, turning an otherwise lethal strike into a grazing blow.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Counter -  you are hit by an attack against which you applied your Dodge Defense.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**:  Make an Acrobatics flip, and if you flip a Diamond you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 you reduce the Health you would lose from the attack by 1. For every 2 points your result is above 9, you reduce the Health you would lose from the attack by an extra 1.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Cunning 2

**Fluid Movement (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You are a blur in combat, capable of dancing about your enemies before they can react.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Shift up to 3 meters.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 3, Cunning 2

**Muscles Make Morale (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your immaculate musculature inspires your comrades, urging them on to glorious battle.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Up to [half your Might Skill Total] allies who can see you.

**Effects**: All targets re-flip any cards 4 or lower on their next attack for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Perform 2

**Rage (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You can fly into a bloodrage in combat, making you a fearsome but reckless warrior.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You enter a rage until the end of the scene. While in the rage the following applies to you:

You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

If one of your weapon attacks is Parried, or if the target's Soak would reduce the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

If a target Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks they are Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Resistance 2

**Twin Fangs (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Wielding two weapons in deadly rhythm, you lash out with a series of strikes.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of both of your weapons.

**Effects**: Make an attack with one of your weapons, then make an attack with your other weapon.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Martial Arts, or Melee 3), Acrobatics 2

**Exert Pressure (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Standing strong in combat, flexing, your presence in a pitched melee is impossible to ignore.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Any number of creatures adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 4, Integrity 3

**Moment of Celerity (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a split-second of intense concentration you can force your body's energy to surge forth, acting with blinding speed for a brief period of time.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Gain an extra Main action this turn.

**Prereqs**: (Might or Centering) 4, (Strength, Dexterity, or Stamina) 3

**Seize the Moment (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly monitoring the flow of battle, you are capable of holding your attack for a moment to wait for a perfect opportunity to capitalize on.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You may adjust your position in turn order to one of your choice.

**Prereqs**:  (Awareness or Insight) 3, (Dexterity or Cunning) 2

**Tumble (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a slight but carefully calculated movement you drop into a defensive stance for but a moment before springing back into action.

**Action**: Counter - an attack targets your Dodge Defense.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain a + [half your Acrobatics Skill Total] bonus to your Dodge Defense against the attack. If you Dodge the attack you may Shift 1 meter.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 4, Cunning 3

**War Cry (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A time-honored tradition across the land, from nomad barbarians to gladiators fighting in pits for the amusement of crowds, and even in negotiations in less respectable establishments, letting loose a ferocious scream can shake even the strongest fighters at times. Unfortunately, as Thorrgar the mighty berserker-turned-merchant learned, screaming about bathing in the entrails of someone rarely works out well on the senate floor.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: A creature within [Intimidate Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits the target is Frightened by you for 3 rounds.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 4, Might 3

**Battlemaster's Iron Howl (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a ferocious yell you shake your foes down to their bones, pulling their attention solely to you and causing chills to run down their spines if they try to turn away from you.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Intimidate Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for 3 rounds.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 5, Integrity 4

**Cleave Magic (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your education in intermediate magical theory has progressed to the point where you are capable of seeing threads of magic being pulled together and sent towards you, and by launching a counterattack at the focal point of the spell you can disperse the energies in a flash of violence.

**Action**: Counter - you are the only target of a spell attack.

**Target**: The attacker who made the spell attack.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack and use the result in place of whichever Defense the spell attack is against. If the spell attack misses, then it instead targets your attacker and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 5, Academics 2, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Frenzy (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You are capable of losing yourself entirely in your bloodlust and fury, entering a berserk state in which you abandon any semblance of self-preservation and launch yourself into your foes.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You enter a frenzy until the end of the scene. While in the frenzy the following applies to you:

While in the frenzy you have Good Luck on all Might flips.

You ignore any stacks of Crippled, Slowed, Weakened, or Wounded you have.

You re-flip any cards 4 or lower for weapon attacks.

You suffer a -3 penalty to Dodge Defense, and you may not Parry.

At the end of your turn, if you are aware of an enemy and do not move towards them or make an attack against them, you gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Might 5, Resistance 4

**Gaze of the Gladiator (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your body has been sculpted by a thousand sword swings, and when you stand in the arena your opponents hesitate to strike at your imposing musculature.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Intimidate Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Frightened by you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 5, Might 4

**Skin of Stone (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You draw a breath and brace yourself, allowing your extremely sturdy physical form to take a tremendous assault with almost no harm.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain [half your Resistance Skill Total] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 4, Might 3

**Final Feint Strikes the Heart (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"He was known as a peerless warrior in his homeland, rumored to have never lost a single fight and never failed to slay a single enemy. They said that even in encounters where the man traded blows evenly with his opponents, or even went on the defensive and appeared to be at a disadvantage, regardless of the circumstances he would lash out with a terrible blow of his spear and skewer the hearts of his foes, none of whom could defend against his fury."

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Make three weapon attacks against the target. The first two weapon attacks deal no damage, but if either of them hit then the third attack ignores the target's Armor Soak. For the third attack you have Good Luck and may treat any card flipped as a Heart.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 5, Dexterity 5, Cunning 5

**The Gatekeeper (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"Your entire division was defeated by that band of rebels?"

"It was their commander. She stood at the gate to their camp, blocking our way even though we could have easily scaled the walls. She spoke, in a calm voice that seemed to carry across the valley, 'through me, first'. Our ladderbearers faltered, our archers couldn't take their eyes off of her. A rebel battlemage sent fire raining from behind the walls, and our lined scattered and ran, constantly looking over their shoulders at the commander until they were all back in the woods."

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Any number of creatures who can see or hear you.

**Effects**: Make an Intimidate attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 5, Integrity 5, Charisma 5

**Unmovable Stone Anchor (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A mighty mountain in the middle of a blizzard, when you stand tall you are capable of withstanding onslaughts that would outright kill lesser men, emerging unscathed from the most terrible of carnage.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You gain infinite temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Might 5, Resistance 5, Stamina 5

**Veil of Dispersing Shadows (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You have achieved an unmatched degree of agility, with a knack for avoiding harm that borders on precognition. With this maneuver, the pinnacle of its kind, as a blow strikes you your form shifts imperceptibly and the attack seems to narrowly glance through your shadow.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Counter - you are hit by an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense.

**Target**: The attack that hit you.

**Effects**: You ignore all effects of the attack, as though the target attack were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are still affected normally.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 5, Insight 5, Dexterity 5

---

## General Martial
*Source: `general-martial_1.html` (Part 2)*

General Martial

**Brace (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You draw a breath, steeling yourself for the struggle ahead.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You gain 1 temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 2, Might 1

**Commander's Opening (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

The skilled commander keeps an eye on the flow of battle, directing their allies when they spy an opening.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 1.

**Target**: A willing ally within [Rapport Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target may adjust their position in turn order by up to [Power] positions.

**Prereqs**: Rapport 2, Insight 1

**Feint (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You quickly fake an attack against a foe, throwing them off-balance.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make an attack with your chosen weapon. Instead of a normal attack, this flip is opposed by the target's Insight, and if you win the target is Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Insight 2, (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 1

**Flashing Steel (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You step into an offensive stance for a moment, proactively countering your foe's assault.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is made against your Parry Defense, after the attack flip but before it hits or misses you).

**Power**: When you use this Technique make a weapon attack, but instead of being a normal attack this Technique's Power is equal to the result, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack its Power is increased by 1.

**Effects**: Your Parry Defense is instead [Power] against the attack, and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 2, Insight 1

**Lucky Dodge (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You twist out of the path of an attack at the last second, though you probably burn through a bit of your luck in the process.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is made against your Dodge Defense, after the attack flip but before it hits or misses you).

**Cost**: Discard a card.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [discarded card's value] + [Acrobatics or Brawl Skill Total] + [any other bonuses to Dodge Defense]

; if you discarded a Diamond, its Power is increased by 1.

**Effects**: Your Dodge Defense is instead [Power] against the attack, and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Dodge Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 2, Insight 1

**Perfect Strike (Level 1) [Martial]**

With a moment of brief concentration, you line up a brilliant attack.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you declare a weapon attack).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You gain Good Luck twice on the weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 2, Meditation 1

**Adaptive Style (Level 2) [Martial]**

Practicing each maneuver tirelessly and endlessly studying how they flow seamlessly into another, the expert fighter can adapt to almost any situation in a heartbeat and abandon strategies in favor of seizing opportunity when it arises.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Martial Encounter Techniques.

**Effects**: You regain the use of one Martial Encounter Technique. The Technique's level cannot be higher than either half of the total levels of Techniques expended to use this Technique, nor [half your Academics Skill Total] .

**Prereqs**: Academics 3

**Call to Arms (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

As combat breaks your allies spring into Action Points on your command, acting as a well-oiled machine under your expert guidance.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (combat begins, before Reflex flips are made).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Rapport Skill Total] .

**Target**: Up to [Power] allies within [Rapport Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target allies have Good Luck on their Reflex flips, and you have Bad Luck on your Reflex flip.

**Prereqs**: Rapport 3, Academics 2

**Challenge (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You bellow out a challenge to an enemy, drawing their attention away from your allies and forcing them to face you.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A hostile creature within [Presence Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Presence 3, Integrity 1, Performance 1

**Cry of Victory (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

As you strike down an enemy you bellow out

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you Down a hostile creature).

**Target**: Any number of hostile creatures within [Presence Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Crippled once.

**Prereqs**: Presence 3, Might 1, Performance 1

**Evasive Drift (Level 2) [Martial]**

Concentrating your reflexes for a brief moment, you slide away from an attack as it connects to turn an otherwise lethal strike into a grazing blow.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense hits you, before its effects are resolved).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**:  Make an Acrobatics flip against a difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed you reduce the Health you would lose from the attack by 1, and for each Extra Success you reduce that Health by an additional 1.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

Fluid Movement (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter] You are a blur in combat, capable of dancing about your enemies before they can react.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: Shift up to 3 meters.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 3

Muscles Make Morale (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter] You display your immaculate musculature in a way that inspires your comrades, urging them on to glorious battle.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Might Skill Total] .

**Target**: Up to [Power] allies within [Might Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: All targets re-flip any cards 4 or lower on the next attack they each make, within 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Performance 3, Might 2

**Rage (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You can fly into a bloodrage in combat, making you a fearsome but reckless warrior.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You enter a rage until the end of the scene.

While in the rage the following applies to you:

You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

If one of your weapon attacks is Parried, or if the target's Resist would reduce the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

If a target Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Athletics 1, Resilience 1

**Turn of Tactics (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

In a brief moment the turn of battle can change, and commanders that fail to act in these moments are doomed to failure. Knowing this, you keep an eye out for these crucial moments and guide your allies to take advantage of the shift.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Rapport Skill Total] .

**Target**: Any number of willing allies within [Rapport Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You may adjust the targets' positions in turn order as you choose, but by no more than a total of [Power] positions.

**Prereqs**: Rapport 3, Insight 2

**Twin Fangs (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Wielding two weapons in deadly rhythm, you lash out with a series of strikes.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Target**: A creature in range of two of your weapons.

**Effects**: Make an attack with one of your weapons, then make an attack with your other weapon.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee 3), Acrobatics 2

**Bone-shaking Iron Howl (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You unleash a ferocious roar that shakes your enemies to their bones, causing their legs to tremble as they step away from you.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 2.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Presence Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the target's Mental Defense. Each target hit is slowed Slowed [Power] times.

**Prereqs**: Presence 4, Might 2, Performance 1

Bull Rush (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter] Barreling through the battlefield, you knock aside your enemies and shove them away from you like rag dolls.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: Shift up to [half your Speed] meters. When you use this Technique, and for each meter you shift, you may make an opposed Might flip against an adjacent creature, and if you win you may Push them 1 meter away from you.

**Prereqs**: Might 4, (Brawl or Athletics) 3

**Emboldening Bellow (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your enduring, relentless cry gives heart to your companions and drives them to fight on through any adversity.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Performance Skill Total] .

**Target**: Up to [Power] allies within [Performance Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Each target gains 1 temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Performance 4, Resilience 3

**Exert Pressure (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Standing strong in combat, flexing, your presence in a pitched melee is impossible to ignore.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Presence 4, Integrity 2, Performance 1

**Rhythmic Battlecry (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A short song or poem of increasing intensity urges your compatriots to move with a quickening tempo.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Performance Skill Total] .

**Target**: Up to [Power] allies within [Performance Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Each target may Shift 1 meter.

**Prereqs**: Performance 4, Awareness 3

**Seize the Moment (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly monitoring the flow of battle, you are capable of holding your attack for a moment to wait for a perfect opportunity to capitalize on.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: Adjust your position in turn order to one of your choice.

**Prereqs**:  (Awareness or Insight) 4

**Skin of Stone (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You briefly strengthen your body, allowing your extremely sturdy physical form to take a tremendous assault with almost no harm.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You gain [half your Resilience Skill Total] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 4, (Athletics or Might) 3

**Tumble (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a slight but carefully calculated movement you roll away from an attack and assume a more advantageous position.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is made against your Dodge Defense, after the attack flip but before it hits or misses you).

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of [half your Acrobatics Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You gain a + [Power] bonus to your Dodge Defense against the attack. If you Dodge the attack, you may Shift 1 meter.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 4, Insight 3

**Cleave Magic (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your education in intermediate magical theory has progressed to the point where you are capable of seeing threads of magic being pulled together and sent towards you, and by launching a counterattack at the focal point of the spell you can disperse the energies in a flash of violence.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a spell attack is declared against you, but no other targets).

**Power**: When you use this Technique make a weapon attack, but instead of being a normal attack this Technique's Power is equal to the result, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack its Power is increased by 1.

**Effects**: The Defense the spell attack targets is instead [Power] , and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Dodge or Parry Defense against the attack. If the spell attack misses, then it instead targets your attacker, is treated as having a flip result of [Power] , and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Academics 2, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Cloak and Dagger (Level 4) [Martial] [Subtle] [Encounter]**

"Lord Highcastle! How did you manage to convince the boss of that criminal gang to stop interfering in our trade routes?"

"Well you see, my dear chamberlain, I agreed to meet the lady in her favorite ale house, and when I sat down at the table I projected an aura of firm confidence, never broke eye contact, and greeted her with a cordial yet strong voice. Then, when she was too busy trying to take the measure of my character, I stabbed her in the heart, arranged her cloak so it looked like she was simply napping on the table, and casually strolled out of the establishment."

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you declare a weapon attack).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Make a Legerdemain flip opposed by the Awareness of each target of the weapon attack. Any targets who lose the flip are Unaware of the attack.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 5

**Frenzy (Level 4)**

You are capable of losing yourself entirely in your bloodlust and fury, entering a berserk state in which you abandon any semblance of self-preservation and launch yourself into your foes.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You enter a frenzy until the end of the scene. While in the frenzy the following applies to you:

While in the frenzy you have Good Luck on all Might flips.

You ignore any stacks of Crippled, Slowed, Weakened, or Wounded you have.

You re-flip any cards 4 or lower for weapon attacks.

You suffer a -3 penalty to Dodge Defense, and you may not Parry.

At the end of your turn, if you are aware of an enemy and do not move towards them or make an attack against them, you gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Might 5,

Athletics 2, Resilience 2

**War Cry (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A time-honored tradition across the land, from nomad barbarians to gladiators fighting in pits for the amusement of crowds, and even in negotiations in less respectable establishments, letting loose a ferocious scream can shake even the strongest fighters at times. Unfortunately, as Thorrgar the mighty berserker-turned-merchant learned, screaming about bathing in the entrails of someone rarely works out well on the senate floor.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Presence Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits the target is Unbalanced for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Presence 5, Might 2, Performance 2

**Final Feint Strikes the Heart (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"He was known as a peerless warrior in his homeland, rumored to have never lost a single fight and never failed to slay a single enemy. They said that even in encounters where the man traded blows evenly with his opponents, or even went on the defensive and appeared to be at a disadvantage, regardless of the circumstances he would lash out with a terrible blow of his spear and skewer the hearts of his foes, none of whom could defend against his fury."

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: A creature within range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against the target's Dodge Defense, and you may treat any card flipped for this attack as a Heart. If the weapon attack hits, for each Extra Success on the attack flip you are considered to have Gambled once.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, (Acrobatics or Might) 5, (Awareness or Insight) 5

**The Gatekeeper (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"Your entire division was defeated by that band of rebels?"

"It was their commander. She stood at the gate to their camp, attempting to block our way; as though we couldn't have easily scaled their walls! She spoke, in a calm voice that seemed to carry across the valley: "Through me, first". Our ladderbearers faltered, our archers couldn't take their eyes off of her. As our soldiers hesitated, a rebel battlemage from behind the walls sent fire raining onto our lines. They all broke and ran, constantly looking over their shoulders at the commander until they were back in the woods."

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Any number of creatures who can see or hear you.

**Effects**: Make a Presence attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Presence 5, Integrity 5, Performance 5

**Unbreakable Stone Anchor (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A mighty mountain in the middle of a blizzard, when you stand tall you are capable of withstanding onslaughts that would outright kill lesser men, emerging unscathed from the most terrible of carnage.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (during your turn).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You gain infinite temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Might 5, Resilience 5, Body 5

**Veil of Dispersing Shadows (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You have achieved an unmatched degree of agility, with a knack for avoiding harm that borders on precognition. With this maneuver, the pinnacle of its kind, as a blow strikes you your form shifts imperceptibly and the attack seems to narrowly glance through your shadow.

**Action Points**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense hits you, before its effects are resolved).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You ignore all effects of the attack, as though it were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are still affected normally.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 5, Insight 5, Agility 5

---

## General Spells
*Source: `general-spells.html` (Part 2)*

General Spells are a loose group of Spells that do useful tricks.

**Conjure Armament (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

You conjure a weapon. Or maybe a suit of armor. You know, whatever you're feeling at the time.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Yourself, an ally, or a space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Special**: This Spell has a Capacity equal to the number of times you know it.

**Effects**: You conjure up to [Capacity] weapons, pieces of armor, or pieces of ammunition of a type of your choice. A conjured armament functions as a regular weapon, armor, or piece of ammunition and lasts until you choose to dismiss it, which you may do at any time. You may have no more than [Capacity] such armaments conjured at a time, and if conjuring a new armament exceeds this limit then the oldest conjured armament(s) will be dismissed until you no longer exceed the limit.

If you target yourself or a willing ally, the armament appears in the target's hand(s) or on the target's body, depending on what is conjured. If you target an unwilling ally or a space, the armament appears on the ground target's space.

When you declare an attack you may cast this Spell as part of the same action, costing no AP, and allowing you to use the conjured weapon for that attack. When doing so, you only conjure armaments for that attack.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Placeholder's Accurate Artistry (Level 1) [General Spell]**

This spell animates a pen, brush, chalk, or other tool of artistry and causes it to rapidly dance across a piece of paper or other surface, drawing a full panorama of what is in front of you in the blink of an eye.

**Action**: 4 AP.

**Cost**: Drawing material (such as ink, a pencil, or chalk) and a medium on which to draw (such as a piece of paper, parchment, or wood).

**Target**: A visual scene in front of you, or a document no more than one page which you can clearly visually examine.

**Effects**: Depending on your target, you either sketch a scene or copy a document. When sketching a scene the Spell draws everything in your line of sight in one direction, including details you might not notice at the time of casting. When copying a document the Spell makes a copy that perfectly mirrors the contents and reproduces the overall document as accurately as possible with the materials you use.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Placeholder's Covert Conclave (Level 1) [General Spell]**

Stitching a tapestry of shadows, or beckoning minor spirits of secrecy and midnight, you create a minor magical barrier around a meeting place that makes it difficult for onlookers to listen in or watch your discussions.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Target**: An area which must be at least somewhat secluded or out of the way, no larger than [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters in any direction.

**Effects**: You ward the target area against eavesdroppers and prying eyes for the scene. While the area is warded anyone who is outside the area has Bad Luck on any flips to see, hear, or otherwise sense anything inside the area other than what is immediately apparent - this includes overhearing any discussions, reading lips, seeing any documents or any other objects that someone in the area is trying to conceal from outside eyes, and so on. Onlookers will notice the effect, although they might not know why it is harder to eavesdrop, and the Spell does nothing to conceal basic facts about the meeting, such as who is in attendance.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Stealth 1

**Placeholder's Instantaneous Instrument (Level 1) [General Spell]**

In a jubilant mood during a night of unparalleled drinking the group of students alleged to be responsible for the fame of the great wizard Placeholder decided they were in the mood for a good song, but they quickly realized that none of them had brought an instrument with them to play music with. Though the collective memory of what followed is hazy at best, in the morning they found a handful of notes they had scrawled that detailed an alteration of the Conjure Weapon spell that instead created a musical instrument. There was, however, no explanation for how one of them managed to lose two fingers on his left hand in the process of creating the spell.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you cast Conjure Armament, in addition to being able to conjure armaments you may also conjure any standard musical instrument with which you are familiar. These instruments are otherwise treated as conjured armaments for the purposes of the Spell.

**Prereqs**: Conjure Armament, Performance 1

**Placeholder's Tender Telekinesis (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

One of the most prominent of Placeholder's legendary one-hundred-and-one incantations, his Tender Telekinesis has become both a favorite of practical magicians and magical instructors alike. Once mastered, through it the caster may leverage small amounts of magical energy to lift small items, pour water, and keep an umbrella over their head without using their hands.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: One mundane object with a Weight no higher than 1 or up to 1 liter of liquid, which must be within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters. You may choose one target for each time you know this Spell.

**Effects**: You conjure a magical force to move the target object(s), causing them to levitate and allowing you to manipulate them and move them no more than [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters. Any use of the object more complicated than pushing, pulling, or lifting the object costs an extra 1 AP and requires an Elementalism or Theurgy flip in addition to any other relevant flip(s). This Spell may not be used to make an attack of any kind. If you stop casting the Spell, or spend a turn without casting this Spell, any target objects fall to the ground.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Might 1

**Placeholder's Wizardly Witchlight (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**
