Another classic spell attributed to the arcanist pseudonym Placeholder, the Witchlight was used by many mischievous students for all sorts of immoral endeavors. By use of the Witchlight one can conjure a torch flame in a variety of colors to illuminate an area around them; in addition the nature of the magic allows the flame to be extinguished and lit with ease, which lends itself nicely to shadowy dealings. However, as one of the more benign and utilitarian of Placeholder's spells this one spread quickly once leaked from the group of students who originally developed it and is now considered a staple of the practical arcanist,  much to many a headmaster's chagrin.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Effects**: You create a flame of any size up to the size of your palm, floating within your space as you wish and casting any amount of light as you choose up to that of a torch. This flame burns in any color of the rainbow you choose, you may alter its size and color at will, and the flame feels gently warm to your touch, although it affects any other people or objects as a normal flame would. The flame lasts until you either lose consciousness or choose to dismiss it, which you may do at any time, and you may have one flame active at a time for each time you know this Spell.

When you cast this Spell you may choose to make the flame ethereal. While in this state only you can see the flame or the light that it casts, and to all others it and its light are invisible. You may spend 1 AP to return the flame to normal, or cause it to become ethereal if it is normal.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Awareness 1

**Quick Sober (Level 1) [General Spell]**

The spell more formally known as 'Quick Sober' is actually a variety of spells, each with their own methodologies and names, developed independently by soothsayers and hedge mages in almost every civilization for the noble purpose of using magic to help someone who's had way too much to drink.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You remove all intoxicants from the target's system, such as alcohol and other drugs, although this does not affect actual poisons the target is afflicted with. If the target does not wish to be made sober, you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against their Bodily Defense to successfully affect them.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Mixology 1

**Summon Debris Golem (Level 1) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the basics of the summoning arts is the debris golem, conjured by a simple but fundamental practice of binding inert matter together with faint amount of magical power. While many summoners quickly fall out of the practice once they learn more intricate summonings, some magical practitioners make frequent use of debris golems for a variety of purposes, like holding coats and opening doors.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An empty space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Debris Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Debris Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Blood Magic (Level 2) [General Spell]**

Mastering the power within your own blood, you can draw it forth to power your spells when your magic begins to wane.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Any amount of Health.

**Effects**: You may regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique with Level equal to the Health paid to cast this Spell.

**Prereqs**: (Resilience or Meditation) 3

**Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana (Level 2) [General Spell]**

"...and the great wizard Placeholder, master of magicks, was said to know a hundred and one incantations and could weave all manner of magicks in circles around lesser spellcasters. For every opponent placed in his way he employed a variety of spellcraft seemingly designed to defeat that opponent in particular, and each of these was executed with the mastery of someone who had spent a lifetime perfecting it. Truly, he was a king ruling over every manner of wizardry."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. III

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Spell Encounter Techniques.

**Effects**: You regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique. That Technique's Level cannot be greater than half of the total Levels of Techniques you expended as a cost for this.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3

**Placeholder's Sneaky Storage (Level 2) [General Spell]**

This is one of the many spells developed by an enterprising group of students at a prestigious wizarding academy. These students, in order to avoid any repercussions for the mischief caused by their spells, attributed all of them to a made-up arcanist by the name of Placeholder, which greatly confused the paperwork of the headmasters of the school in trying to figure out who was responsible. This spell in particular creates a small extra-dimensional space and stores items inside of it, invisible to any searching eyes as well as weighing nothing on the caster.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Effects**: You manifest a small magical space which you can store objects into and retrieve objects from. The space can hold objects totaling a Weight of 2. The space is always considered an easily-accessible location for storing and retrieving objects. When you store objects in the space they disappear from your hand as though placed into an invisible bag, and when you retrieve objects from the space it either appears in your hand(s) or at your feet.

The space lasts indefinitely, although you may end the Spell's effects at any time, and when you lose consciousness for any reason the Spell's effects end. When the Spell's effects end, all contents of the extra-dimensional space are emptied into adjacent empty spaces.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Stealth 2

**Prepared Magic (Level 2) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

Superior control over your magic allows you to begin shaping magic without releasing it, readying the spell to leap from your fingertips.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: When you cast this Spell, choose another Spell you know that takes no more than 2 AP to cast. During your next turn you may cast that Spell for 0 AP.

You can only have one Spell prepared in this way at a time. If you cast this Spell while you already have a Spell prepared, the previous Spell is no longer prepared.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Integrity 1

**Raise Corpse (Level 2) [General Spell]**

The cornerstone of popular necromancy and a timeless classic, this spell binds magical power into a dead body to animate it under your command.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A pile of humanoid remains within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon the target pile of remains as a

Reanimated Corpse

.

You may control up to [Mind] corpses at any time. If you raise another corpse beyond this limit, your oldest raised corpse will be de-animated. You may withdraw control from, and thereby de-animate, any of your controlled corpses on your turn without spending any AP.

If you raise remains that are larger or smaller than a standard humanoid, for the purposes of your limit of corpses controlled at a single time they will count as a number of corpses equal to their volume as measured in standard humanoid corpses (minimum 1). However, larger corpses raised by this spell will still use the Reanimated Corpse statistics for combat, and smaller corpses will usually have worse combat statistics.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Medicine 2

**Summon Spectre (Level 2) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You summon a low-level shade, either bringing one from the Otherlands or forming one from ambient shadow energy. It aids you in combat, attempting to claw the life from your enemies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: An empty space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Spectre

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Spectre lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 2

**Practical Necromancy (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Well-learned in practical applications of your magic, you have learned to do more than simply raise corpses and rip the life from beings. A multi-disciplinary approach gives you ability to greatly modify remains before animating them, allowing for a great deal of creativity in your day-to-day problem solving.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Any items you create from the remains of a living being can be animated with Raise Corpse. This allows any 'joints' and the like to move as they would on a normal reanimated corpse, giving crafted devices a way to move around. These are treated as Summoned creatures, although their combat statistics will not be better than those of a Reanimated Corpse, and are unlikely to ever be relevant.

These animated devices count against your maximum number of raised corpses controlled for Raise Corpse. For this limit, determine how many humanoid corpses worth of remains were needed to make the device, and the device counts as

half

that many raised corpses.

**Prereqs**: Raise Corpse, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Craft 2, Medicine 1

**Spellblade (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your practice with both sorcery and martial combat has given you the ability to seamlessly integrate magic with your swordplay, archery, or fighting style of choice, mixing magical power in with your weapon strikes.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Spell Encounter Technique.

**Target**: A creature in range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make a weapon attack which is modified by the chosen option.

Regardless of the type of spell expended, you may choose one of these options:

The attack has its range increased by [twice the expended spell's level] meters.

If the attack hits, the target is Pushed [twice the expended spell's level] meters away from you.

If the attack hits, it deals no damage and instead the target gains [expended spell's level] stacks of Bleeding.

You gain a + [half expended spell's level] stacks of Fire, Frost, or Shadow Ward.

If you expended an Elementalism Spell, you may choose one of these options:

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Vulnerable [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Necrotic [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack Downs the target, they are instantly killed and you draw a card.

If you expended a Theurgy Spell, you may choose one of these options:

Before or after the attack, you may Shift up to [expended spell's level] meters.

You gain [expended Spell's level] stacks of Protected.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended spell's level] times.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 3

**Swordsoul (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your understanding of both martial combat and magical theory allows you to use your martial forms to work magic, guiding energy with the same motions that you use to weave attacks through an opponent's defenses. Through this practice you can launch bolts of magical force that harry and drive back your foes as though you were fencing with a blade or swinging with a warhammer.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Martial Encounter Technique.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The attack deals

[expended Technique's level] + [Mind]

Physical damage, and is modified by the chosen option:

You may adjust your position in turn order by up to [expended Technique's level] positions.

Before or after the attack, you may Teleport up to [twice the expended Technique's level] meters.

You gain [expended Technique's level] stacks of Protected.

If the attack is Parried, the target gains [expended Technique's level] stacks of Bleeding.

If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back [twice the expended Technique's level] meters.

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Harried [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for [expended Technique's level] rounds.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 3

**Telepathic Link (Level 3) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

You trace a magical link between you and an ally, allowing telepathic communication while the link lasts.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself and/or any number of allies you touch, up to a total of [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] creatures for each time you know this Spell.

**Effects**: All targets are linked telepathically and may communicate telepathically at a much faster rate than normal conversation, allowing entire ideas and sentences to be communicated almost instantly. Any linked allies who are farther than [Mind] kilometers from you cannot hear or "speak" through the link until they return within range. The link lasts until you dismiss it, which you may do at any time, and any linked allies may remove themselves from the link at any time. You may only have one instance of this Spell active at a time.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Rapport 3

**Spell-Rending Arcanology (Level 4) [General Spell] [Stance]**

Practiced mostly by the sort of arcanist who went to a prestigious wizarding school and was top of his class and made sure everyone knew it, sufficient knowledge of the magical arts allows one to recognize the threads of magic another spellcaster is weaving and sunder them by use of overwhelming magical force, dissipating the incantation entirely.

**Effects**: When a creature within [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters begins casting a Spell, you can tell what level of Spell it is.

You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (a creature within [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters begins casting a Spell) and expend the use of a Spell Encounter Technique of a higher level than the creature's Spell. Then you make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of that creature, and if the attack hits the target's Spell is Countered. If the target's Spell required Focus to cast then you must spend an equal amount of Focus, otherwise the Spell is not Countered.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Academics 4

**Summon Earthen Golem (Level 4) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause the earth itself to form a golem and fight by your side.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Earthen Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it as well as the Focus spent to cast it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Earthen Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell and Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Survival 4

---

## General Spells
*Source: `general-spells_1.html` (Part 1)*

**Conjure Armament (Level 1) [General Spell]**

You conjure a weapon. Or maybe a suit of armor. You know, whatever you're feeling at the time.

**Action**: Extra, or part of the same action as an attack

(see below)

.

**Target**: Yourself, an ally, or a space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Special**: This Spell has a Capacity of 1.

**Effects**: You conjure up to [Capacity] weapons, pieces of armor, or pieces of ammunition of a type of your choice. A conjured armament functions as a regular weapon, armor, or piece of ammuntion and lasts until you choose to dismiss it, which you may do as a Non-action. You may have no more than [Capacity] such armaments conjured at a time, and if conjuring a new armament exceeds this limit then the oldest conjured armament(s) will be dismissed until you no longer exceed the limit.

If you target yourself or a willing ally, the armament appears in the target's hand(s) or on the target's body, depending on what is conjured. If you target an unwilling ally or a space, the armament appears on the ground target's space.

You may cast this Spell as part of the same action you use to make a weapon attack, as long as you only conjure armaments for that attack.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases its Capacity by 1.

**Freeze/Scorch (Level 1) [General Spell]**

One of the fundamental lessons of magical study is how many energies exist in dual forms in the world. The most obvious of these is heat and cold, and once an apprentice spellcaster has understood this they are capable of moving that energy into or from an object to affect its temperature.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A mundane object, no larger than a campfire, within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Choose whether you want to heat or chill the object. You may heat the object enough that water will boil and combustible items such as tinder or cloth will catch on fire. You may chill the object enough that water will freeze and other objects will be affected as though kept in an ice chest.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Resistance 1

**Glittering Trail (Level 1) [General Spell]**

Tracing glittering magic around you and your friends, you make sure that you will be able to follow each other's tracks even in the most twisting labyrinths.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself and up to [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] allies you touch.

**Effects**: For the scene each target leaves a trail of glittering light behind them wherever they go. Each target may choose the color of their trail when the Spell is cast, and regardless of color all targets of the Spell can tell whose trail is whose. These trails do not provide any real illumination, may only be seen by targets of the Spell and last for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Identify Magic (Level 1) [General Spell]**

By sending out tendrils of magic and focusing your perception on them, you can feel out the presence of magic in the area.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Magical effects within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism or Theurgy flip, with Good Luck, to detect and/or identify any magical effects within the target area. This can include persistent effects of spells, as well as any enchanted or magical items such as Masterwork items created through magical means. This spell can only affect a magical effect once per scene.

Prereqs: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Academics 1

**Minor Illusion (Level 1) [General Spell]**

~

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You may either create a phantasmal image or a sound. If you create a sound it originates from the target point, and if you create an image it appears within the target point, although it can be no larger than a cubic meter. The sound or image can be of anything you can imagine/are familiar with, however anyone who sees the image or hears the sound automatically knows that it is fake. You may maintain the illusion indefinitely, although you can only have one instance of this Spell active at any given time.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Insight 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the Spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**Placeholder's Accurate Artistry (Level 1) [General Spell]**

This spell animates a pen, brush, chalk, or other tool of artistry and causes it to rapidly dance across a piece of paper or other surface, drawing a full panorama of what is in front of you in the blink of an eye.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: See below.

**Effects**: Casting this Spell requires drawing material such as ink, a pencil, or chalk, and a surface to draw upon such as paper, parchment, or wood. Over the course of the round the drawing implement is used to create an image on your chosen medium, drawing one of the following:

The entire scene within your line of sight is drawn, including every single detail in as fine detail as is possible at the distance you are when you cast the Spell, including objects or creatures you might not notice when you cast the Spell

A perfectly accurate copy is made of a document as long as you can freely examine the document while casting the Spell, although you only have to do so visually. The materials you use may differ from those the document was created from, but in all other ways the image you create will mirror the copied document as best it can

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Placeholder's Covert Conclave (Level 1) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

~

**Action**: 1.

**Target**: An area which must be at least somewhat secluded or out of the way, with defined boundaries no greater than [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters in any direction.

**Effects**: You ward the target area against eavesdroppers and prying eyes for the scene. While the area is warded anyone who is outside the area has Bad Luck on any flips to see, hear, or otherwise sense anything inside the area other than what is immediately apparent - this includes overhearing any discussions, reading lips, seeing any documents or any other objects that someone in the area is trying to conceal from outside eyes, and so on.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Stealth 1

**Placeholder's Instantaneous Instrument (Level 1) [General Spell]**

In a jubilant mood during a night of unparalleled drinking the group of students alleged to be responsible for the fame of the great wizard Placeholder decided they were in the mood for a good song, but they quickly realized that none of them had brought an instrument with them to play music with. Though the collective memory of what followed is hazy at best, in the morning they found a handful of notes they had scrawled that detailed an alteration of the Conjure Weapon spell that instead created a musical instrument. There was, however, no explanation for how one of them managed to lose two fingers on his left hand in the process of creating the spell.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you cast Conjure Armament, in addition to being able to conjure armaments you may also conjure any standard musical instrument with which you are familiar. These instruments otherwise follow all of the rules of Conjure Armament.

The Capacity of your Conjure Armament is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: Conjure Armament, Perform 1

**Placeholder's Meticulous Mending (Level 1) [General Spell]**

~

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A mundane object, no bigger than 2 meters in any dimension, that is adjacent to you.

**Effects**:  You magically repair damage to a mundane object. This can fix holes, tears, and other minor to moderate damage to an object, but cannot re-connect any pieces of the object that have been completely removed. In addition you also clean and freshen up the object, removing anything that has soiled the object as well as any signs of dirtying that come from regular wear.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Placeholder's Tender Telekinesis (Level 1) [General Spell]**

One of the most prominent of Placeholder's legendary one-hundred-and-one incantations, his Tender Telekinesis has become both a favorite of practical magicians and magical instructors alike. Once mastered, through it the caster may leverage small amounts of magical energy to lift small items, pour water, and keep an umbrella over their head without using their hands.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A mundane object within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You conjure a magical force which can manipulate the target object with up to 5 kilograms of force, and if applied to a liquid this force can affect up to 5 liters of liquid in a similar manner. You may maintain the force indefinitely, although you can only have one instance of the Spell active at any given time. Simply maintaining the force, allowing the object to levitate, can be done passively but manipulating the object in a specific manner requires a Main action. Any use of the object more complicated than pushing, pulling, or lifting the object requires intense concentration and an Elementalism or Theurgy flip to successfully perform.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Might 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the Spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**Placeholder's Wizardly Witchlight (Level 1) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

Another classic spell attributed to the arcanist pseudonym Placeholder, the Witchlight was used by many mischievous students for all sorts of immoral endeavors. By use of the Witchlight one can conjure a torch flame in a variety of colors to illuminate an area around them; in addition the nature of the magic allows the flame to be extinguished and lit with ease, which lends itself nicely to shadowy dealings. However, as one of the more benign and utilitarian of Placeholder's spells this one spread quickly once leaked from the group of students who originally developed it and is now considered a staple of the practical arcanist,  much to many a headmaster's chagrin.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You create a flame of any size up to the size of your palm, floating within your space as you wish and casting any amount of light as you choose up to that of a torch. This flame burns in any color of the rainbow you choose, you may alter its size and color at will, and the flame feels gently warm to your touch, although it affects any other people or objects as a normal flame would. You may only have one instance of this Spell active at a time. The flame lasts until you either lose consciousness or choose to dismiss it as a Non-action.

When you cast this Spell you may choose to make the flame ethereal. While in this state only you can see the flame or the light that it casts, and to all others it and its light are invisible. As an Extra action you may return the flame to normal, or cause it to become ethereal if it is normal.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Awareness 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the Spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**Quick Sober (Level 1) [General Spell]**

The spell more formally known as 'Quick Sober' is actually a variety of spells, each with their own methodologies and names, developed independently by soothsayers and hedge mages in almost every civilization for the noble purpose of using magic to help someone who's had way too much to drink.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You remove all intoxicants from the target's system, such as alcohol and other drugs, although this does not affect actual poisons the target is afflicted with. If the target does not wish to be made sober, you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against their Resistance Defense to successfully affect them.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Resistance 1

**Summon Debris Golem (Level 1) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the basics of the summoning arts is the debris golem, conjured by a simple but fundamental practice of binding inert matter together with faint amount of magical power. While many summoners quickly fall out of the practice once they learn more intricate summonings, some magical practitioners make frequent use of debris golems for a variety of purposes, like holding coats and opening doors.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An empty space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Debris Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Debris Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Sympathetic Sight (Level 1) [General Spell]**

~

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You become sympathetically linked to the target for the scene. If the target is unwilling you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy attack against the target's Mental Defense to affect them, and if the attack misses the target is immune to further castings of this Spell by you for the scene.

While linked to the target, as an Extra action you may switch your sight so that instead of seeing out of your own eyes you see from the eyes of your target and are aware of everything visually that the target is aware of, or you may switch your sight back to your own eyes. If the target is unwilling, at the start of their turn you must make an Elementalism or Theurgy attack against the target's Mental Defense, and if you do not hit the spell ends.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Telepathic Sending (Level 1) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

~

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A sentient creature you can see within [twice your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You send a telegraphic message, which can be no longer than a single sentence, to the target and they hear it in their mind. If the target is unwilling you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy attack against the target's Mental Defense to affect them, and if the attack misses the target is immune to further castings of this Spell by you for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Integrity 1

**Wisps (Level 1) [General Spell]**

Flickering, ephemeral lights spring to life around you and illuminate your surroundings in multicolored lights.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Any number of spaces within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You conjure up to [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] dancing, multicolored lights in the target space(s). Each of these lights shed light roughly as a candle, can be in any mix of colors you desire, and follow you as you move. These last indefinitely, although you may only have one instance of this Spell active at a time.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Awareness 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the Spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**Blood Magic (Level 2) [General Spell]**

Mastering the power within your own blood, you can draw it forth to power your spells when your magic begins to wane.

**Cost**: Any amount of non-temporary Health, but no more than [half your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Action**: Non-action.

**Effects**: You may regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique; its level cannot be higher than the amount of Health spent to cast this spell.

**Prereqs**: (Resistance or Centering) 3

**Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana (Level 2) [General Spell]**

"...and the great wizard Placeholder, master of magicks, was said to know a hundred and one incantations and could weave all manner of magicks in circles around lesser spellcasters. For every opponent placed in his way he employed a variety of spellcraft seemingly designed to defeat that opponent in particular, and each of these was executed with the mastery of someone who had spent a lifetime perfecting it. Truly, he was a king ruling over every manner of wizardry."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. VII

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Spell Encounter Techniques, but no more than [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Action**: Non-action.

**Effects**: You regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique; its level cannot be higher than half of the total levels of spells expended to cast this spell.

Prereqs: (Academics or Cunning) 3

**Placeholder's Sneaky Storage (Level 2) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

This is one of the many spells developed by an enterprising group of students at a prestigious wizarding academy. These students, in order to avoid any repercussions for the mischief caused by their spells, attributed all of them to a made-up arcanist by the name of Placeholder, which greatly confused the paperwork of the headmasters of the school in trying to figure out who was responsible. This spell in particular creates a small extra-dimensional space and stores items inside of it, invisible to any searching eyes as well as weighing nothing on the caster.

**Action**: Non-action.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You manifest a small magical space which you can store objects into and retrieve objects from. The space can hold objects totaling a Weight of 2. The space is always considered an easily-accessible location for storing and retrieving objects. When you store objects in the space they disappear from your hand as though placed into an invisible bag, and when you retrieve objects from the space it either appears in your hand(s) or at your feet.

The space lasts indefinitely, although you may end the Spell's effects as a Non-action, and when you lose consciousness for any reason the Spell's effects end. When the Spell's effects end, all contents of the extra-dimensional space are emptied into adjacent empty spaces.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Legerdemain 2

**Raise Corpse (Level 2) [General Spell]**

The cornerstone of popular necromancy and a timeless classic, this spell binds magical power into a dead body to animate it under your command.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A pile of humanoid remains within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon the target pile of remains as a

Reanimated Corpse

.

You may control up to [Will] corpses at any time. If you raise another corpse beyond this limit, your oldest raised corpse will be de-animated. You may withdraw control from, and thereby de-animate, any of your controlled corpses as a Non-action.

If you raise remains that are larger or smaller than a standard humanoid, for the purposes of your limit of corpses controlled at a single time they will count as a number of corpses equal to their volume as measured in standard humanoid corpses (minimum 1). However, larger corpses raised by this spell will still use the Reanimated Corpse statistics for combat, and smaller corpses will usually have worse combat statistics.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Medicine 2

**Sudden Abjuration (Level 2) [General Spell]**

Talented and practiced in the manipulation of energy, in a pinch you are capable of expending a great deal of it to deflect blows aimed at you.

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Spell Encounter Techniques, whose combined levels cannot be more than [half your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Action**: Counter - you are hit by an attack.

**Effects**: You gain temporary Health equal to the total levels of the expended spells, which lasts until after the attack.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3

**Summon Spectre (Level 2) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You summon a low-level shade, either bringing one from the Otherlands or forming one from ambient shadow energy. It aids you in combat, harassing your enemies and attempting to claw the life from them.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Spectre

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Spectre lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 2

**Counterspell (Level 3) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

Practiced mostly by the sort of arcanist who went to a prestigious wizarding school and was top of his class and made sure everyone knew it, sufficient knowledge of the magical arts allows one to recognize the threads of magic another spellcaster is weaving and sunder them by use of overwhelming magical force, dissipating the incantation entirely.

**Action**: Counter - the target begins casting a Spell.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters

which is casting a Spell.

**Effects**: Make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of the target. If the attack hits the target's Spell is Countered and has no effect but is still expended for the encounter, as are any other resources that were spent to cast it such as Soul. If the attack hits and the target's Spell required Soul to cast, you must spend equal Soul to counter it, otherwise the Spell is not Countered.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Insight 2, Academics 2

**Practical Necromancy (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Well-learned in practical applications of your magic, you have learned to do more than simply raise corpses and rip the life from beings. A multi-disciplinary approach gives you ability to greatly modify remains before animating them, allowing for a great deal of creativity in your day-to-day problem solving.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Any items your create from the remains of a living being can be animated with Raise Corpse to allow any 'joints' and the like to move as they would on a normal reanimated corpse, giving crafted devices a form of locomotion. These are treated as Summoned creatures, and if their statistics are ever relevant for combat at best they are treated as creatures per Raise Corpse.

These animated devices count against your maximum number of raised corpses controlled. For this, they are considered to be a number of raised corpses equal to half of the number of average humanoid corpses worth of materials used to craft the device.

**Prereqs**: Raise Corpse, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Craft 2, Medicine 2

**Spellblade (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your practice with both sorcery and martial combat has given you the ability to seamlessly integrate magic with your swordplay, archery, or fighting style of choice, mixing magical power in with your weapon strikes.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Spell Encounter Technique.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make a weapon attack which is modified by the chosen option.

Regardless of the type of spell expended, you may choose one of these options:

If the attack is close-range, it has its range increased by [twice the expended spell's level] meters.

If the attack is ranged, your Strength is increased by [expended spell's level] for the purpose of determining the attack's range.

If the attack hits, the target is Pushed [twice the expended spell's level] meters away from you.

If the attack hits, it deals no damage and instead the target gains [expended spell's level] stacks of Bleeding.

If you expended an Elementalism Spell, you may choose one of these options:

The attack deals up to [twice the expended spell's level] of its damage as your choice of Fire, Frost, or Shadow damage.

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended spell's level] times for 1 round.

If the attack hits, the target is Vulnerable [expended spell's level] times for 1 round.

If the attack hits, the target is Necrotic [expended spell's level] times for 1 round.

If the attack Downs the target, they are instantly killed and you draw a card.

If you expended a Theurgy Spell, you may choose one of these options:

Before or after the attack, you may shift up to [expended spell's level] meters.

You gain a + [expended spell's level] bonus to your choice of Fire, Frost, or Shadow Soak for 1 round.

You gain [expended Spell's level] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended spell's level] times for 1 round.

If the attack hits, the target is Weakened [expended spell's level] times for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 3

**Swordsoul (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your understanding of both martial combat and magical theory allows you to use your martial forms to work magic, guiding energy with the same motions that you use to weave attacks through an opponent's defenses. Through this practice you can launch bolts of magical force that harry and drive back your foes as though you were fencing with a blade or swinging with a warhammer.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Martial Encounter Technique.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The attack deals

1 + [expended Technique's level] + [Will]

Physical damage, and is modified by the chosen option:

The range for the attack is increased by [twice the expended Technique's level] meters.

You may adjust your position in turn order by up to [expended Technique's level] positions.

You gain [expended Technique's level] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

Before or after the attack, you may Shift up to [expended Technique's level] meters.

If the attack is Parried, the target gains [expended Technique's level] stacks of Bleeding.

If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back [expended Technique's level] meters.

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended Technique's level] times for 1 round

If the attack hits, the target is Harried [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for [expended Technique's level] rounds.

You gain a + [expended Technique's level] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round

Prereqs: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, 3

**Telepathic Link (Level 3) [General Spell]**

You trace a magical link between you and an ally, allowing telepathic communication while the link lasts.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself and/or any number of allies you touch, up to [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] total targets.

**Effects**: All targets are linked telepathically and may communicate telepathically at a much faster rate than normal conversation, allowing entire ideas and sentences to be communicated almost instantly. Any linked allies who are farther than

2 × [Will]

kilometers from you cannot hear or "speak" through the link until they return within range. The link lasts until you dismiss it, which you may do as a Non-action, and any linked allies may remove themselves from the link as a Non-action. You may only have one instance of this Spell active at a time.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Insight 3

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the Spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**Infuse Summon (Level 4) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

By channeling raw energy into a creature under your command you can give them a detailed order and have them fight in perfect concert

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: A creature you have Summoned within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You may give the target a turn in combat.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Charisma 3

**Summon Earthen Golem (Level 4) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause the earth itself to form a golem and fight by your side.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Earthen Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it as well as the Soul spent to cast it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Earthen Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell and the Soul committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Survival 4

## General
*Source: `general.html` (Part 2)*

General

**Conjure Armament (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

You conjure a weapon. Or maybe a suit of armor. You know, whatever you're feeling at the time.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Yourself, an ally, or a space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Special**: This Spell has a Capacity equal to the number of times you know it.

**Effects**: You conjure up to [Capacity] weapons, pieces of armor, or pieces of ammunition of a type of your choice. A conjured armament functions as a regular weapon, armor, or piece of ammunition and lasts until you choose to dismiss it, which you may do at any time. You may have no more than [Capacity] such armaments conjured at a time, and if conjuring a new armament exceeds this limit then the oldest conjured armament(s) will be dismissed until you no longer exceed the limit.

If you target yourself or a willing ally, the armament appears in the target's hand(s) or on the target's body, depending on what is conjured. If you target an unwilling ally or a space, the armament appears on the ground target's space.

You may cast this Spell as part of the same action you use to make a weapon attack, costing no AP, as long as you only conjure armaments for that attack.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Freeze/Scorch (Level 1) [General Spell]**

One of the fundamental lessons of magical study is how many energies exist in dual forms in the world. The most obvious of these is heat and cold, and once an apprentice spellcaster has understood this they are capable of moving that energy into or from an object to affect its temperature.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A mundane object, no larger than a campfire, within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Choose whether you want to heat or chill the object. You may heat the object enough that water will boil and combustible items such as tinder or cloth will catch on fire. You may chill the object enough that water will freeze and other objects will be affected as though kept in an ice chest.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Resilience 1

**Placeholder's Accurate Artistry (Level 1) [General Spell]**

This spell animates a pen, brush, chalk, or other tool of artistry and causes it to rapidly dance across a piece of paper or other surface, drawing a full panorama of what is in front of you in the blink of an eye.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Cost**: Drawing material (such as ink, a pencil, or chalk) and a medium on which to draw (such as a piece of paper, parchment, or wood).

**Target**: A visual scene in front of you, or a document no more than one page which you can clearly visually examine.

**Effects**: Depending on your target, you either sketch a scene or copy a document. When sketching a scene the Spell draws everything in your line of sight in one direction, including details you might not notice at the time of casting. When copying a document the Spell makes a copy that perfectly mirrors the contents and reproduces the overall document as accurately as possible with the materials you use.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Placeholder's Covert Conclave (Level 1) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

Stitching a tapestry of shadows, or beckoning minor spirits of secrecy and midnight, you create a minor magical barrier around a meeting place that makes it difficult for onlookers to listen in or watch your discussions.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Target**: An area which must be at least somewhat secluded or out of the way, no larger than [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters in any direction.

**Effects**: You ward the target area against eavesdroppers and prying eyes for the scene. While the area is warded anyone who is outside the area has Bad Luck on any flips to see, hear, or otherwise sense anything inside the area other than what is immediately apparent - this includes overhearing any discussions, reading lips, seeing any documents or any other objects that someone in the area is trying to conceal from outside eyes, and so on. Onlookers will notice the effect, although they might not know why it is harder to eavesdrop, and the Spell does nothing to conceal basic facts about the meeting, such as who is in attendance.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Stealth 1

**Placeholder's Instantaneous Instrument (Level 1) [General Spell]**

In a jubilant mood during a night of unparalleled drinking the group of students alleged to be responsible for the fame of the great wizard Placeholder decided they were in the mood for a good song, but they quickly realized that none of them had brought an instrument with them to play music with. Though the collective memory of what followed is hazy at best, in the morning they found a handful of notes they had scrawled that detailed an alteration of the Conjure Weapon spell that instead created a musical instrument. There was, however, no explanation for how one of them managed to lose two fingers on his left hand in the process of creating the spell.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you cast Conjure Armament, in addition to being able to conjure armaments you may also conjure any standard musical instrument with which you are familiar. These instruments are otherwise treated as conjured armaments for the purposes of the Spell.

**Prereqs**: Conjure Armament, Performance 1

**Placeholder's Tender Telekinesis (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

One of the most prominent of Placeholder's legendary one-hundred-and-one incantations, his Tender Telekinesis has become both a favorite of practical magicians and magical instructors alike. Once mastered, through it the caster may leverage small amounts of magical energy to lift small items, pour water, and keep an umbrella over their head without using their hands.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: One mundane object with a Weight no higher than 1 or up to 1 liter of liquid, which must be within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters. You may choose one target for each time you know this Spell.

**Effects**: You conjure a magical force to move the target object(s), causing them to levitate and allowing you to manipulate them and move them no more than [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters. Any use of the object more complicated than pushing, pulling, or lifting the object costs an extra 1 AP and requires an Elementalism or Theurgy flip in addition to any other relevant flip(s). This Spell may not be used to make an attack of any kind. If you stop casting the Spell, or spend a turn without casting this Spell, any target objects fall to the ground.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Might 1

**Placeholder's Wizardly Witchlight (Level 1) [General Spell] [Re-Learning] [Subtle]**

Another classic spell attributed to the arcanist pseudonym Placeholder, the Witchlight was used by many mischievous students for all sorts of immoral endeavors. By use of the Witchlight one can conjure a torch flame in a variety of colors to illuminate an area around them; in addition the nature of the magic allows the flame to be extinguished and lit with ease, which lends itself nicely to shadowy dealings. However, as one of the more benign and utilitarian of Placeholder's spells this one spread quickly once leaked from the group of students who originally developed it and is now considered a staple of the practical arcanist,  much to many a headmaster's chagrin.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You create a flame of any size up to the size of your palm, floating within your space as you wish and casting any amount of light as you choose up to that of a torch. This flame burns in any color of the rainbow you choose, you may alter its size and color at will, and the flame feels gently warm to your touch, although it affects any other people or objects as a normal flame would. The flame lasts until you either lose consciousness or choose to dismiss it, which you may do at any time, and you may have one flame active at a time for each time you know this Spell.

When you cast this Spell you may choose to make the flame ethereal. While in this state only you can see the flame or the light that it casts, and to all others it and its light are invisible. You may spend 1 AP to return the flame to normal, or cause it to become ethereal if it is normal.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Awareness 1

**Quick Sober (Level 1) [General Spell]**

The spell more formally known as 'Quick Sober' is actually a variety of spells, each with their own methodologies and names, developed independently by soothsayers and hedge mages in almost every civilization for the noble purpose of using magic to help someone who's had way too much to drink.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You remove all intoxicants from the target's system, such as alcohol and other drugs, although this does not affect actual poisons the target is afflicted with. If the target does not wish to be made sober, you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against their Bodily Defense to successfully affect them.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Mixology 1

**Summon Debris Golem (Level 1) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

One of the basics of the summoning arts is the debris golem, conjured by a simple but fundamental practice of binding inert matter together with faint amount of magical power. While many summoners quickly fall out of the practice once they learn more intricate summonings, some magical practitioners make frequent use of debris golems for a variety of purposes, like holding coats and opening doors.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An empty space within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Debris Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Debris Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Craft 1

**Sympathetic Sight (Level 1) [General Spell]**

You create a magical link between yourself and another creature, letting you see what an ally sees or trying to hijack an enemy's eyes.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You become sympathetically linked to the target for the scene. If the target is unwilling you must make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the target's Mental Defense to affect them. While linked to an unwilling target, at the start of each of their turns you must make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the target's Mental Defense, and if you miss the spell ends. If you miss with a spell attack in either case, the target is immune to further castings of this Spell by you for the scene.

While linked to the target, you may spend 1 AP to switch your sight so that instead of seeing out of your own eyes you see from the eyes of your target and are aware of everything visually that the target is aware of, or you may switch your sight back to your own eyes. If you attempt to perform any actions while seeing through another's eyes, you have Bad Luck on those actions.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Rapport 1

**Telepathic Sending (Level 1) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

Touching another's mind with traces of magic, you send them a small mental message.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A sentient creature you can see within [twice your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You send a telegraphic message, which can be no longer than a single sentence, to the target and they hear it in their mind. If the target is unwilling you must hit with an Elementalism or Theurgy attack against the target's Mental Defense to affect them, and if the attack misses the target is immune to further castings of this Spell by you for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2, Rapport 1

**Blood Magic (Level 2) [General Spell]**

Mastering the power within your own blood, you can draw it forth to power your spells when your magic begins to wane.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Any amount of non-temporary Health, but no more than [half your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You may regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique with level equal to the Health paid to cast this Spell.

**Prereqs**: (Resilience or Meditation) 3

**Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana (Level 2) [General Spell]**

"...and the great wizard Placeholder, master of magicks, was said to know a hundred and one incantations and could weave all manner of magicks in circles around lesser spellcasters. For every opponent placed in his way he employed a variety of spellcraft seemingly designed to defeat that opponent in particular, and each of these was executed with the mastery of someone who had spent a lifetime perfecting it. Truly, he was a king ruling over every manner of wizardry."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. III

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Spell Encounter Techniques.

**Effects**: You regain the use of one Spell Encounter Technique. The Spell's level cannot be higher than either half of the total levels of Spells expended to cast this Spell, nor [half your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] .

Prereqs: Academics 3

**Placeholder's Sneaky Storage (Level 2) [General Spell] [Subtle]**

This is one of the many spells developed by an enterprising group of students at a prestigious wizarding academy. These students, in order to avoid any repercussions for the mischief caused by their spells, attributed all of them to a made-up arcanist by the name of Placeholder, which greatly confused the paperwork of the headmasters of the school in trying to figure out who was responsible. This spell in particular creates a small extra-dimensional space and stores items inside of it, invisible to any searching eyes as well as weighing nothing on the caster.

**Action Points**: 0 AP.

**Effects**: You manifest a small magical space which you can store objects into and retrieve objects from. The space can hold objects totaling a Weight of 2. The space is always considered an easily-accessible location for storing and retrieving objects. When you store objects in the space they disappear from your hand as though placed into an invisible bag, and when you retrieve objects from the space it either appears in your hand(s) or at your feet.

The space lasts indefinitely, although you may end the Spell's effects at any time, and when you lose consciousness for any reason the Spell's effects end. When the Spell's effects end, all contents of the extra-dimensional space are emptied into adjacent empty spaces.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Stealth 2

**Raise Corpse (Level 2) [General Spell]**

The cornerstone of popular necromancy and a timeless classic, this spell binds magical power into a dead body to animate it under your command.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A pile of humanoid remains within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon the target pile of remains as a

Reanimated Corpse

.

You may control up to [Mind] corpses at any time. If you raise another corpse beyond this limit, your oldest raised corpse will be de-animated. You may withdraw control from, and thereby de-animate, any of your controlled corpses on your turn without spending any AP.

If you raise remains that are larger or smaller than a standard humanoid, for the purposes of your limit of corpses controlled at a single time they will count as a number of corpses equal to their volume as measured in standard humanoid corpses (minimum 1). However, larger corpses raised by this spell will still use the Reanimated Corpse statistics for combat, and smaller corpses will usually have worse combat statistics.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Medicine 2

**Summon Spectre (Level 2) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You summon a low-level shade, either bringing one from the Otherlands or forming one from ambient shadow energy. It aids you in combat, harassing your enemies and attempting to claw the life from them.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Spectre

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Spectre lasts as long as you keep this Spell committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 2

**Practical Necromancy (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Well-learned in practical applications of your magic, you have learned to do more than simply raise corpses and rip the life from beings. A multi-disciplinary approach gives you ability to greatly modify remains before animating them, allowing for a great deal of creativity in your day-to-day problem solving.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Any items your create from the remains of a living being can be animated with Raise Corpse to allow any 'joints' and the like to move as they would on a normal reanimated corpse, giving crafted devices a form of locomotion. These are treated as Summoned creatures although their combat statistics, if ever relevant, will not be better than those of a Reanimated Corpse.

These animated devices count against your maximum number of raised corpses controlled for Raise Corpse. For this limit, determine how many humanoid corpses worth of remains were needed to make the device, and the device counts as

half

that many raised corpses.

**Prereqs**: Raise Corpse, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Craft 2, Medicine 2

**Spellblade (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your practice with both sorcery and martial combat has given you the ability to seamlessly integrate magic with your swordplay, archery, or fighting style of choice, mixing magical power in with your weapon strikes.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Spell Encounter Technique.

**Target**: A creature in range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make a weapon attack which is modified by the chosen option.

Regardless of the type of spell expended, you may choose one of these options:

If the attack is close-range, it has its range increased by [twice the expended spell's level] meters.

If the attack hits, the target is Pushed [twice the expended spell's level] meters away from you.

If the attack hits, it deals no damage and instead the target gains [expended spell's level] stacks of Bleeding.

You gain a + [half the expended spell's level] bonus to your choice of Fire, Frost, or Shadow Resist for 1 round.

If you expended an Elementalism Spell, you may choose one of these options:

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Vulnerable [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Necrotic [expended spell's level] times for the scene.

If the attack Downs the target, they are instantly killed and you draw a card.

If you expended a Theurgy Spell, you may choose one of these options:

Before or after the attack, you may shift up to [expended spell's level] meters.

You gain [expended Spell's level] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended spell's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Weakened [expended spell's level] times.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 3

**Swordsoul (Level 3) [General Spell]**

Your understanding of both martial combat and magical theory allows you to use your martial forms to work magic, guiding energy with the same motions that you use to weave attacks through an opponent's defenses. Through this practice you can launch bolts of magical force that harry and drive back your foes as though you were fencing with a blade or swinging with a warhammer.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: Expend the use of a Martial Encounter Technique.

**Target**: A creature within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Choose one of the options below, then make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge or Parry Defense. The attack deals

[expended Technique's level] + [Mind]

Physical damage, and is modified by the chosen option:

You may adjust your position in turn order by up to [expended Technique's level] positions.

Before or after the attack, you may Shift up to [expended Technique's level] meters.

You gain [expended Technique's level] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

You gain a + [expended Technique's level] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

If the attack is Parried, the target gains [expended Technique's level] stacks of Bleeding.

If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back [expended Technique's level] meters.

If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Harried [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [expended Technique's level] times.

If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for [expended Technique's level] rounds.

Prereqs: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Academics 3

**Telepathic Link (Level 3) [General Spell] [Re-Learning]**

You trace a magical link between you and an ally, allowing telepathic communication while the link lasts.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself and/or any number of allies you touch, up to a total of [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] creatures for each time you know this Spell.

**Effects**: All targets are linked telepathically and may communicate telepathically at a much faster rate than normal conversation, allowing entire ideas and sentences to be communicated almost instantly. Any linked allies who are farther than [Mind] kilometers from you cannot hear or "speak" through the link until they return within range. The link lasts until you dismiss it, which you may do at any time, and any linked allies may remove themselves from the link at any time. You may only have one instance of this Spell active at a time.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Rapport 3

**Infuse Summon (Level 4) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

By channeling raw energy into a creature under your command you can give them a detailed order and have them fight in perfect concert alongside you.

**Action Points**: 0 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: A creature you have Summoned within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You may give the target a turn in combat.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5

**Summon Earthen Golem (Level 4) [General Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause the earth itself to form a golem and fight by your side.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: An empty space

within [Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon an

Earthen Golem

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene. When you cast this Spell you may choose to commit it as well as the Focus spent to cast it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Earthen Golem lasts as long as you keep this Spell and Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Nature Lore 4

## Healing Magic
*Source: `healing-magic.html` (Part 2)*

**Gentle Mending (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

The art of theurgical healing begins with the gentle manipulation of simple powers; forcing too much energy into a living creature can have unfortunate consequences, but even as a novice one can do work that saves lives.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details).

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose one

Healing Magic Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**Healing Magic (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

The staple of theurgy magicks, and an invaluable incantation for any magic user who sees frequent combat, this fundamental form of magic weaves power to protect and heal the recipient. Depending on the precise way in which the power is invoked it can mend wounds, drive impurities from the body, and restore balance to allies who have had their guard knocked aside.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details).

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

Healing Magic Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell.

**Restoration (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

While many hedge mages and neophyte magic users can stitch wounds with magical energy, an increased proficiency in theurgy allows more subtle ways of manipulating power to save lives. Advanced practitioners learn to work in tune with the bodily energy of their patients, channeling greater power in times of greater need and even saving them from an otherwise fatal blow.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details).

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose three features from the lists of

Restoration Features

and

Healing Magic Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell

**Revivify (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of the art of theurgy can weave their power both broadly and with great intensity, with displays of magical prowess that match the efforts of multiple less-skilled mages.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2

(this affects some of its features, see features for details).

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose one feature from the list of

Revivify Features

and two features from the lists of

Restoration Features

and

Healing Magic Features

to modify the Spell, and the resulting Spell is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Spell

Healing Features

**Blessing**: The spell's Power is increased by 1. This feature may be chosen multiple times.

**Miracle**: You may cast the spell as an Extra action instead of a Main action.

**Reach**: The spell is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Purify**: Remove up to 2 Bleeds from the target. If the target is suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed. If the target is infected with any diseases they may make a Resistance flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty, ending the disease's effects on a success.

**Freedom**: Remove all stacks of Snared or Slowed from the target, and if they are Prone they may stand up immediately as a Non-action.

**Sharpen Instincts**: Remove all stacks of Crippled, Enervated, and Weakened from the target.

**Balance**: The target Regains Balance.

**Vitalize**: Instead of the spell's usual effect, the target gains

2 + [Power]

temporary Health which lasts for the scene.

**Rejuvenate**: Instead of the spell's usual effect, the target heals 1 Health immediately and then heals an extra 1 Health at the start of each of their turns for

1 + [Power]

rounds.

Restoration Features

**Time of Need**: You may cast the spell as a Counter - the target is hit by an attack.

**Boon of Fate**: The target draws a card.

**Triage**: The spell's Power is increased by [target's current stacks of Wounded] .

**Resurgent Spirit**: For the rest of the scene, if the target spends any Soul they heal 2 Health for each Soul spent.

**Guardian Angel**: The next time this scene that an attack or other effect would cause the target to become Downed, before they lose any Health from the attack or effect, they gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts until the attack or effect is resolved.

Revivify Features

**Simplicity**: Choose two features from the lists of

Restoration Features

and

Healing Magic Features

.

**Circle of Mending**: The spell may target an additional creature; this must be a different target from the first.

**Overflowing Vitality**: The target gains 3 temporary Health which lasts for the scene.

**Residual Healing**: The target heals 1 Health at the start of each of their turns for the next 3 rounds.
