## Healing Magic
*Source: `healing-magic_1.html` (Part 1)*

Some of the most iconic of Theurgy rituals, Healing Magic allows you to mend broken bones and infuse a battered companion with new life.

You may apply multiple copies of a feature, unless stated otherwise

Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique

**Renewal (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

The staple of theurgy magicks, and an invaluable incantation for any magic user who sees frequent combat, this fundamental form of magic weaves power to protect and heal the recipient. Depending on the precise way in which the power is invoked it can mend wounds, drive impurities from the body, and restore balance to allies who have had their guard knocked aside.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals 1 Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose four basic healing magic features to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Restoration (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

While many hedge mages and neophyte magic users can stitch wounds with magical energy, an increased proficiency in theurgy allows more subtle ways of manipulating power to save lives. Advanced practitioners learn to work in tune with the bodily energy of their patients, channeling greater power in times of greater need and even saving them from an otherwise fatal blow.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals 1 Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose six basic healing magic features to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4

**Revivification (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of the art of theurgy can weave their power both broadly and with great intensity, with displays of magical prowess that match the efforts of multiple less-skilled mages.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals 1 Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose one expert healing magic feature and four basic healing magic features to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5

Basic Healing Magic Features

**Miracle**: The Spell takes 0 AP to cast.

**Reach**: The spell is instead ranged, and may target a creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Healing**: The Spell's healing is increased by 1. This takes the place of two features.

**Rejuvenate**: Instead of the Spell's normal effect, the target heals 1 Health at the start of each of their turns for

2 + [Spell's normal healing]

rounds. You may only apply one copy of this feature. This takes the place of two features.

**Triage**: The target heals an extra [their current Wounded penalty] Health. You may only apply one copy of this feature. This takes the place of two features.

**Apostle**: The Spell loses the [Encounter] tag.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2

**Purify**: Remove up to 2 stacks of Bleeding from the target. Reduce the effects of each poison afflicting the target as though an hour had passed.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 2

Expert Healing Magic Features

**Simplicity**: When you choose this feature, choose four basic healing magic features to modify the spell.

**Healing Pact**: The spell instead applies to two targets.

**Resurgent Spirit**: For the rest of the scene, when the target spends any Focus they heal 3 Health for each Focus spent.

**Guardian Angel**: The next time this scene the target would be Downed, instead they are reduced to 1 Health in their 'Maimed' Health level.

**Boon of Fate**: The target draws a card.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3

---

## Healing Magic
*Source: `healing-magic_2.html` (Part 1)*

Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once. Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique. Each time you learn one of these Techniques you learn a separate copy, based on the features chosen.

Healing Magic Techniques

**Mending Rite (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

The art of theurgical healing begins with the gentle manipulation of simple powers; forcing too much energy into a living creature can have unfortunate consequences, but even as a novice one can do work that saves lives.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose one

basic healing magic features

to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Renewal (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

The staple of theurgy magicks, and an invaluable incantation for any magic user who sees frequent combat, this fundamental form of magic weaves power to protect and heal the recipient. Depending on the precise way in which the power is invoked it can mend wounds, drive impurities from the body, and restore balance to allies who have had their guard knocked aside.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose two

basic healing magic features

to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Restoration (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

While many hedge mages and neophyte magic users can stitch wounds with magical energy, an increased proficiency in theurgy allows more subtle ways of manipulating power to save lives. Advanced practitioners learn to work in tune with the bodily energy of their patients, channeling greater power in times of greater need and even saving them from an otherwise fatal blow.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose three features from

advanced healing magic features

and/or

basic healing magic features

to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4

**Revivification (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Masters of the art of theurgy can weave their power both broadly and with great intensity, with displays of magical prowess that match the efforts of multiple less-skilled mages.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Power**: This spell has a Power of 2.

**Effects**: Your target heals [Power] Health.

When you learn this Technique you choose one

expert healing magic feature

and two features from

advanced healing magic features

and/or

basic healing magic features

to modify the spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5

Healing Magic Features

Basic Features

**Blessing**: The spell's Power is increased by 1. This feature may be chosen multiple times.

**Miracle**: : You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell.

**Reach**: The spell is instead ranged, and may target creatures within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Purify**: Remove up to 2 stacks of Bleeding from the target. If the target is suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed. If the target is infected with any diseases they may make a Resilience flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty, ending the disease's effects on a success.

**Sharpen Instincts**: Remove all stacks of Crippled and Weakened from the target.

**Vitalize**: Instead of the spell's usual effect, the target gains

2 + [Power]

temporary Health which lasts for the scene.

1 + [Power]

rounds.

basic healing magic features

to apply to the spell.

## Health & Toughness
*Source: `health-toughness.html` (Part 1)*

Health & Toughness

**Body of the Donkey (Level 2)**

You have the toughness of a pack animal, capable of enduring beyond what most people could. Though you may be battered and bruised, it takes a bit more punishment to bring you down.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Maimed (3)' Health level by two and your 'Bloodied (2)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 2, Stamina 2

**Learning**:

You may learn this technique multiple times.

**Power Through (Level 2)**

Even when good sense and your body's physical limits would keep you down, you find the strength to push on (regardless of whether or not you should).

**Cost**: Lose 1 non-temporary Health.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You ignore any stacks of Crippled, Slowed, Weakened, or Wounded you have for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 3, Might 2

**Pranic Nourishment (Level 2)**

You can tune your body into the metaphysical weave of your surroundings, sustaining yourself off of the energy of the world around you in addition to that which exists within all matter.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You only need to meditate for half an hour in order to get a full night's rest, and you heal an extra 1 Health when you get a full night's rest. You only need to eat one bite of food and drink one drink of water in order to fully sustain yourself for a day.

In addition, you may eat almost anything as long as you can safely get it in your mouth and it will sustain you as regular food would with no adverse effects, rendering you effectively immune to ingested poisons. This effect does not prevent most forms of intoxication, such as from alcohol or similar substances, but will prevent extreme forms that cause noticeable harm to you, such as alcohol poisoning or very harmful drugs.

**Prereqs**: (Might or Resistance) 2, Centering 2, Stamina 2

**Second Wind (Level 2) [Encounter]**

Far from being frail and sickly, even when you've been battered and bruised you can catch your breath and keep pushing forward.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You heal 3 Health.

**Prereqs**: (Might, Resistance, or Integrity) 3, (Stamina or Will) 2

**Body of the Mule (Level 3)**

You're not quite as durable as an ox, but you're the next best thing.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Bloodied (-2)' Health level by two and your 'Scratched (-1)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 3, Stamina 3

**Learning**: You may learn this technique multiple times.

**Flamebreath (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Your body is sturdy enough, inside and out, to ignite alcohol and direct the ensuing fireblast away from you, scorching others while you remain unharmed.

**Cost**: 1

alcoholic drink

.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: All creatures within a 3 meter long cone.

**Effects**: Make a Resistance attack against the Dodge Defense of each target. The attack deals

[Stamina] + [half the alcoholic drink's Minimum Skill Total to create]

Fire Damage.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 4

**Plaguebearer (Level 3)**

Your physiology has been trained by strange rituals to no longer resist disease, instead allowing them to coexist symbiotically within your system.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you are exposed to a mundane disease you make no Resistance flip and instead are automatically infected with the disease, which lasts indefinitely instead of its normal duration, however you are immune to the effects of any mundane disease you are infected with and are not contagious as one would normally be while infected with the diseases. All mundane cures for these diseases fail, and any magical effect that would cure a disease you are infected with instead causes you to lose 2 Health.

**Special**: As a Main action you may lose 1 Health and draw enough of your blood to coat a blade or other weapon. You may also perform this as an Extra action by drawing the blood with a weapon or piece of ammunition, coating the weapon instead of collecting into a container. In this way your blood functions as a poison except the victim instead is exposed to one disease of your choice that you are infected with, and must make a Resistance flip or be affected by the disease. After 24 hours the blood loses its potency, although if the disease has managed to spread from there it may exist as normal.

**Prereqs**: Stamina 3, Resistance 3

**Strength from the Slain (Level 3)**

Many barbarian tribes practice the ritual of eating the hearts of enemy warriors slain in combat. This is thought to grant one the courage and strength of their fallen enemies, and while many people deride the practice and uncivilized and inhuman you know there is value to it, whether to further a fierce reputation or out of simple pragmatism.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Target**: A humanoid who you or an ally Downed this scene.

**Effects**: You ritually eat the heart of the target, killing them if they were not already dead and gaining the following benefits:

You gain 1 temporary Health which lasts until you get a full night's rest.

You are sustained as though you had eaten a full meal.

You re-flip any cards 4 or lower for Strength flips for 1 hour.

You may draw a card. If you do, discard a card.

You may only use this Technique once per scene.

**Prereqs**: Might 2, Resistance 2, Stamina 3

**Body of the Ox (Level 4)**

Your resilience is legendary, rivaling that of a dozen beasts of burden.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Scratched (-1)' Health level by two and your 'Winded (-0)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 4, Stamina 4

**Special**:

You may learn this technique multiple times.

**Hardened Resolve (Level 4)**

Through a mix of physical fitness and pure grit you can take more punishment than the average person before collapsing.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**:

Increase the maximum Health of your

'Maimed (-3)' and

'Bloodied (-2)'

Health levels each by two and your

'Scratched (-1)' Health level

by one.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Resistance 3, Will 3

**Learning**:

You may learn this technique multiple times.

**Mind and Body Unbroken (Level 4)**

Through sheer force of will you can force your body to carry on despite what would ordinarily be crippling wounds.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Non-action - you would become Downed.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You are not Downed and instead your total Health is set to 3 in your 'Maimed (-3)' Health level.

**Prereqs**: Will 4

**Body of the Immortal (Level 5)**

If an assassin tried to kill you, they would find that one cowardly stab wouldn't do the trick. If you were brought before an executioner, they would need something heavier than an axe. Defying fate, you simply refuse to die.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Winded (-0)' Health level by three.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 5, Stamina 5, Might 5

**Special**:

You may learn this technique multiple times.

---

## Instinct
*Source: `instinct.html` (Part 2)*

Instinct

**Intuitive Maneuver (Level 1) [Encounter]**

Acting on pure instinct, you adjust your tactics at the last moment.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you play a card from your hand).

**Effects**: Play the top card of your deck in place of the card you just played.

**Prereqs**: Insight 2

**Secret Cant (Level 1)**

Above and beyond common parlance used by thieves and information brokers, you know a wealth of double-meanings that you can weave into conversation with a similarly practiced friend in order to conduct covert business in broad daylight.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can spend an hour working with any number of other people who know this Technique in order to create a secret cant. When you do, decide what general topic(s) the cant will cover - combat strategy, smuggling and distribution of illicit goods, etc. Once your group has developed such a cant, you may use it to discuss those topic(s) and any outside listeners will perceive your conversation to be something else entirely. Particularly observant listeners might be able to pick up on a second meaning to your discussion, but they will not be able to figure out what you are truly discussing.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 2, Academics 1

**Speak With Animals (Level 1)**

Your insight and ability to relate with nature has granted you the ability to understand and make yourself understood by many of the beasts that roam the wilderness.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can communicate with animals and most of them will be able to understand your words. Similarly, if they choose to communicate back you are able to understand them, although due to their intelligence this tends to be base instincts and simple thoughts rather than complex sentences or anything of the like. Note that beings of animal intelligence are generally too simple-minded to be properly deceived.

**Prereqs**: Nature Lore 2, Insight 1

**Wisdom of the Woods (Level 1)**

"Tryin' to make the trek north to Rivera? That's a tricky one, roads aren't worn in the ground anymore up there, easy to get lost. Watch for broken branches, make sure you keep where you can see other people been passin' through. You're leavin' pretty late in the day too, you'll be at it part of the night at least, so make sure you got plenty of torches. What's that? No, your magic light-makin' won't help here, unless it's an actual fire it won't keep the wolves from gettin' curious enough to take a nibble at you. Oh, and make sure to pack some cinnamon too; that's not for the wolves or anything, you just want to keep your liver in good shape."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: As a student of nature and traveling lore, you have the following benefits:

Once per day when you make a Nature Lore flip to recall knowledge about a subject, you can also recall an interesting fact that is related in some way to the subject - this may not be directly relevant, but will be at least somewhat tangential wisdom.

While traveling you may forage for food, water, or other supplies without slowing your pace.

You automatically succeed when giving Backup on Nature Lore flips.

**Prereqs**: Nature Lore 2

**Air of Mystery (Level 2)**

You have an easily forgettable face, and people usually need to "meet" you more than once to recognize you. Anyone who only gets a glimpse of you won't be able to make out any notable features, and official records of you tend to be rather light on details.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Anyone attempting to gather rumors on you or otherwise investigate your involvement in any matter has Bad Luck on any related flips. Whenever you make a Masquerade flip to appear to be an ordinary or nondescript person, you have Good Luck on any related flips.

**Prereqs**: (Masquerade or Streetwise) 3

**Conscious Instinct (Level 2)**

By training your instinct and reflexes you have attained a reaction speed that ensures you are rarely caught off-guard.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on Reflex flips.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3

**Shape of the Leaf (Level 2) [Daily]**

"Wait! Don't walk through that patch of brush, you'll regret it in the morning. Look at the shape of the leaves, there: three long points, like a cat's claw, tells you it'll itch like you got yourself scratched by one if you brush against them too much. It was almost your unlucky day... but looks like it's good luck for us instead! I can take these leaves and get the hurtin' oils off of them, makes a real nasty tincture if you get it in a wound, and if we're gonna be dealing with some bandits I wouldn't mind the help..."

**Action**: 15 minutes.

**Condition**: You have access to wilderness or somewhere else where herbs and other plants can be found.

**Effects**: Make a Nature Lore flip against difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose a

Basic Poison

and you gather natural materials which function as a fresh dose of the chosen poison. For each Extra Success on the flip you may choose to either increase the poison's Difficulty by 2 or increase its Potency by 1. After you get a full night's rest, the dose loses its potency and becomes useless.

**Prereqs**: Nature Lore 3, Insight 2

**Ventriloquy (Level 2)**

Your practice with impersonating others has given you the ability to speak in a variety of voices and even make it seem as though you are not the one speaking.

**Action**: Varies.

**Effects**: You throw your voice, making it seem as though your speech is coming from an area, creature, or object within [your Masquerade Skill Total] meters. Make a Masquerade flip opposed by Awareness flips of any onlookers, any onlookers against whom you win are convinced that the speech is coming from the target, and any onlookers who win the flip know that the speech is a deception but not where it is coming from.

**Prereqs**: Masquerade 3

**Gut Instinct (Level 3) [Daily]**

You've lived this long by listening to your instincts, and when your gut has you feeling a certain way you don't ignore it.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Effects**: Look at the top [Insight Skill Total] cards of your deck, discard any number of them, then shuffle your deck.

**Prereqs**: Insight 4

**Hunter's Mark (Level 3)**

"You know, greenhorn, I have a saying - you can tell a lot about someone by their boots. Once you get a good look at someone's footprints, how they walk on the ground, the kind of boots they wear... why, you can learn an awful lot. Boots are how someone talks to the earth, how they go everywhere, how they live their life. The way you walk around tells me where I can find you... and if things come down to a fight, it also tells me how you carry your weight, and how I can throw you off your game. If you got the guts to wear your boots around me, for your sake they'd better be your very best boots."

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature, set of tracks, or some sign of a creature's passing within [Nature Lore Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target creature or creature who left the tracks/sign becomes your mark, lasting until you mark another creature or choose to end the mark. While the creature is marked you have Good Luck on any flip to track them, inquire about them, or otherwise gather information about them. If you hit the marked creature with a weapon attack, they are Slowed once.

**Prereqs**: Nature Lore 4

**One of Many Faces (Level 3)**

You have mastered the art of infiltration and imitation. You can move between personas in the space of a breath, and even if someone manages to see through one of your masks

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: As long as you are not being observed, you may assume a disguise with Masquerade by spending 3 AP rather than the several minutes it would normally take. If someone successfully sees through your disguise, as long as it is not removed they will not be able to determine your actual identity, merely that you are an impostor.

**Prereqs**: Masquerade 4

**Preparation (Level 3)**

The old saying goes "an ounce of prevention is worth a pound of cure", and likewise your boundless intellect and strategy frequently saves the day when you just so happen to have prepared the perfect solution to a problem.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

Cost: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You reveal that you have made preparations for a situation you are currently in. These preparations can include stashing supplies ahead of time in the location you happen to be in, sending a letter or otherwise corresponding with someone about something, having conveniently researched a topic beforehand which has just become relevant, or simply having brought a certain item with you which is now particularly useful. In order to make any preparation which would have cost you money, such as "gaining" a new item by revealing you had it all along, you must pay all costs at market value.

While you can reveal a wide variety of very unlikely preparations with this Technique, there are some that are impossible to have made ahead of time. If a certain good was not available to you when you would have made a particular preparation, or you would not have had knowledge of something to research it ahead of time, or you would have had absolutely no reason to send a letter to someone "just in case something happened", then the GM can rule a preparation is invalid, although this does not necessarily rule out similar preparations.

**Prereqs**: (Insight, Nature Lore, or Streetwise) 4

**Rumormonger (Level 3) [Social] [Encounter]**

You know all the ways a rumor can spread, and are adept at starting rumors then fanning them into wildfires.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: The general social crowd present while in a larger social situation such as a formal party, moderately crowded tavern, or town hall.

**Effects**: You attempt to start a rumor in a larger social situation such as a formal party, moderately crowded tavern, or town hall. Make a Streetwise flip to determine how widely spread and believed this rumor is over the course of the following few days or weeks; the difficulty of the flip will vary based on the circumstances surrounding the subject of the rumor and how believable it is.

Once you have successfully spread a rumor, you may take advantage of it during your turn in a social contest. This gives you Good Luck on one social statement, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. The rumor must somehow be relevant to the matter at hand in order to take advantage of it, and you may only do so once per social contest.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 4

**Grease the Wheels (Level 4)**

.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on flips for any actions related to or impacted by bureaucracies or formal organizations of any kind, from governments to guilds.

**Prereqs**: Streetwise 5

**Preternatural Reflexes (Level 5)**

Your instincts are razor sharp, and you react to danger the instant it presents itself. It is impossible to catch you off-guard and as soon as any conflict breaks out you are leaping into action while everyone else is still drawing their swords.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When a combat begins, if either there would not normally be a surprise round or if there would be but you would not be included in it, instead there is a surprise round and you are part of it but you only get 2 AP at the start of combat. You are never Unaware of attacks against your Dodge or Parry Defense.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 5, Insight 5, Instinct 5

---

## Intuition & Inspiration
*Source: `intuition-inspiration.html` (Part 1)*

Intuition & Inspiration

**Intuitive Maneuver (Level 1) [Encounter]**

Acting on pure instinct, you adjust your tactics at the last moment.

**Action**: Non-action - you play a card.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Play the top card of your deck in place of the card you just played.

**Prereqs**: (Insight or Cunning) 2

**Minor Oracle (Level 1)**

By using a primitive form of divination magic you can attempt to tell a person's fortune. Though it usually doesn't give a terribly specific result, there's always some knowledge to be gleaned... assuming you do it right.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Target**: A willing, adjacent person.

**Effects**: Choose the divining tool you are using, whether to use Elementalism or Theurgy for the fortune telling, and what in the target's near future the fortune telling will be about. A flip with the chosen Skill is made for you, in secret, to determine the accuracy and importance of the divination. The result will always be relevant to the subject, but a better result on the secret flip will give a divination that is more accurate to what is upcoming, whereas a poor result may return very generic and vague advice.

The first time each day that you tell a person's fortune, that person may look at the top 2 cards of their deck and discard any of them; if they do, they shuffle their deck.

**Prereqs**: Insight 1, (Survival or Academics) 1, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 1

**Focus and Conviction (Level 2)**

You can commit yourself entirely to a course of action, and when you do so others tremble at the force of your will.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you spend Soul to draw a card, you may instead draw 2 cards and discard a card.

**Prereqs**: Will 2

**Grim Resolve (Level 2)**

When you get backed into a corner, you push yourself to your absolute limits to do what needs to be done.

**Cost**: Lose 3 non-temporary Health.

**Action**: Non-action.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Draw a card.

**Prereqs**: Might 2, Resistance 2, Integrity 2

**Lucky (Level 2)**

Things just seem to go your way. Sometimes, this luck even extends to those that travel with you.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: After you get a full night's rest, draw a card.

**Prereqs**: (Any Stat) 3, (Acrobatics, Savvy, or Academics) 2

**Morning's Determination (Level 2)**

When you wake up each day you always remember what needs doing. Every new day reminds you of your responsibilities and goals, and this gives you the drive to persevere despite hardship.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: After you get a full night's rest, when you would discard any cards in your hand, you instead discard any cards in your hand except for 1 card.

**Prereqs**: Will 3, (Might or Resistance) 2

**Words of Encouragement (Level 2)**

When your allies' spirits begin to wane, you know just what to say to light a fire in their hearts.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: An ally within [Perform Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target ally draws two cards, then discards a card.

**Prereqs**: Perform 3

**Font of Inspiration (Level 3)**

Whenever their backs are against the wall, your friends know they can always look to you to boost their spirits.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever an ally within [Perform Skill Total] meters spends Soul, for 1 round they re-flip any cards 4 or lower.

**Prereqs**: Perform 4

**Gut Instinct (Level 3)**

You've lived this long by listening to your instincts, and when your gut has you feeling a certain way you don't ignore it.

**Action**: Non-action - once per day.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Look at the top [Insight Skill Total] cards of your deck, discard any number of them, then shuffle your deck.

**Prereqs**: Insight 4

**Raise Spirits (Level 3)**

Even though you may not know how to do everything, you know how to inspire your friends to do anything.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When giving Backup to an ally you re-flip any cards 4 or lower, and you may use Perform instead of the Skill used by your ally; this may let you give Backup in situations where it would otherwise not be possible.

**Prereqs**: Perform 4

**Channel Life Force (Level 4)**

You have unlocked a piece of the secret power that lies in the life force of others, and by harvesting their vitality you can grant yourself a measure of power temporarily.

**Cost**: A living, sentient being.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You kill the sentient being, and then if you have no cards in hand you draw a card.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 3, Academics 3, Medicine 3

**Rally to Victory (Level 4)**

In every great war story, when the battle is grim and the army is on the brink of defeat a hero will rise above the masses and inspire them to victory. When your friends are struggling against a great foe you appear as something out of a storybook, the great hero who pushes his companions to greatness.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Up to [Perform Skill Total] allies who can see or hear you.

**Effects**: Each target draws a card. At the end of the scene, each target discards a card.

**Prereqs**: Perform 5

---

## Martial Strikes
*Source: `martial-strikes.html` (Part 2)*

Martial Strikes are a set of special, customizable Techniques that allow you to choose from lists of effects in order to determine what they do. Each Martial Strike will explain which features you may choose to apply to it, with higher level Techniques having access to more fantastic and lethal options.

You may apply multiple copies of a feature, unless stated otherwise

Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique

**Squire's Strike (Level 1) [Encounter] [Martial]**

The foundation of martial training, every military recruit and self-taught swordsman learns how to attack in many strategic ways, knocking opponents off-guard and carving the way for victory.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

When you learn this Technique, choose one feature from the list of basic martial features. This feature applies to the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 2

**Soldier's Strike (Level 2) [Encounter] [Martial]**

The practiced soldier hones their swordplay and trains to throw their opponents off-guard with a variety of tactics and maneuvers.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

When you learn this Technique, choose two basic martial features. These features apply to the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 3

**Fighter's Strike (Level 3) [Encounter] [Martial]**

As a martial practitioner sharpens their fighting style, their movements become sublime and imperceptible to their foes until the moment the blow lands, sealing their fate.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's basic or advanced martial features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

When you learn this Technique, choose three basic and/or advanced martial features. These features apply to the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 4

**Warlord's Strike (Level 4) [Encounter] [Martial]**

"The warrior from the barbarian tribe seemed unimposing, compared to the champion we had sent to duel her. Our champion had the finest metal armor, she had scraps of fur; our champion had a great blade smithed by our finest craftsmen, she had a small pig sticker that looked rough and poorly sharpened. But once the duel began she sprang into action: dipping low around her lumbering foe, finding a gap in his armor to shove through that cruel blade of hers with all her might, then twisting away to shower the ground with his blood. It was a move rehearsed a thousand times, it seemed, and it was the last time our tribe saw those lands."

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, choose one of this Technique's basic or advanced martial features and treat the attack as though it had an extra copy of that feature.

When you learn this Technique, choose one expert martial feature and two basic and/or advanced martial features. These features apply to the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5

Basic Martial Features

**Wind Slash**: For the attack the chosen weapon's range is increased by 3.

**Calculated**: The Technique takes an extra 1 AP to use, and you have Good Luck on the attack.

**Guarding**: After you make the attack, you gain 1 stack of Protected.

**Harrying**: If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Harried once.

**Wing Clip**: If the attack hits, the target is Crippled once.

**Hamstring**: If the attack hits, the target is Slowed once.

**Lunging**: Before or after the attack, you may Shift 1 meter.

Prereqs: Acrobatics 2

**Turn Elements**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. After you make the attack, you gain 1 stack of [chosen Element] Ward.

Prereqs: (Academics, Elementalism, or Theurgy) 2

**Lodestone**: If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed 2 meters back.

Prereqs: Might 2

**Distracting**: If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you once.

Prereqs: Presence 2

**Victorious**: If the attack Downs the target, draw a card. You may only apply one copy of this feature.

Prereqs: Insight 2

**Elemental**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. The attack deals its damage as that type instead, and if the attack would cause the target to lose Health instead they gain that many stacks of Bleeding.

Prereqs: Elementalism 2

**Battering**: If the attack is Parried, or if the target's Resist reduces the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

Prereqs: Might 2

Advanced Martial Features

**Viper's Sting**: The Technique costs 1 fewer AP to use. The Technique cannot cost less than 1 AP to use.

**Whirlwind**: If your chosen weapon is a close-range weapon, the attack may target an additional target.

**Piercing Shot**: If your chosen weapon is a ranged weapon, the attack may target an additional target as long as all targets are all in a line from you.

**Flurry**: You instead make two weapon attacks, each with Bad Luck. You may only apply one copy of this feature.

Expert Martial Features

**Battlemaster**: When you choose this feature, choose two basic and/or advanced features to apply to the Technique.

**Inescapable Claw**: If the attack does not hit, you may make another attack against all targets the first attack missed. This attack has the same features as the first, except for this one, and has the same effects such as Good/Bad Luck and Gambling.

**Unparalleled Precision**: If the attack hits, the target gains 3 stacks of Bleeding.

**Ascended Blade**: You may have the attack deal its damage as Brilliant damage.

Prereqs: (Integrity, Meditation, or Theurgy) 4

---

## Martial Strikes
*Source: `martial-strikes_1.html` (Part 1)*

Martial Strikes are a set of special, customizable Techniques that allow you to choose from lists of effects in order to determine what they do. Each Martial Strike will explain which features you may choose to apply to it, with higher level Techniques having access to more fantastic and lethal options. Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once.

**Squire's Strike (Level 1) [Encounter] [Martial]**

The foundation of martial training, every military recruit and self-taught swordsman learns how to attack in many strategic ways, knocking opponents off-guard and carving the way for victory.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 1

(this determines how some of its features work, see below).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack with the chosen weapon against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the Technique's Power is increased by 1 for the rest of the attack.

When you learn this Technique, choose one feature from the list of

Battle Features

to modify the weapon attack. The resulting attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 2

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Technique

**Soldier's Strike (Level 2) [Encounter] [Martial]**

The practiced soldier hones their swordplay and trains to throw their opponents off-guard with a variety of tactics and maneuvers.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 1

(this determines how some of its features work, see below).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack with the chosen weapon against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the Technique's Power is increased by 1 for the rest of the attack.

When you learn this Technique, choose two features from the list of

Battle Features

to modify the weapon attack. The resulting attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Technique

**Fighter's Strike (Level 3) [Encounter] [Martial]**

As a martial practitioner sharpens their fighting style, their movements become sublime and imperceptible to their foes until the moment the blow lands, sealing their fate.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 1

(this determines how some of its features work, see below).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack with the chosen weapon against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the Technique's Power is increased by 1 for the rest of the attack.

When you learn this Technique, choose three features from the lists of

Fighter Features

and

Battle Features

to modify the weapon attack. The resulting attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 4

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Technique

**Warlord's Strike (Level 4) [Encounter] [Martial]**

"The warrior from the barbarian tribe seemed unimposing, compared to the champion we had sent to duel her. Our champion had the finest metal armor, she had scraps of fur; our champion had a great blade smithed by our finest craftsmen, she had a small pig sticker that looked rough and poorly sharpened. But once the duel began she sprang into action: dipping low around her lumbering foe, finding a gap in his armor to shove through that cruel blade of hers with all her might, then twisting away to shower the ground with his blood. It was a move rehearsed a thousand times, it seemed, and it was the last time our tribe saw those lands."

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your chosen weapon.

**Power**: This Technique has a Power of 1

(this determines how some of its features work, see below).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack with the chosen weapon against your target. If you flip a Diamond on the attack, the Technique's Power is increased by 1 for the rest of the attack.

When you learn this Technique, choose one feature from the list of

Warlord Features

and two features from the lists of

Fighter Features

and

Battle Features

to modify the weapon attack. The resulting attack is the version of this Technique you learn.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 5

**Learning**: If you already know this Technique, learning it again lets you either learn an extra use of the same version or learn a new, separate version of the Technique

Battle Features

**Parting Shot**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action - when a creature within range of a close-range weapon you are wielding moves or is Pushed to a space outside of that range. If you do, the attack must be made with that close-range weapon.

**Prereqs**: (Legerdemain or Insight) 2

**Magehunter**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action - a creature within your weapon's range begins casting a Spell. If you do, the attack must target that creature.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Precision**: The Technique's Power is increased by 1.

**Alacrity**: You may spend an extra 1 Soul to use the Technique. If you do, you may use the Technique as a Non-action at any time.

**Mirror Blades**: You may spend an extra 1 Soul to use the Technique. If you do, you may make two extra weapon attacks with the chosen weapon against your target, and all other features that apply to the first attack apply to these attacks as well.

**Wind Slash**: For the attack the chosen weapon's range is increased by

[twice its Power] × [Strength]

.

**Mobile**: Before or after you make the attack, Shift [Power] meters.

**Tactical**: After you make the attack, you may adjust your position in turn order by [Power] positions.

**Guarding**: After you make the attack, you gain a + [Power] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

**Invigorating**: After you make the attack, you gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Battering**: If the attack is Parried, or if the target's Soak would reduce the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

**Harrying**: If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Harried [Power] times.

**Lodestone**: If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Wing Clip**: If the attack hits, the target is Crippled [Power] times for 1 round.

**Dazing**: If the attack hits, the target is Dazed for [Power] rounds.

**Magebane**: If the attack hits, the target is Enervated [Power] times for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Hamstring**: If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [Power] times for 1 round.

**Distracting**: If the attack hits, the target is Taunted by you for [Power] rounds.

**Prereqs**: Intimidate 2

**Victorious**: If the attack Downs the target, draw a card.

**Elemental**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. The attack deals up to [twice its Power] damage as damage of the chosen type.

Prereqs: Elementalism 2

Fighter Features

**Counterstrike**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action - when you Dodge or Parry an attack. If you do, the attack must target that attacker.

**Prereqs**: (Legerdemain or Insight) 3

**Cleave**: If your chosen weapon is a close-range weapon, the attack may target an additional target.

**Splintershot**: If your chosen weapon is a ranged weapon, the attack may target an additional creature adjacent to the initial target.

**Frightening**: If the attack hits, the target is Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Forceful**: If the attack hits, the target is knocked Prone.

**Unbalancing**: If the attack hits, the target is knocked Off-Balance for 1 round.

**Outmaneuver**: If the attack hits, you may adjust the target's position in turn order to one of your choice.

**Smash and Grab**: If the attack hits, after the attack is resolved, you may try to grapple one of the targets hit as though you had hit with the Martial Arts attack to start a grapple attempt, as long as you would be able to grapple the target normally.

**Hemorrhage**: The attack ignores Armor Soak. If the attack would cause the target to lose Health, instead they gain half that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

Warlord Features

**Viper's Sting**: You may use the Technique as a Move action.

**Battlemaster**: The Technique's Power is increased by 2.

**Inescapable Claw**: If the attack misses one or more targets, you may make a second attack using the same weapon against all targets the first attack missed. All other features that apply to the first attack apply to this attack as well.

**Execute**: If the attack hits, after the attack the target gains [half the Health they are missing] stacks of Bleeding.

**Whirlwind**: If your chosen weapon is a close-range weapon, the attack instead may target up to [half your Might Skill Total] creatures within range of your weapon, and you may not Gamble on it.

**Hail of Missiles**: If your chosen weapon is a ranged weapon, the attack instead may target up to [half your Might Skill Total] creatures within a [chosen weapon's range] -meter-long cone, and you may not Gamble on it.

**Ascended Blade**: You may spend an extra 1 Soul to use the Technique. If you do, the attack has all of its damage converted to Spirit damage.

**Prereqs**: (Might, Centering, or Theurgy) 4

**Ten-Ton Hammer**: You may spend an extra 1 Soul to use the Technique. If you do, the bonus damage from gambling is doubled for the attack.

**Cloak and Dagger**: You may spend an extra 1 Soul to use the Technique. If you do, before making the attack, make a Legerdemain flip opposed by the Awareness of each target. Any targets who lose the opposed flip are Unaware of the attack.

## Martial Strikes
*Source: `martial-strikes_2.html` (Part 1)*

Martial Strikes are a set of special, customizable Techniques that allow you to choose from lists of effects in order to determine what they do. Each Martial Strike will explain which features you may choose to apply to it, with higher level Techniques having access to more fantastic and lethal options.

Unless otherwise stated, a feature cannot be chosen more than once. Features may have their own prerequisites, which then apply to learning the Technique. Each time you learn one of these Techniques you learn a separate copy, based on the features chosen.

Martial Strike Techniques

to modify the weapon attack.

Advanced Martial Features

and

Basic Martial Features

to modify the weapon attack.

**Warlord's Strike (Level 4) [Encounter] [Martial]**

"The warrior from the barbarian tribe seemed unimposing, compared to the champion we had sent to duel her. Our champion had the finest metal armor, she had scraps of fur; our champion had a great blade smithed by our finest craftsmen, she had a small pig sticker that looked rough and poorly sharpened. But once the duel began she sprang into action: dipping low around her lumbering foe, finding a gap in his armor to shove through that cruel blade of hers with all her might, then twisting away to shower the ground with his blood. It was a move rehearsed a thousand times, it seemed, and it was the last time our tribe saw those lands."

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

When you learn this Technique, choose one feature from the list of

Expert Martial Features

and two features from the lists of

Advanced Martial Features

and

Basic Martial Features

to modify the weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5

Martial Features

Basic Features

Modifications

**Precision**: The Technique's Power is increased by 1. You may choose this feature multiple times.

**Wind Slash**: For the attack the chosen weapon's range is increased by

[Power] × [Body]

.

**Elemental**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, or Shadow. You may have the attack deal its damage as damage of the chosen type, and if the attack would cause the target to lose Health instead they gain that many stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Elementalism 2

**Parting Shot**: You may spend 1 AP to use the Technique as an Interrupt (a creature within range of a close-range weapon you are wielding moves or is Pushed to a space outside of that range). If you do, the attack must be made with that close-range weapon.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Insight) 2

**Magehunter**: You may spend 1 AP to use the Technique as an Interrupt (a creature within your weapon's range begins casting a Spell). If you do, the attack must target that creature.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Alacrity**: You may spend an extra 1 Focus when you use the Technique. If you do, the Technique costs 1 AP as an Interrupt (any time).

Bolstering

**Mobile**: Before or after you make the attack, Shift [Power] meters.

**Invigorating**: After you make the attack, you gain [Power] temporary Health which lasts for 1 round.

**Turn Elements**: When you choose this feature, choose Fire, Frost, Brilliant, or Shadow. After you make the attack, you gain + [Power] Resist against the chosen type for 1 round.

**Tactical**: After you make the attack, you may adjust your position in turn order by up to [Power] positions.

**Guarding**: After you make the attack, you gain a + [Power] bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

**Victorious**: If the attack Downs the target, draw a card.

Debuffing

**Battering**: If the attack is Parried, or if the target's Resist reduces the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

Advanced Features

Modifications

**Smash and Grab**: If the attack hits, after the attack is resolved, you may attempt to grapple one of the targets you hit as long as you would be able to grapple them normally.

Debuffing

**Unbalancing**: If the attack hits, the target is Unbalanced for 1 round.

**Outmaneuvering**: If the attack hits, you may adjust the target's position in turn order by up to [Power] positions.

Expert Features

Modifications

**Viper's Sting**: The Technique costs 1 AP to use.

**Battlemaster**: When you choose this feature, choose two Basic Features to apply to the Technique.

**Ascended Blade**: You may have the attack deal its damage as Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: (Might, Meditation, or Theurgy) 4
