**Hail of Missiles**: If your chosen weapon is a ranged weapon, the attack instead may target up to [half your Might Skill Total] creatures within a [chosen weapon's range] -meter-long cone, and you may not gamble on it.

**Inescapable Claw**: If the attack misses one or more targets, you may make a second attack using the same weapon against all targets the first attack missed. All other features that apply to the first attack apply to this attack as well.

**Ten-Ton Hammer**: You may spend an extra 1 Focus to use the Technique. If you do, each time you Gamble on the attack it increases the attack's damage by an extra 1.

---

## Martial Techniques
*Source: `martial-techniques.html` (Part 2)*

Martial Techniques

Martial Techniques are most, but not all, of the techniques related to the use of weapons and are almost entirely centered around combat. Everything from fancy sword-swings to trick shots with a knife to the ability to shoot an apple from someone's head from a mile away would generally be included under this section.

---

## Martial
*Source: `martial.html` (Part 2)*

Martial

Martial Techniques cover a range of uses in traditional combat. These might include fighting with various weapons, defending yourself against such attacks, or any number of tactics that come into play in such situations. If you plan to spend a considerable amount of time in these situations, you might want to learn some Martial Techniques.

---

## Medicine & Healing
*Source: `medicine-healing.html` (Part 2)*

Medicine & Healing

**Bedside Manner (Level 1)**

You are a practiced physician, sawbones, or battle medic, with enough practice to tend to groups of patients while they rest.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Action**: While all targets are getting a full night's rest; during this time you may not get a full night's rest.

**Target**: Up to [half your Medicine Skill Total] creatures.

**Effects**: After all targets have gotten a full night's rest make a Medicine flip, difficulty 11, which succeeds automatically if you flip a Spade. If you succeed each target heals 1 Health. A creature can only benefit from this Technique once per rest.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2

**First Aid (Level 2)**

With the right supplies and a quick moment of focus you have the medical training to patch up some light wounds.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Target**: A willing humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 you may heal the target 1 Health, or on a result of at least 13 you may heal the target 2 Health.

Regardless of result, an individual can only have First Aid used on them once per day.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

**Hand of the Chirurgeon (Level 3)**

With a great amount of practice and knowledge you have learned how to perform a variety of life-saving acts of medicine, keeping all manner of illness at bay.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Action**: 1 minute or Main (see below).

**Target**: Yourself or a willing humanoid patient adjacent to you.

Effects: You can perform acts of medicine to help with a variety of ailments, each of which requires you to expend specially prepared bandages, healing herbs, or some similar medicinal supply. For each of these acts requiring a flip, if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7.

As a Main action, you can attempt to stanch a bleed on the patient. When you do so, make a Medicine flip and on a result of at least 9 you may remove 1 stack of Bleeding from the patient. For every 2 points your result is above 9, you may remove an additional stack of Bleeding from the patient.

You may spend 1 minute carefully tending to the target, then the target loses 1 Health and you make a Medicine flip. For each poison the target is affected by, if your result exceeds that poison's Difficulty then the poison is removed from the patient.

You may tend to a patient while they get a full night's rest. After they rest, if they make a flip against the ongoing difficulty of a disease then you may make a Medicine flip, and the patient may use your result in place of their flip against the disease.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 4, Academics 1

**Lay On Hands (Level 4)**

Those with sufficient courage and will are capable of stoking their own life's flame to burn bright and use that to comfort their allies and burn away any impurities.

**Cost**: 1 charge of this Technique

(see below)

.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent willing creature.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following:

The target heals 1 Health.

Remove up to 2 Bleeds from the target.

If the target is suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed.

If the target is infected with any diseases, they make a Resistance flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty and end the disease's effects on a success.

**Special**: After you get a full night's rest, you may spend any amount of Soul. For each Soul you spend this way you gain [half your Integrity Skill total] charges for this Technique, which last until you get a full night's rest.

**Prereqs**: Resistance 3, Integrity 3, Charisma 3

**Pressure Point Revitalization (Level 4) [Encounter]**

Thanks to your almost encyclopedic knowledge of the human(oid) body, you can tell at a glance where a patient's energy is pooling. By delivering precise pressure to those areas, you can stoke their life force and use it to mend their wounds.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself or a humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: You touch the target, who must have at least 1 Soul remaining, and which requires a successful Martial Arts attack if they are unwilling. The target loses 1 Soul and you make a Medicine flip, and if you flip a Spade you may treat the value of that card as being 7. On a result of at least 9 the target heals 1 Health. For every 2 points your result is above 9, the target may heal an extra 1 Health.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 5, Legerdemain 2, Academics 2

---

## Mind
*Source: `mind.html` (Part 1)*

Mind

**All Juiced Up (Level 1)**

Whether through many long nights of sampling your own brews or through a freakish acclimation to certain reagents, potions and elixirs keep you feeling sharp and awake.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: While you are affected by a Potion's effects, you have Good Luck on Reflex flips.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 2, Resilience 1

**Bedside Manner (Level 1)**

You are a practiced physician, sawbones, or battle medic, with enough practice to tend to groups of patients while they rest.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply per target.

**Target**: Up to [half your Medicine Skill Total] allies.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, which succeeds automatically if you flip a Spade. If you succeed each target heals 1 Health when they next get a full night's rest, so long as they do so directly after you use this Technique. A creature can only benefit from this Technique once per rest.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2

**Dark Powers of Commerce (Level 1)**

By keeping tabs on the activity of local merchants as well as current events, you make sure to get the best prices for all of your wares.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can account for market forces, and as such you may always buy and sell goods and services at market value. In addition, you have Good Luck on any flips to research or investigate any current effects on the local markets and economy.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2, Streetwise 1

**Encrypt Letter (Level 1)**

Knowing enough about written language and mathematic principles, you can cipher your letters so that anyone who intercepts it will be none the wiser as to its contents.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever you write a document (such as a letter or note) you may choose to write it in cipher. Make an Academics flip and note the result, which becomes the difficulty to break the cipher, and determine how the document should appear to those who cannot break the cipher - a mundane letter, total gibberish, etc.

Once someone suspects the document is in code, in order to see the document for what it actually is they must make an Academics flip against the difficulty to break the cipher. If they succeed then they may read the document's actual contents, but if they fail then they may not make another attempt for the same document unless they somehow gain additional knowledge that might help them crack the cipher.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2, Streetwise 1

**Immaculate Saucepan Methodology (Level 1)**

With access to enough spices and resources you can adjust the flavor of just about any meal, salvaging any mistakes and satisfying even the most picky eaters.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: A culinary dish adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Mixology flip, with a difficulty based on how complicated the flavor and intricacy of the dish is, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed you change or mask a flavor the dish has in a basic, straightforward way. For each Extra Success you get you may dramatically change a flavor the dish has, either to something else of your choosing or effectively remove that flavor from the dish.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 2, Academics 1

**Perfect Poise (Level 1)**

You remain perfectly composed in any situation, and you retain perfect composure at all times. You never lose your poker face, and even when ambushed you never do more than raise an eyebrow. Your physical appearance is always meticulous, and even if you are forced to crawl in the mud a quick dusting off of your clothing will return it to pristine condition.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You gain the following benefits:

Any Insight flips against you that are not for the purpose of discerning if you are lying automatically fail.

You ignore all penalties to flips for anything related to grooming, hygiene, or your appearance.

The first time in a social contest when you are the front member and would gain Demerits from a Forceful social statement, you instead gain one fewer Demerits.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 2

**Perfect Visual Analysis (Level 1)**

Your memory is extremely sharp, and with some quick mental shortcuts you can gauge all manner of numerical measures with stunning speed and accuracy.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can always perfectly remember anything you have seen or otherwise sensed or witnessed. Your keen senses also allow you to instantly determine distances, weights of objects, and the number of objects in a collection.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2

**Stately Tailoring (Level 1)**

Well-versed in multiple eras of high-society fashion, you have a keen eye for the ways that different styles of clothing can differ from each other - and how similar they might be.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Target**: A garment adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Craft flip, with a difficulty based on how intricate the garment is, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed you may change the garment in a basic, simple way, or alter one aspect of it. For each Extra Success you get you may completely alter one additional aspect of the garment.

**Prereqs**: Craft 2, Academics 1

**Trivia Master (Level 1) [Daily]**

You have browsed literature on a staggering number of topics, and whenever a conversation breaks out you always have a fun fact with which to enrich the discussion.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Effects**: You recall a fact about the subject matter at hand. Make an Academics flip to determine the value of the fact, with the difficulty depending on the subject matter. While the facts may not be terribly helpful, they will usually be interesting.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2

**Analyze Stance (Level 2)**

"Based on how far down the handle he's holding his sword and the fact his left foot is forward but at a slight angle, he's likely a practitioner of the Shiawasena Tanoshī Jikan (幸せな楽しい時間) style of swordplay. Keep your guard up and look for the downward slash; that's your opening to strike."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever a creature within [Awareness Skill Total] meters enters a Stance or declares the use of a Martial Technique, you automatically recognize it and know exactly what it will do as long as its level is no greater than [half your Academics Skill Total] . If a combatant you can see applies any of their Resists against an attack, you automatically know the value of those Resists.

You may spend 1 AP and make an Insight flip opposed by the Stealth of a combatant within [Awareness Skill Total] meters. If you win you learn the target's Dodge Defense, as well as their Parry Defense and relevant Skill Total with any weapons they are wielding.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3,  Insight 2

**Deploy Mounted Launcher (Level 2) [Encounter]**

The cunning tinker is never caught off-guard, and always carries an assortment of parts that can be assembled into a defensive contraption at a moment's notice.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Gold worth of Craft materials.

**Target**: An empty space adjacent to you.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Mounted Launcher

which appears in the target space and lasts for the scene, after which it is automatically destroyed. When you use this Technique you may choose to commit it, and if you do then after the scene ends the Mounted Launcher lasts as long as you keep this Technique committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 3, Academics 2

**First Aid (Level 2)**

With the right supplies and a quick moment of focus you have the medical training to patch up some light wounds.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: A humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the target heals 1 Health, and if you get at least two Extra Successes the target heals an extra 1 Health.

Regardless of result, an individual can only have First Aid used on them once per day.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

**Grim Resolve (Level 2)**

When you get backed into a corner, you push yourself to your absolute limits to do what needs to be done.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 2 non-temporary Health.

**Effects**: You draw a card.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 3, Might 2

**Morning's Determination (Level 2)**

When you wake up each day you always remember what needs doing. Every new day reminds you of your responsibilities and goals, and this gives you the drive to persevere despite hardship.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: After you get a full night's rest, instead of discarding any cards in your hand you discard all but 1 card.

Prereqs: Integrity 3, (Might or Resilience) 2

**Philosopher's Metallurgy (Level 2)**

Having studied the similarities between all manner of objects and substances, you have begun to grasp some fundamental truths of all matter. By means of subtle manipulations, you can produce surprising products from unlikely resources.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may use any raw materials to create an item, as long as the relevant Skill for those raw materials is at least the item's Minimum Skill Total.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 3, Academics 2

**Ritual Sacrifice (Level 2)**

You have unlocked a piece of the secret power that lies in the life force of others, and by harvesting their vitality you can grant yourself a measure of power temporarily.

**Action**: 5 minutes.

**Cost**: A sentient, living creature, or 1 Gold worth of other living creatures.

**Effects**: The creature(s) used as the cost are killed. You draw a card.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3, (Medicine, Elementalism, Meditation, or Theurgy) 2

**Salvage (Level 2)**

While many craftsmen can work with prepared materials you have a more pragmatic approach and are a deft hand at rendering finished products into serviceable materials for re-use.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You can use existing items as though they were raw materials of the appropriate type for item creation. Items used this way count as having half of their standard value (that they would have if they were new).

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 3

**Scraptrops (Level 2) [Encounter Technique]**

By keeping spare pieces of metal and wood from your various tinkerings, you always have a source of caltrops to scatter at your foes' feet.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Gold worth of Craft materials.

**Target**: Each space within a 2-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: You scatter caltrops in the target spaces. Each space with caltrops in it gains [half your Craft Skill Total] levels of Difficult Terrain; a creature may choose to ignore this Difficult Terrain, but if they do they gain a stack of Bleeding for each space of caltrops across which they move.

You may spend 1 minute gathering the caltrops. If you do, they are removed and you regain this Technique's Cost.

**Prereqs**: Craft 3

**Strength and Conviction (Level 2)**

You can commit yourself entirely to a course of action, and before your force of will others tremble in comparison.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You draw 2 cards, then discard a card.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 3, Resilience 2

**Supportive Treatment (Level 2)**

By securing a comfortable, compressing bandage or applying medicinal salve to certain muscle groups, you keep the patient's body operating at full strength under certain kinds of stress.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: A humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: Choose Crippled, Slowed, or Weakened, then make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the next time the target would gain a stack of the chosen debuff instead they do not, and for each Extra Success this applies to an additional stack of that debuff. This effect lasts until the target gets a full night's rest.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 3

**Hand of the Chirurgeon (Level 3)**

With a great amount of practice and knowledge you have learned how to perform a variety of life-saving acts of medicine, keeping all manner of illness at bay.

**Action**: 2 AP; or varies (see below).

**Cost**: 1 Medicinal Supply.

**Target**: Yourself or a willing humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: You can perform acts of medicine to help with a variety of ailments:

You may spend 2 AP to attempt to stanch a bleed on the patient. Make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed you remove 1 stack of Bleeding from the target, and for each Extra Success you remove another stack of Bleeding.

You may spend 1 minute carefully tending to the target, choose a poison they are affected by, then make a Medicine flip. If your result exceeds that poison's Difficulty then the poison is removed from the patient.

You may tend to a patient while they get a full night's rest. After they rest, if they make a flip against the ongoing difficulty of a disease then you may make a Medicine flip, and the patient may use your result in place of their flip against the disease.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 4

**Inspirited Armaments (Level 3)**

The armaments that you craft hold secrets and potential that you can unlock for your allies.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 or 2 Focus, your choice.

**Target**: A non-Masterwork item that you personally crafted, which you are touching.

**Effects**: The target item becomes a

Masterwork Item

of your choice, up to level 3 if you spent 1 Focus or up to level 5 if you spent 2 Focus. The target item must be of the appropriate type for the chosen Masterwork item and you must meet all prerequisites to otherwise create the chosen Masterwork item, including Minimum Skill Total and owning/knowing the recipe. The item remains a Masterwork Item until you get a full night's rest, although when you use this Technique you may choose to commit the Focus spent and instead the item remains Masterwork as long as you keep the Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4

**Reactant Brewing (Level 3)**

Your diverse study of alchemical ingredients and their interdependent effects has given you the ability to adjust formulae on the fly, turning potions into mimics of others.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: An alchemical good which you are holding.

**Effects**: You permanently turn the alchemical good into another alchemical good of your choice. The resulting good must be one that you know how to make, and it may not have a higher level than the original good.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 4, Academics 3

**Saboteur (Level 3)**

A skilled infiltrator can pick a lock or disable a trap. A truly gifted infiltrator can reach deeper into a given contraption, rigging it to turn an enemy's stronghold into their own domain.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you successfully disable a mechanical device, such as a trap or similar contraption, you may attempt to change how the device will function. Make an Academics flip against the same difficulty as the flip to disable the device, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, you may set the device to work in a way of your choosing, within its original function. For each Extra Success you get, you may change or grant a function of the device, such as adding a timed delay to something that had no such feature.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4, Academics 3

**Scrapcraft (Level 3)**

You are in the habit of keeping a variety of half-completed spare parts in your pack, and in the practice of making common items from various odds and ends. After some time doing both of these, you can pull together anything from a sword to a lantern in a moment's notice.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Effects**: You create an item, which must have Craft as its relevant Skill and for which you meet the requirements to create, as long as the materials required for the item are within your easy reach. The item lasts until the end of the scene, at which time it falls apart into the materials used to make it.

When you use this Technique you may choose to commit 1 Focus to the item you create. If you do then after the scene ends the created item lasts as long as you keep the Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: Craft 4, Acrobatics 2, Insight 2

**Vessel of Belief (Level 3)**

In darkest hour your companions know they can rely on the arms and elixirs you create to see them through their troubles, drawing strength from your belief in them.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: An item you created, such as with Craft or Mixology, which you are holding.

**Effects**: You commit any amount of Focus, which you place within the target item. If the target item is consumed when used, such as a potion, when the item is consumed any Focus you had placed within it is uncommitted and the person who consumed the item gains temporary Focus equal to that which was placed in the item, which lasts for the scene. If the item is a non-consumable item, such as a weapon, while that item is being worn or used the wielder may choose to un-commit any amount of Focus you had placed in it and gain that much temporary Focus, which lasts for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Craft or Mixology) 4, Integrity 3

**Artisan's Eye (Level 4)**

Your discerning eye sees all when it comes to your craft. A moment's glance is enough to tell you exactly how an item was crafted and how it is designed to function.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A created item, or schematic/recipe to create an item, within [Awareness Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You learn the item's statistics, including what materials it is made from, if it differs from the standard version of that weapon, and so on. Depending on the situation it might be impossible to gain all of this information, or rare cases where the item is too foreign to properly analyze, but you will know that there is something you cannot analyze about the item.

In addition, if you meet the item's Minimum Skill Total in its Required Skill then you learn how to create the item and permanently learn it as a recipe for item creation.

**Prereqs**: Academics 5, (Craft or Mixology) 4

**Bottomless Bottles (Level 4)**

Each week your fingers are stained a different color from different alchemical reagents. Each time one of your friends gets hurt, or needs a pick-me-up to help them finish writing an important letter, or wants a bottle of acid to clean something with, you have just the thing they need. As a resourceful and cunning mixologist, you always seem to have a little bit of something you can mix together.

**Action**: 15 minutes.

**Cost**: Any amount of Focus you choose.

**Effects**: You create items with a total Gold cost no greater than the amount of Focus spent, and you do not require any materials to create them. These items must have Mixology as their Relevant Skill, and you must meet all requirements to create the item. These items last until you get a full night's rest, although when you create them you may choose to commit the Focus spent and instead the created items lasts as long as you keep the Focus committed.

**Prereqs**: Mixology 5

**Mind and Body Unbroken (Level 4)**

Through sheer force of will you can force your body to carry on despite what would ordinarily be crippling wounds.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you would become Downed).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You are not Downed, and instead your total Health becomes 3 in your 'Maimed (-3)' Health level.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 5

**Pressure Point Revitalization (Level 4)**

Thanks to your almost encyclopedic knowledge of the human(oid) body, you can tell at a glance where a patient's energy is pooling. By delivering precise pressure to those areas, you can stoke their life force and use it to mend their wounds.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or a humanoid patient adjacent to you.

**Effects**: If the target has any Focus remaining, they lose 1 Focus and you make a Medicine flip against a difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed the target heals 1 Health, and for each Extra Success they heal an extra 1 Health.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 5

**Mastermind (Level 5)**

Your very smart and also brainful.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your maximum Focus is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Academics, Craft, Medicine, or Mixology) 5, Integrity 5, Mind 5

---

## Misc. Techniques
*Source: `misc-techniques.html` (Part 1)*

Misc. Techniques

Unlike martial and spell techniques, which tend to be more focused in what they do, miscellaneous techniques are exactly as the name implies: assorted techniques that use a variety of skills and accomplish a diverse set of goals. Generally speaking, miscellaneous techniques expand the uses of some skills, providing new functionality, or make skills easier to use in a larger variety of situations.

While there are some miscellaneous techniques that have combat applications a good number of them are more universally beneficial, so anyone looking to round out their adventuring repertoire beyond bashing heads in might consider taking a look.

---

## Monastic
*Source: `monastic.html` (Part 2)*

Through practice and meditation, practitioners of the various martial arts are capable of fighting toe-to-toe with warriors clad in plate armor, stopping blades with their bare hands, and strengthening their bodies to an incredible degree.

**Placeholder's Eldritch Enlightenment (Level 1) [Discipline]**

"So it was that was that the arcanist Placeholder dealt a final, decisive strike to the Master of the School of the Fifth Dragon Fist, completing the trial that had been laid before him. The School's council gathered and summarily declared Placeholder worthy of learning the secrets of the Fifth Dragon, notably the final chakra that was the missing piece of one of Placeholder's great theses of power. With it unlocked, and his thesis complete, Placeholder had mastered the pathways which allowed him to freely manipulate energy between his corporeal being and his environment, forging a unified practice of the arcane and meditative arts."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. IV

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may treat Disciplines you know as though they were Spells for any use of Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana.

**Prereqs**: Placeholder's Pragmatic Prana, Meditation 1

**Shugen Style - Spirit Bolt (Level 1) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

An initiate of the spiritual arts learns to direct their energy into tangible bolts directed at their enemies.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the attack hits, it deals [Mind] Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 2

**Snake Style - Striking Constrictor (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

One of the basic maneuvers of the Snake style, known to dozens of other martial arts across the world, trains the user to turn an unarmed attack effortlessly into a limb lock.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you hit with an Unarmed weapon attack, after the attack is resolved).

**Effects**: Make a Brawl flip to attempt to grapple the target (as though you had hit with the initial Brawl attack).

**Prereqs**: Brawl 2, Acrobatics 1

**Bear Style - Boulder Toss (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You grab someone and launch them away from you, often into a wall or one of their friends.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you successfully grapple a creature).

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You end the grapple and throw the target up to [half your Might Skill Total] meters in a straight line.

If the target is thrown into an object, and the object is sturdy enough to stop the target, then the target strikes the object. Otherwise the object is knocked aside, damaged, and otherwise affected as is appropriate, and the target continues onward.

If the target would be thrown through a space occupied by a creature, make a Might attack against that creature's Dodge Defense. If it hits, then the target strikes the creature.

If the target strikes a creature or object, the attack deals

2 + [your Body]

Physical damage to both the target and that creature or object. The target then lands in the space in front of that creature or object.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Might 2

**Demon Style - Hands of Defilement (Level 2) [Stance]**

The hands are key points for manifestation of energy. With the right knowledge and practice, a simple shift in stance twists the flow of your own energy to conflict violently with those you strike bare-handed.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: If a creature is hit by one of your Unarmed weapon attacks, they gain a stack of Vulnerable.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Might 1, Meditation 1

**Demon Style - Plague Fist (Level 2) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

A corruption of traditional hand-to-hand

ki

maneuvers, you burn your own spirit's energy to devastate your victim and desecrate their life force.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits the target gains [half your Meditation Skill Total] stacks of Necrotic, plus an extra stack if you flip a Diamond on the attack. You gain 2 stacks of Necrotic.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Might 1, Meditation 1

**Flurry of Blows (Level 2) [Stance]**

Through much practice and exercise you have learned to move all of your limbs in concert, throwing multiple strikes where an average brawler would try a simple, single punch.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: Whenever you would make an Unarmed weapon attack, instead you make two of those attacks, and you have Bad Luck on those attacks. Any effects that would apply to the target of the initial attack are applied to both attacks, but the action otherwise happens the same.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Acrobatics 2

**Great Old Oak Style - Boughs Unbroken (Level 2) [Stance]**

Ancient oaks, standing for thousands of years, are said to have bark that no mortal can mar with axe or fire. By channeling the ancient wisdom of the oak, you strengthen your limbs like those of a great tree.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: Whenever you make an Unarmed weapon attack, before you flip for the attack, you may remove a stack of Crippled or Slowed from yourself.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Athletics 1, Meditation 1

**Great Old Oak Style - Branches in the Wind (Level 2) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

After the style had been developed and passed down for a handful of generations, one group of disciples took it upon themselves to improve the practice. To contrast against the steady, predictable nature of the style they learned to manifest its defensive power by means of a quick counterattack that provided much-needed variation in strategy. Some historians argue that this maneuver made the style robust enough that its monks survived the great

Shui Zhao

conflict, preserving the school into the current age.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Dodge or Parry Defense, and the attacker is within range of your Unarmed weapon).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits, for [half your Meditation Skill Total] rounds, if you have any stacks of Harried you ignore 1 of them.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Meditation 1, Athletics 1

**Ignore Pain (Level 2) [Discipline]**

Sometimes there are things that need doing, and even if your leg is broken it isn't a good enough reason to stop.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Remove up to [half your Meditation Skill Total] total stacks of Crippled and/or Slowed from yourself. You are Bolstered [half your Meditation Skill Total] times.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3, Resilience 2

**Reinforced Spirit (Level 2) [Discipline] [Re-Learning]**

Dedicated reflection and meditation has allowed you to further reinforce your metaphysical avatar, granting it greater mystical power.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following, which applies to your Spirit Avatar:

The Avatar's Spirit Strike attack has a range of [half your Meditation Skill Total] meters

The Avatar's Spirit Strike attack may be made against the target's Mental Defense

The Avatar's Spirit Strike attack may deal its damage as Shadow

If the Avatar's Spirit Strike attack hits a target, they gain a stack of Vulnerable

The Avatar has a Brawl Skill Total of [your Meditation Skill Total] for the purpose of grappling; each turn your Avatar must spend 2 AP to maintain a grapple

**The Avatar's Spirit Bond distance instead becomes [three times your Mind] Prereqs**: Summon Spirit Avatar

**Shugen Style - Iron Skin (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

As an attack weaves its way past your guard, you concentrate your energy into your skin to deflect the force of the blow.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you are hit by an attack, before the attack is resolved).

**Effects**: If you would lose any Health from the attack, that amount is reduced by [half your Meditation Skill Total] .

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Shugen Style - Shatter Mind (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

A calm and focused mind is a deadly weapon waiting to be drawn. Yours is honed to perfection, ready to cut down your foes.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Mental Defense. if the attack hits, the target gains [Mind] stacks of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Shugen Style - Through the Void (Level 2) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Just as water rushes to fill an empty vessel, by regulating the flow of your

ki

you can pull your physical being through space to another's, and potentially pull them to where you were.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Teleport to any empty space adjacent to the target. You may then make a Meditation attack against the target's Mental Defense, and if the attack hits the target Teleports into the space you were originally in.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3

**Snake Style - Heelbiter (Level 2) [Stance]**

When an enemy tries to disengage from combat you creep around them and trip up their movement, steadily constricting them.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: You may spend 1 AP - Interrupt (a creature within range of your Unarmed weapon attempts to move away from you) and make an Unarmed weapon attack against that creature. If the attack hits, the creature's does not move and instead their movement for that action or effect is Countered.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Acrobatics  2

**Snake Style - Serpentine Redirection (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly shifting and swaying in the middle of pitched combat, you can set yourself up to strike an enemy's arm, leg, or other pivotal point in order to misdirect the force of an attack to harm them instead.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Parry Defense).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack flip, but instead of a normal attack just note the result. Your Parry Defense against the attack becomes the result instead, and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack.

If you Parry the attack then it instead targets your attacker, is treated as having a flip result equal to your flip result, and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Acrobatics 2

**Bear Style - Defensive Mauling (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

While lesser men hide behind armor and swing bits of metal at you, with the might of a bear you brush aside their puny attacks.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Parry Defense, and the attacker is within range of your Unarmed weapon).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Target**: The attacking creature.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits or is Parried, you Parry the target's attack and they are Frightened of you once.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Might 3

**Bear Style - Furious Swipes (Level 3) [Stance]**

In an unorthodox method of unarmed fighting you focus the force of your blows in furious, crossing lines of aggression, leaving your prey unable to fully defend themselves against you.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: Your Unarmed weapon attacks cannot be Parried.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Might 3

**Corrupted Blood (Level 3) [Discipline]**

One belief that many civilizations across the land share is that, for a variety of reasons, they consider blood to be important, and perhaps even sacred. This is for good reason - blood sustains living things and is in many ways a conduit for the power a person can wield, and for this very reason that blood magic is so feared. It is also for this reason that some monks perfected this technique, training control over their body's energy to the point where they can corrupt their own blood, turning it to poison on par with that of the most venomous of beasts.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 2 Health.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation flip against difficulty of 11, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose either

Deadly Poison

,

Graven Poison

, or

Wasting Poison

, and you draw enough of your blood to create a fresh dose of the chosen poison. For each Extra Success on the flip you may choose to either increase the poison's Concentration by +2 or increase its Potency by 1.

You may spend 1 AP to use this Technique, and instead you draw the blood with a weapon or piece of ammunition and the poison then coats that weapon instead of collecting into a container.

You are immune to poison created with your blood through this Technique. 24 hours after the poison's creation it loses its potency, becoming a mundane vial of blood.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4, Resilience 3

**Demon Style - Ripjaw Gambit (Level 3) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

By sharpening your

ki

with pure malice and pushing your powers to their brink, you can succeed against all odds and dominate those who stand against you.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target's Mental Defense. The attack deals its damage as Shadow, and if it hits the target gains a stack of Bleeding for each time you Gambled on the attack, plus an extra stack if you flipped a Diamond on the attack.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Meditation 2, Might 1

**Great Old Oak Style - Bark Over Flesh (Level 3) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

An ancient martial form that was the original cornerstone of the style, it is said to have been learned by a master who studied the energy of a great oak tree for an entire moon cycle. He taught his students to suffuse their bodies in power as they fought, dramatically improving their endurance.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Parry Defense hits you).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: You Parry the attack, and it does not hit you.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Meditation 2, Athletics 1

**Shugen Style - Fist of the Third Dragon (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Dragons are creatures who hold great amounts of power, and due to this are naturally skilled in its use. By studying the wisdom and technique of the dragons, acolytes have learned how to channel an enormous amount of energy in impressive displays of power.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: All creatures in a 2-meter-cone from you.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against each target's Bodily Defense. The attack deals

1 + [Mind]

Brilliant damage, and if the attack hits then that target is Slowed once and Pushed back 2 meters; this increases to Slowed twice and Pushed back 4 meters if you flip a Diamond.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Shugen Style - Slip Through Reality (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

By attuning your energies to those of the world around you, you can seemingly step through thin air as a volley of arrows fills the place you were standing just a moment ago.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is declared against your Dodge Defense, before the attack flip is made).

**Effects**: Teleport up to [half your Meditation Skill Total] meters. The attack no longer targets you and you suffer no effects from it, as though it were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are affected normally.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Shugen Style - Unbreakable Spirit Meditation (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Engaging your foe in a battle of wills, you send them reeling with your iron resolve.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Mental Defense. If the attack hits, you Frighten the target twice.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 4

**Snake Style - Turn the Tables (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With a certain limb-lock you can twist a creature you are grappling in the way of oncoming blows, allowing you to deflect some attacks by using your victim as a human(oid) shield.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you successfully grapple a creature).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Target**: The creature you grappled.

**Effects**: You use the target as a shield, lasting until the grapple is ended. While using the target as a shield you may Parry with them as a Brawl weapon with a Defense of +0. If you successfully Parry an attack with the target, the attack instead targets their Parry Defense and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Acrobatics 3

**Summon Spirit Avatar (Level 3) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Intense meditation can unlock the ability to project a fragment of one's own spirit, creating a metaphysical avatar of their own energy.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: An empty space within [Mind] meters.

**Effects**: You Summon a

Spirit Avatar

which appears in the target space and lasts for the encounter.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 3, Integrity 2, Awareness 2

**Bear Style - The Grizzly Awakens (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

With true mastery over the style of the Bear, you shatter the earth with the force of several bears and send a shockwave forward to violently destroy all in its path.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Condition**: You may only use this Technique if your hands are empty.

**Target**: All creatures and large, unattended objects in a 3-meter-long cone. The area of effect passes through any affected objects and is not blocked by them.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the Dodge Defense of each target creature. If it hits and the target is wearing armor, they also gain [target's armor's Physical Resist] stacks of Bleeding.

Any target object that is large enough to be affected is treated as though you had tried to knock it over or batter it down, based on your Might Skill Total.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Might 4

**Demon Style - Wasting Claw (Level 4) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

"Abasi the Devil was known as a master of shadow magicks but also one of the pioneers of the Demon style of supernatural arts. Unlike his contemporaries, however, he studied not in a monastery but instead from the corpses of hundreds of foes, torturing each to their last breath as he ripped their life from them. In doing so he perfected one of the zenith practices of the form, his so-called Wasting Claw that renders its victim so broken that many monks refuse to acknowledge it as a meditative practice at all, referring to it simply as base and terrible necromancy."

- Arcanists of Myth and Legend, Vol. III

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you hit with an attack with your Unarmed weapon, after the attack is resolved).

**Target**: The creature you hit.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the creature's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target gains a stack of Bleeding for each stack of Vulnerable and Necrotic they have, plus an extra stack if you flipped a Diamond. For the encounter, each time they lose Health from Bleeding they gain a stack of Necrotic.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Might 2, Meditation 2

**Great Old Oak Style - Thirsting Roots (Level 4) [Discipline] [Martial] [Encounter]**

The oak sends its roots deep into the forest, drinking life from the ground to grow itself. When your body begins to lose strength you too can reach out and drink life to sustain yourself.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you hit with an attack with your Unarmed weapon, after the attack is resolved).

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the creature's Bodily Defense, and if you flip a Diamond and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you hit, the target gains a stack of Bleeding, plus an additional stack for each Extra Success; then, you gain that number of stacks of Protected.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Athletics 2, Meditation 2

**Harmonious Mending (Level 4) [Discipline]**

You can concentrate toxins and other impurities within your body into a small portion of your body's energy and then expel it from yourself, removing foreign influences and restoring harmony to your body as well as re-balancing your energies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation flip against difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose one of the following:

Remove up to 2 stacks of Bleeding from yourself.

Remove all stacks of Vulnerable from yourself.

If you are suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed.

You heal 1 Health.

For each Extra Success on the flip, you may choose an extra option from the list. You may choose the same option more than once.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Shugen Style - Empowered Blood (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

When an opponent draws your blood, you can send your energy through it and cause it to to sear them in a gruesome counter-attack.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack from an adjacent creature causes you to lose Health, after you lose the Health).

**Target**: The attacker.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the target's Dodge Defense. The attack deals

5 + [Mind]

Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Shugen Style - Indomitable Ascension (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Your spirit surges with power, giving you an otherworldly aura as you shed mortal fears and cause your enemies to uncontrollably avert their eyes from your brilliance.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Meditation Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Remove all stacks of Frightened and Taunted from yourself. Make a Meditation attack against the Mental Defense of each target, and if it hits then you Frighten that creature twice.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Shugen Style - Whirlwind of the Ethereal Emperor (Level 4) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Charging the

ki

of your enemies, you pull them into your grasp with the force of a raging wind.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [half your Meditation Skill Total] meters of you.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the Bodily Defense of each target. If the attack hits, the target is Teleported into the empty space closest to you; if two spaces would be tied, you may choose. Any target not hit by the attack is Pushed 2 meters in a direction of your choice.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5

**Snake Style - Chainbreaker (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"One of the most historically esteemed practitioners of the martial tradition of the Snake, Master Raha, is said to have been imprisoned by a local warring nation when he was a young man. Though he was strong in the art of the Snake, he was not a Master then, and he toiled in labor camps for a few long years under their oppressive regime. Every night and morning he meditated, looking for guidance and a way to free himself and others. One day he grew strong enough to break his shackles, and using them as a weapon he defeated scores of guards and freed the entire camp. Once he returned to his monastery he taught of what he had learned, how one's limbs flow outward like chains to strike many places at once, and the art of the Snake flourished for his wisdom."

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Condition**: Your hands are empty.

**Target**: A creature within range of your Unarmed weapon.

**Effects**: Make an Unarmed weapon attack against the target. If the attack hits, you regain the use of this Technique.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Acrobatics 4

**Shugen Style - Immolate Soul (Level 5) [Discipline] [Encounter]**

Charging the

ki

of your enemies, you pull them into your grasp with the force of a raging wind.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus; any amount of Health (minimum 1)

**Target**: All creatures within 3 meters of you.

**Effects**: Make a Meditation attack against the Bodily Defense of each target. The attack deals

5 + [Health paid as cost]

Brilliant damage.

**Prereqs**: Meditation 5, Spirit 5, Mind 5

## Movement, Reflex, & Strength
*Source: `movement-reflex-strength.html` (Part 1)*

Movement, Reflex, & Strength

**Blinkstep (Level 2) [Encounter]**

You are especially quick on your feet, capable of bursts of blinding speed.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Teleport up to [Acrobatics Skill Total] meters. For this Teleport your destination must be a space you could have reached by moving, but you do not need to be able to see your destination.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3

**Bound Across Mountains (Level 2) [Encounter]**

Extensive practice in leaping and bounding over fences and across streams has trained you to the point where, with an explosive burst of effort, you can hurl yourself over truly vast obstacles.

**Action**: Move.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You jump [Athletics Skill Total] meters horizontally or half that distance vertically. For this jump you do not need a running start, and when determining falling damage after the jump you consider the distance you fell to be [Athletics Skill Total] meters shorter.

Prereqs: Athletics 3, Might 2

**Overbear (Level 2) [Encounter]**

When the strength of your arm begins to fail you, you are capable of drawing deep from within yourself to carry on.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Your Carrying Capacity is doubled for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Resistance 2

**Hulking Hurl (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Adapting methods used in a variety of strongman sports, you can launch large objects surprising distances.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An object with a Weight no greater than [half your Might Skill Total] .

**Effects**: You hurl the target object up to [Might Skill Total] meters. If the object would collide with another object, they are both affected appropriately. If the object would collide with a creature, make a Might attack against the creature's Dodge Defense, dealing

[your Strength] + [object's Weight]

Physical damage on a hit.

**Prereqs**: Might 4, Athletics 2, Resistance 2

**Step of the Wind (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Your balance and agility are without peer, allowing you to move across terrain in ways that lesser people would think defies gravity.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Until the end of your turn you gain a +2 bonus to Speed and you may move across vertical surfaces and on top of water or similar surfaces at full Speed as long as you begin and end all of your movement on otherwise stable footing.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 4

**Preternatural Reflexes (Level 4)**

Your instincts are razor sharp, and you react to danger the instant it presents itself. It is impossible to catch you off-guard and as soon as any conflict breaks out you are leaping into action while everyone else is still drawing their swords.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When combat begins, after turn order is determined but before the first combatant takes a turn, you may take up to one Move action and up to one Extra action as though it were your turn.

If there is a s urprise round, at the end of the surprise round you may take up to one Move action and up to one Extra action as though it were your turn. You are also aware of any attacks against you or any effects that include you as a target during the surprise round.

**Prereqs**: Cunning 4

**Spider Climb (Level 4)**

Your limbs are strong enough that you can traverse vertical distances as easily as you do horizontal ones.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may climb across any surface as long as that surface has enough texture to hold onto. You may move at your full Speed while climbing without requiring any flips.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 5, Might 2, Acrobatics 2

**Lightning Stride (Level 5)**

Your athletic prowess and near-perfect coordination have given you unparalleled speed, dancing around lesser people

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your Speed is increased by 3. You have Good Luck on any flips where running speed is a factor, such as chasing or outrunning anyone.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 5, Acrobatics 5, Dexterity 5

---

## Overpowering
*Source: `overpowering.html` (Part 2)*

Whether through superior strength, speed, or durability, Overpowering abilities let you succeed through sheer capability.

**Brace (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You draw a breath, steeling yourself for the struggle ahead.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You gain 2 stacks of Protected.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 2, Might 1

**Tavern Brawler (Level 1)**

A veteran of melees in taprooms, bars, and other places where booze flows and tempers run high, you are well-versed in fighting with just about any object that might be at hand.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may treat any Improvised weapons you wield as standard Melee or Thrown weapons, with the exact type depending on the object.

Once per scene when an attack you make with an Improvised weapon Downs a creature, you may destroy the Improvised weapon. If you do, any number of creatures adjacent to the Downed creature gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Might 2, Resilience 1

**Bladeslinging (Level 2)**

By adopting an unorthodox grip on your weapon, you can balance it in a way that allows you to leverage a greatsword with only one arm or throw the entire weight of your body behind a small blade.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may change how you wield a close-range weapon, using the same action as though you were drawing or sheathing a weapon. This allows you to  wield a one-handed weapon with two hands as though it were a two-handed weapon, or vice-versa. A weapon that becomes two-handed this way has its Damage increased by 1, and one that becomes one-handed this way has its Damage decreased by 1.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Acrobatics 1, Academics 1

**Blindsight (Level 2)**

By learning to effectively use echolocation, feel the motion of air against your skin, or simply honing and leveraging multiple senses to compensate for your sense of sight, you are capable of doing without it and navigating in total darkness.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You are aware of everything within [Awareness Skill Total] meters as though you could see it, although fine details such as color and facial features are difficult or impossible to tell. Depending on circumstances, you may be able to tell some details through physical objects. Effectively, you ignore Cover from anything that does not occupy any space.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 3

**Body of the Donkey (Level 2) [Re-Learning]**

You have the toughness of a pack animal, capable of enduring beyond what most people could. Though you may be battered and bruised, it takes a bit more punishment to bring you down.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Maimed (3)' Health level by two and your 'Bloodied (2)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 3, (Might or Integrity) 2

**Fluid Movement (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You are a blur, capable of dancing around others before they can react.

**Action**: 0 AP.

**Effects**: Shift up to [Speed] meters.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3

**For Medicinal Purposes (Level 2)**

As someone with a seasoned liver, you know exactly which liquors cure which ailments (all of them and any of them, respectively).

Action: 2 AP.

Cost: 1 alcoholic drink, which you must have in hand.

Effects: You drink the Alcoholic drink, then choose one:

You heal ([the alcoholic drink's Level] - 1) Health.

Reduce the effects of one poison afflicting you as though ([the alcoholic drink's Level] - 1) hours had passed.

Prereqs: Resilience 3

**Overbear (Level 2)**

Your companions know you as a human(oid) pack animal. Your arm never seems to tire.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on flips to carry, drag, push, or otherwise move large objects. You ignore Might Skill Total requirements for backpacks and similar containers.

**Prereqs**: Might 3

**Pranic Nourishment (Level 2)**

You can tune your body into the metaphysical weave of your surroundings, sustaining yourself off of the energy of the world around you in addition to that which exists within all matter.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You only need to meditate for half an hour in order to get a full night's rest, and you heal an extra 1 Health when you get a full night's rest. You only need to eat one bite of food and drink one drink of water in order to fully sustain yourself for a day.

In addition, you may eat almost anything as long as you can safely get it in your mouth and it will sustain you as regular food would with no adverse effects, rendering you effectively immune to ingested poisons. This effect does not prevent most forms of intoxication, such as from alcohol or similar substances, but will prevent extreme forms that cause noticeable harm to you, such as alcohol poisoning or very harmful drugs.

**Prereqs**: (Might or Resilience) 3, Meditation 2

**Quick Draw (Level 2)**

You have an uncanny knack for both always having just what you need right at hand and having quick fingers readying your equipment.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: It takes you 0 AP to retrieve or store an item, regardless of location, although it must still be within reach.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

**Rage (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You can fly into a bloodrage in combat, making you a fearsome but reckless warrior.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You enter a rage until the end of the encounter, gaining the following:

You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

If one of your weapon attacks is Parried, or if the target's Resist would reduce the attack's damage to 0, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

If a target Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Athletics 1, Resilience 1

**Second Wind (Level 2)**

Far from being frail and sickly, even when you've been battered and bruised you can catch your breath and keep pushing forward.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You heal 3 Health.

**Prereqs**: (Might, Resilience, or Integrity) 3

**Trout Seeming (Level 2)**

You were born to swim, taking to the water like a natural. Even in the midst of hectic combat you can effortlessly glide across a raging river.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may swim or otherwise move through water at your full Speed without making any flips. You have Good Luck on all flips to hold your breath.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 3, Resilience 2

**Victory Swig (Level 2)**

Everyone knows that proper taproom etiquette dictates you pour a bit of a drink out for a fallen fighter... even if you put them there.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (you Down a creature).

**Cost**: 1

alcoholic drink

, which you must have in hand.

**Target**: Any number of creatures who can see you.

**Effects**: You drink the Alcoholic drink, then make a Resilience attack against the Mental Defense of each target. If the attack hits, you Frighten the target [the alcoholic drink's Level] times.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 3, Presence 1, Streetwise 1

**Body of the Mule (Level 3) [Re-Learning]**

You're not quite as durable as an ox, but you're the next best thing.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Bloodied (-2)' Health level by two and your 'Scratched (-1)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 4, (Might or Integrity) 3

**Bound Across Mountains (Level 3)**

Extensive practice in leaping and bounding over fences and across streams has trained you to the point where, with an explosive burst of effort, you can hurl yourself over truly vast obstacles.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When jumping you do not need to flip, and may instead treat the action as though you had flipped a 13.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 4, Might 3

**Falling Tumble (Level 3) [Encounter]**

With perfect balance you turn the momentum from a fall into a nimble roll.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you fall from a height that is no greater than [Acrobatics Skill Total] meters).

**Effects**: You automatically succeed your flip to avoid harm from the fall. At the end of the fall, you may Shift up to [half the fall's height] meters in one direction.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 4

**Flamebreath (Level 3)**

Your body is sturdy enough, inside and out, to ignite alcohol and direct the ensuing fireblast away from you, scorching others while you remain unharmed.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1

alcoholic drink

, which you must have in hand.

**Target**: All creatures within a 2-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: You drink the Alcoholic drink, then make a Resilience attack against the Dodge Defense of each target. The attack deals

[Body] + [the alcoholic drink's Level]

Fire Damage.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 4

**Instantaneous Observation (Level 3)**

With a moment of extreme concentration you can perfectly survey the room you're in, taking in the scene with a glimpse.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Everything within [half your Awareness Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make an Awareness flip, and you learn about the target area as though you had thoroughly searched it with your result. For closed containers and other objects that cannot be feasibly searched, you don't gain exact knowledge about their contents but instead more general knowledge about the object and its possible contents - what it is most likely to contain, whether or not a door or drawer is locked, and so on.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 4

**Power Through (Level 3)**

Even when good sense and your body's physical limits would keep you down, you find the strength to push on (regardless of whether or not you should).

**Action**: 0 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Health.

**Effects**: Remove a stack of either Crippled or Slowed from yourself. You are Bolstered once.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 4, Might 2, Integrity 1

**Skin of Stone (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You briefly strengthen your body, allowing your extremely sturdy physical form to take a tremendous assault with almost no harm.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You gain [half your Resilience Skill Total] stacks of Protected.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 4, (Athletics or Might) 3

**Strength from the Slain (Level 3) [Encounter]**

Many barbarian tribes practice the ritual of eating the hearts of enemy warriors slain in combat. This is thought to grant one the courage and strength of their fallen enemies, and while many people deride the practice and uncivilized and inhuman you know there is value to it, whether to further a fierce reputation or out of simple pragmatism.

**Action**: 1 minute.

**Effects**: You ritually eat the heart of a humanoid who you or an ally Downed within the last scene. If they were not already dead this instantly kills them. You then gain the following benefits:

You heal 1 Health.

You are sustained as though you had eaten a full meal.

You have Good Luck on the next Athletics, Might, Presence, or Resilience flip you make in the next hour.

**Prereqs**: Might 3, Resilience 3, Presence 2, Integrity 1

**Body of the Ox (Level 4) [Re-Learning]**

Your resilience is legendary, rivaling that of a dozen beasts of burden.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Scratched (-1)' Health level by two and your 'Winded (-0)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 5, (Might or Integrity) 4

**Destructive Rush (Level 4) [Encounter]**

Leveraging the strength of your entire body, you launch yourself forward and destroy everything in your path.

**Action**: 3 AP.

**Effects**: You Shift up to 3 meters in a single direction.

If any objects are in your way, make a Might flip with Good Luck to destroy them. If you succeed, that object is completely destroyed; this can affect sections of wall and similar material. If you fail to destroy an object, your movement (and this Technique's effects) immediately end.

If any creatures are in your way, make a Might attack with Good Luck against their Dodge Defense. If they Dodge the attack they move 1 meter away from you, and if they cannot instead they are hit by the attack. If the attack hits, the target gains a stack of Bleeding, plus an extra stack if you flipped a Spade, and an extra stack for each Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Might 5

**Frenzy (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You are capable of losing yourself entirely in your bloodlust and fury, entering a berserk state in which you abandon any semblance of self-preservation and launch yourself into your foes.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Effects**: You enter a frenzy until the end of the scene. While in the frenzy the following applies to you:

You have Good Luck on Might flips.

You ignore any stacks of Crippled, Slowed, or Wounded you have.

You have Good Luck on weapon attacks.

You suffer a -3 penalty to Dodge Defense, and you may not Parry.

At the end of your turn, if you are aware of an enemy and do not move towards them or make an attack against them, you gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: Might 5, Athletics 2, Resilience 2

**Hardened Resolve (Level 4) [Re-Learning]**

Through a mix of physical fitness and pure grit you can take more punishment than the average person before collapsing.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Maimed (-3)' and 'Bloodied (-2)' Health levels each by two and your 'Scratched (-1)' Health level by one.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 3, Might 3, Integrity 3

**Spider Climb (Level 4)**

Your limbs are strong and agile enough that you can traverse vertical distances as easily as you do horizontal ones.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may climb across any surface as long as that surface has enough texture to hold onto. You may move at your full Speed while climbing without requiring any flips, as long as your hands are free.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 5, Might 2, Acrobatics 2

**Body of the Immortal (Level 5) [Re-Learning]**

If an assassin tried to kill you, they would find that one cowardly stab wouldn't do the trick. If you were brought before an executioner, they would need something heavier than an axe. Defying fate, you simply refuse to die.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Increase the maximum Health of your 'Winded (-0)' Health level by three.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 5, Might 5, Integrity 5

**Lightning Stride (Level 5)**

Your athletic prowess and near-perfect coordination have given you unparalleled speed, dancing around lesser people

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your Speed is increased by 2. You have Good Luck on any flips where running speed is a factor, such as chasing or outrunning anyone.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 5, Athletics 5, Agility 5

**Unbreakable Stone Anchor (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

A mighty mountain in the middle of a blizzard, when you stand tall you are capable of withstanding onslaughts that would outright kill lesser men, emerging unscathed from the most terrible of carnage.

**Action**: 0 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Until the start of your next turn you cannot lose any Health. This does not apply to Health spent as a cost.

**Prereqs**: Might 5, Resilience 5, Body 5

**Veil of Dispersing Shadows (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You have achieved an unmatched degree of agility, with a knack for avoiding harm that borders on precognition. With this maneuver, the pinnacle of its kind, as a blow strikes you your form shifts imperceptibly and the attack seems to narrowly glance through your shadow.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense hits you, before its effects are resolved).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You ignore all effects of the attack, as though it were Countered. Any other targets of the attack are still affected normally.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 5, Insight 5, Agility 5
