## Social
*Source: `social.html` (Part 1)*

Social

**Bow Out (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

You excuse yourself from the conversation, shifting some of the focus of the discussion onto yourself in a way that makes your friends look a bit better.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Maneuvering social statement against the other party, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, the next time your party would gain Demerits they ignore one of those Demerits, and you are removed from the social contest.

**Prereqs**: (Persuasion or Rapport) 2, Insight 1

**Brawn Over Banter (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

"...because of which I would strongly urge that you consider our offer."

"Yes, yes, I've heard all of this before, and I still fail to see... the reason why I would..."

"Is there a problem?"

"I... no, but it's quite difficult to sort this business out while your shirtless friend over there keeps flexing his muscles..."

"Then we had best wrap this up quickly, no? Trust me, he can do that all day."

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Maneuvering social statement against the other party, using Might as the relevant Skill. If you flip a Spade and succeed, you gain an Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Might 2, Insight 1

**Gift of Garrulousness  (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

Sometimes when you get talking you just keep going on and on... and nobody can seem to get a word in edgewise.

**Action**: During your turn in a social contest, once per turn

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You may immediately take an extra turn in the current social contest. For this extra turn you are considered to be the front member of your party, but are not considered to have any back members.

**Prereqs**: (Presence, Persuasion, or Rapport) 3, Integrity 2

**Menacing Insinuation (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

"Entirely unrelated to the current matter, but I think the dust scorpion is a fascinating creature; did you know its venom can pass into the body by mere touch? A century ago it was a favored tool of merchant princes, whose spies would use the venom to kill the families of competitors who refused trade agreements with those princes. Its use is so hard to tell, we only know about the practice from memoirs - the assassins were never caught. But anyway, I apologize for rambling, back to our negotiations..."

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Forceful social statement against the other party, using Streetwise as the relevant Skill. If you flip a Spade and succeed, you gain an Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Savvy 2, Integrity 1

**"Well, Actually..." (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

Having read entire shelves of books on some of the most obscure topics, you can call up factoids and figures from your studies so that when someone tries to argue with you, you can correct them on the spot.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Debate social statement against the other party, using Academics as the relevant Skill. If you flip a Spade and succeed, you gain an Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2

**Witty Quote (Level 1) [Social] [Encounter]**

Erudite and practiced in theatrics, you have a flair for quoting books and plays in the middle of discussions, catching those who disagree with you quite off-guard.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Debate social statement against the other party, using Performance as the relevant Skill. If you flip a Spade and succeed, you gain an Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Performance 2

**Corner and Interrogate (Level 2) [Social] [Encounter]**

"No, excuse me, I was talking to your friend and I would appreciate it if you'd stop trying to interrupt. She's a big girl, you don't have to stand up for her, let her speak for herself."

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest, while you are the front member of your party.

**Effects**: Make a Debate social statement against the other party, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose one member of the other party and note who they are. On each of the other party's turns, if they do not declare that person as the front member of their party they suffer a Demerit. This effect lasts for the rest of the social contest or until your team does not declare you as the front member of your party.

**Prereqs**: Persuasion 3, Insight 2

**Gritty Countenance (Level 2) [Social] [Encounter]**

Whether you're at a podium having a debate or sitting at a tavern glaring at someone from behind your friend's back, your stern demeanor makes people think twice before criticizing you or your friends too harshly.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Forceful social statement against the other party, using Presence as the relevant Skill. If you flip a Spade and succeed, you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, until your party's next turn your front member may use

8 + [your Presence Skill Total]

in place of any of their Defenses.

**Prereqs**: Presence 3, Resilience 2

**Logos Overwhelming (Level 2) [Social] [Encounter]**

With your study in formal logic and philosophical reasoning, you can thoroughly shut down an opponent's argument.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Debate social statement against the other party, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, the other party has Bad Luck on the next Debate social attack that any of their front members make this social contest.

**Prereqs**: Persuasion 3, Academics 2

**Undermine Confidence (Level 2) [Social] [Encounter]**

"No, I don't quite agree with your stance. You've fundamentally misunderstood every factor at play here, and furthermore you greatly overestimate your leverage in this situation. I'll humor you by reiterating my offer once more, and if you want to keep stubbornly insisting you deserve more than I am willing to give in this exchange then it will make it crystal clear to me you are not worth dealing with."

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a social statement against the other party, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, note the type of social statement you made and who the other party's front member is - that person has Disadvantage on all social statements of that type for the rest of the social contest.

**Prereqs**: (Presence, Persuasion, or Rapport) 3, Insight 2

**Bully Into Submission (Level 3) [Social] [Encounter]**

By gauging someone's insecurities and whittling away at their confidence, over the course of a discussion you can make even the most headstrong people in the room end up sulking in the corner for the evening.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Forceful social statement against the other party using Presence, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, choose one member of the other party and note who they are. If you win the social contest then that person will remove themselves from the scene as much as they reasonably  can, and for at least the rest of the day they will act more subdued and withdrawn. Left to their own devices they will most likely recover, but other events might influence their mood more negatively and with more long-lasting consequences.

**Prereqs**: Presence 4, Insight 3

**Leading Question (Level 3) [Social] [Encounter]**

You've mastered the art of setting someone up in a conversation; anyone who discusses sensitive matters with you unwary of your skill may easily end up revealing something they'd rather stay secret.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Maneuvering social statement against the other party, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed and ultimately win the social contest, that person lets slip a piece of information about the subject of the social contest about which they were otherwise trying to avoid mentioning or keep secret. This might be something they were lying about, trying to omit, or speaking in circles around, but it must be specifically related to the topic that was discussed. Based on the social contest outcome you choose, that person may be willing - or coerced into - giving more information about the subject.

**Prereqs**: (Persuasion or Rapport) 4, Insight 3

**New Best Friend (Level 3) [Social] [Encounter]**

"Oh yeah, guys, meet Ben! We've been talking over drinks all night, did you know he's the guard captain around here? Ben, show 'em your sword; guys isn't that the nicest blade you've seen since we were at the Duke's last month? Anyways, I told Ben here about how we're looking for the one guy, and he was thinking maybe we could stop by the barracks tomorrow and flip through some files he has that might have some stuff about the one incident with the goats that started all of this."

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Effects**: Make a Maneuvering or Forceful social statement against the other party using Rapport, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed, ultimately win the social contest, and choose Friendly as the outcome, even after the scene ends the shift in the target's opinion from the outcome will be permanent and from that point on. Unless you do something to change their opinion then they will treat you with a favorable attitude, as though you are a friend whose approval they value.

**Prereqs**: Rapport 4, Insight 3

**Verbal Beatdown (Level 5) [Social] [Encounter]**

Using a variety of conversational techniques you meticulously deconstruct someone's argument as well as their choice of vocation and life choices, closing out with a scathing dismissal of everything they stand for.

**Action**: Your turn in a social contest.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Make a Maneuvering, Debate, and a Forceful social statement against the other party. For each of these, if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Presence 5, Persuasion 5, Rapport 5

---

## Specialties
*Source: `specialties.html` (Part 2)*

Specialties

A

specialty

represents a singular facet of a Skill that you are particularly skilled at. For Skill rolls that fit a Specialty you have, you get one automatic success added to your result.

The below is not an exhaustive list of Specialties; if there is one which is not listed that you think is appropriate for a Skill, ask your DM!

In addition to the listed Specialties, for any Skill you can choose to specialize in a specific Technique; when using that Technique you gain an automatic success on Skill rolls and +2 to Skill pools for the Skill you purchased the Specialty for.

You may

not

choose Parry, Dodge, Resistance, or Mental Defenses as a Specialty, and no Specialty can improve them.

**Athletics**: Jumping, swimming, climbing

**Might**: Moving things, breaking things

**Acrobatics**: Tumbling, balancing, escaping from grapples/restraints, any one specific weapon/category (as listed under Weapons and Armor)

**Archery**: Any one specific weapon/category (as listed under Weapons and Armor)

**Legerdemain**: Sleight of hand, lockpicking, trap finding/disabling,

**Martial Arts**: Any one specific weapon/category (as listed under Weapons and Armor)

**Melee**: Any one specific weapon/category (as listed under Weapons and Armor), improvised weapons

**Stealth**: Hiding behind cover, sneaking past someone, avoiding notice

**Endurance**: Extended marches/chases, resist pain/trauma, going without food/water/sleep (adds 1 day)

**Resistance**: Resisting poison, disease, exposure

**Awareness**: Sight, hearing, taste/smell

**Sense Motive**: Detecting lies/truth, determining true motive Survival: Navigation, finding water/food in the wild, handling animals, tracking

**Craft**: Choose any one field

**Knowledge**: Choose any one field

**Linguistics**: n/a

**Medicine**: First aid, surgery, anatomy

**Occult**: Choose a specific Arcane Spell; gain an automatic success on Occult rolls with that spell, or +2 to Occult pools for that spell

**Centering**: Meditation

**Integrity**: Resisting fear

**Disguise**: A specific category of disguises; guardsmen, commonfolk, merchants, and so forth

**Intimidate**: Interrogate, cow

**Persuasion**: Lying, negotiation, "seduction"/winning over

**Savvy**: Gather information, streetwise

**Diplomacy**: Inspiring others, endearing yourself to another, seduction

**Perform**: Choose any one field

**Prayer**: Choose a specific Divine Spell; gain an automatic success on Prayer rolls with that spell, or +2 to Prayer pools for that spell

---

## Spell Techniques
*Source: `spell-techniques.html` (Part 1)*

Spell Techniques

Spells are Techniques that invoke or manipulate various kinds of magical energies to do a variety of things from mending wounds to creating enormous plumes of fire to controlling the weather itself. Spells are broken into three broad disciplines - Elementalism, Theurgy, and General Spells. Elementalism manipulates energy into very blatant forms and channels great deals of power to obliterate enemies and create great magical effects. Theurgy is a more subtle art, drawing on energies to infuse allies with power, mend wounds, or bind enemies and sway the flow of battle in your favor. General Spells is a category that contains a wide variety of utility and convenience magic that does not require a specific sort of magical prowess to work, just the ability to channel sufficient power.

If a Spell requires an attack flip, it will list the Skill used for the flip. Generally, Elementalism Spells use Elementalism and Theurgy Spells use Theurgy.

Some Spells will list a specific range at which they can affect a target. Specifically, any targets the Spell has must fall within this range, and if it affects an area then the area's center must fall within this range.

The range might be listed as an 'adjacent' target, saying to make a 'melee [Skill] attack' or a 'ranged [Skill] attack', or simply listing a range. The default range for ranged Spells or Spells without a specific range is twice its relevant Skill Value, in meters. Elementalism Spells have a default range of

2 × [Intelligence + Elementalism]

meters and Theurgy Spells have a default range of

2 × [Charisma + Theurgy]

meters.

---

## Spells
*Source: `spells.html` (Part 2)*

Spells

These Techniques are Spells, abilities with which you use your knowledge of magic to blast enemies, aid allies, and create all kinds of amazing effects.

---

## Spirit
*Source: `spirit.html` (Part 2)*

Spirit

**Storied Adventurer (Level 1)**

Your stories and deeds have a tendency to make it to local taverns, and with your winning attitude and adventurous demeanor you frequently find yourself celebrating your victories.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Word of your good deeds, successful adventures, and similar accomplishments is much more likely to spread to nearby settlements, being retold in inns and taverns. These stories will spread based on the actions you take, size of the nearby population, and other similar factors.

Those who have heard of your accomplishments tend to give you the benefit of the doubt, are inclined to think you're an honorable sort, and have a somewhat positive initial impression of you, so long as they have no particular reason to distrust you. You gain a bonus to social interactions involving such people - this may mean free drinks at a tavern, certain people offering information they might not otherwise, or simply Good Luck on relevant flips.

**Prereqs**: (Streetwise, Performance, or Rapport) 2

**Instill Emotion (Level 2)**

Your music, speeches, or other performances are so powerful and moving that they are capable of inspiration that borders on mind control. Rare is the man who cannot be brought to tears by one of your melancholy dirges.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Effects**: Choose an emotion you would like to influence bystanders towards and make a Performance flip with a difficulty based on the crowd's current mood; if you flip a Spade and succeed then you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed then the overall mood of the social venue will shift based on the emotion you chose, and the shift will be greater for each Extra Success. This effect lasts for the scene, but the change in mood might last longer depending on the situation.

**Prereqs**: Performance 3

**Words of Encouragement (Level 2)**

When your allies' spirits begin to wane, you know just what to say to light a fire in their hearts.

**Action Points**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: An ally within [Performance Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target ally draws two cards, then discards a card.

**Prereqs**: Performance 3

**Font of Inspiration (Level 3)**

Whenever their backs are against the wall, your friends know they can always look to you to boost their spirits.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Whenever an ally within [Performance Skill Total] meters spends any Focus, for [Focus spent] rounds they re-flip any cards 4 or lower.

**Prereqs**: Performance 4

**Raise Spirits (Level 3)**

Even though you may not know how to do everything, you know how to inspire your friends to do anything.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When giving Backup to an ally you re-flip any cards 4 or lower. You may also use Performance instead of the Skill used by your ally, which may let you give Backup in situations where it would otherwise not be possible.

**Prereqs**: Performance 4

**Lay On Hands (Level 4)**

Those with sufficient courage and will are capable of stoking their own life's flame to burn bright and use that to comfort their allies and burn away any impurities.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 charge of this Technique (see below).

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent willing creature.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following:

The target heals 1 Health.

Remove up to 2 Bleeds from the target.

If the target is suffering from any poisons, their effects are reduced as though an hour had passed.

If the target is infected with any diseases, they make a Resistance flip against the disease's ongoing difficulty and end the disease's effects on a success.

**Special**: After you get a full night's rest, you may spend any amount of Focus. For each Focus spent you gain [half your Rapport Skill total] charges for this Technique, which last until you get a full night's rest.

**Prereqs**: Rapport 3, Presence 2, Integrity 2

**Rally to Victory (Level 4)**

In every great war story, when the battle is grim and the army is on the brink of defeat a hero will rise above the masses and inspire them to victory. When your friends are struggling against a great foe you appear as something out of a storybook, the great hero who pushes his companions to greatness.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: Up to [Perform Skill Total] willing allies who can see or hear you.

**Effects**: Each target draws a card. At the end of the scene, each target discards a card.

**Prereqs**: Performance 5

---

## Stances
*Source: `stances.html` (Part 1)*

Stances

You may enter a Stance as an Extra action, and leave a Stance as a Non-action. You may not leave a Stance in the same turn in which you entered it.

You may only be in one Stance at a time, and entering a Stance will cause you to leave any other Stance you are in.

Some Stances list a condition that must be met in order to enter that Stance. Some Stances may also list a condition where you will automatically leave the Stance.

**Alacritous Posture (Level 1) [Stance]**

Rather than dashing around the battlefield, you focus your attention on making many small movements and reacting to those around you.

**Condition**: You have taken no actions this turn.

**Effects**: You may use Main and Move actions as though they were Extra or Counter actions. If you do not use them during your turn, they may be used as Counter actions after your turn.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 2, Cunning 1

**Half-Guard (Level 1) [Stance]**

You shift your concentration towards your own defense, strengthening your guard.

**Condition**: You may enter this Stance if you have not moved this turn.

**Effects**: You gain a +1 bonus to Parry Defense. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: (Martial Arts or Melee) 3, Insight 2

**Lawman's Hand (Level 1) [Stance]**

Bounty hunters, local guards, and spies alike all share the need to bring a target down without killing them. Whether to be delivered in front of some authority or to question them at length, a number of methods exist for taking the fight out of someone without taking their life as well.

**Effects**: You may not gamble on weapon attacks. If one of your weapon attacks would cause a creature to become Downed they are instead knocked unconscious for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2, (Intelligence or Cunning) 1

**Breaching Blows (Level 2) [Stance]**

You leverage your large weapon against your foes, swinging with enough force that they find it difficult to avoid injury entirely.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: You must Gamble at least once when you make a weapon attack with a two-handed close-range weapon. If one of these attacks is Parried, the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: (Martial Arts or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Defensive Roll (Level 2) [Stance]**

By remaining properly centered in the middle of a fight, you can react fluidly to an opponent that tries to knock you off-balance and use the force to tumble into an advantageous position.

**Condition**: You may enter this Stance if you have not moved this turn.

**Effects**: If an effect would knock you Prone you may ignore that effect. If an effect would Push you, instead you may Shift in any direction up to the distance you would have been Pushed. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 3, Athletics 2

**Doubleshot (Level 2) [Stance]**

With a firm grip and steady aim you can launch volleys of missiles at an enemy, wearing them down and suppressing them with your barrage.

**Effects**: Instead of making attacks with ranged weapons as a Main action, you must make them as a Full action. When you do, you may make up to 2 weapon attacks against your target. This does not change how any of your Techniques work.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Archery) 3, Legerdemain 2

**Drunken Brawling (Level 2) [Stance]**

With a style of close quarters combat known affectionately as "drunken brawling", you learn to move with an unpredictable swaying and swagger that helps you evade combatants with ease.

**Condition**: You have not moved this turn.

**Mechanics**: If a creature makes an attack against you as part of a Counter action, they have Bad Luck on that attack. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 3, Insight 2

**Earthen Fist Posture (Level 2) [Stance]**

By standing firmly and leveraging your strength to deliver strikes with as much force as possible, you trade hand-to-hand defense for oppressive offense.

**Effects**: Whenever a target Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks they are Pushed back 1 meter. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defenses.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Flurry of Strikes (Level 2) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: When a target applies their Dodge or Parry Defense against one of your weapon attacks, that target is Harried once.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Martial Arts, or Melee) 3, Acrobatics 2

**Group Bulwark (Level 2) [Stance]**

You extend your shield out, sacrificing your own defenses to provide cover for one of your friends-in-arms.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: When you enter this Stance, you focus on an ally and choose a shield you are wielding. While you are adjacent to that ally, they gain a + [chosen shield's Defense] bonus to Parry Defense and you may not Parry with the chosen shield.

Prereqs: Melee 3, Acrobatics 2

**Ki Style - Dragon's Fang (Level 2) [Stance]**

Rather than using raw strength to power your unarmed attacks, you alter your energy such that your limbs become conduits for your spiritual power.

**Effects**: Your Unarmed weapon attacks deal their damage as Spirit damage and you cannot parry with your Unarmed weapon.

**Prereqs**: Centering 3, Martial Arts 2

**Overchanneling (Level 2) [Stance]**

Many styles of warmage training focus on overwhelming power and raw eldritch offense over all else, and each of those that do have built their practices with this stance as a foundation. With a series of motions that leverage your entire body to gather magical energy and intricate double-handed gestures to keep control over it, you wring every last measure of potency from your incantations.

**Condition**: Both your hands are empty. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: When you make a damaging spell attack, that spell attack deals an extra 1 damage of any type it would deal. You may not Parry attacks.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Stamina 2, Will 2

**Sentinel (Level 2) [Stance]**

You focus on enemies around you, matching their every step and following their movements carefully to keep them under control.

**Condition**: You have not moved this turn. Leave if you move.

**Effects**: Whenever an enemy adjacent to you moves or Shifts you may immediately Shift into the space they moved from, and you may continue to follow them in this way as long as they move or Shift.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

**Shifting Ground (Level 2) [Stance]**

Light on your feet and keeping constant momentum, you dance away from your enemies' blows and keep yourself from being pinned down in combat.

**Condition**: You have not moved this turn.

**Effects**: Whenever you Parry or Dodge an attack you may Shift 1 meter. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

**Sixth Sense Blade Rhythm (Level 2) [Stance]**

As a practitioner of the duelist's arts you are constantly mindful of both your enemies' and your own movements, making your defenses seem impenetrable to the untrained eye.

**Condition**: You are fencing. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: You may treat any weapon you wield as having a Defense of +3.

**Prereqs**: (Martial Arts or Melee) 3, Insight 2

**Concussive Swings (Level 3) [Stance]**

Aiming your attacks at limbs and joints rather than vitals, you cause the force of your attacks to unbalance your enemies rather than wound them.

**Effects**: If one of your weapon attacks would cause a creature to lose Health, instead that creature is Harried [twice the Health that would have been lost] times.

**Prereqs**: (Martial Arts or Melee) 4, Might 3

**Follow Through (Level 3) [Stance]**

When your blows fell an enemy you can carry the force from the attack forward, striking another foe down.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

Effects: When one of your weapon attacks with a two-handed close-range weapon Downs a creature, you may immediately make an attack with that weapon as a Non-action. Once you make one of these attacks, you may not make another until the start of your next turn.

Prereqs: (Martial Arts or Melee) 4, Might 3

**One-Man Phalanx (Level 3) [Stance]**

Widening your guard to better encompass all of your enemies, although you cannot block weapons at quite the proper angle you stand in such a way that you can knock aside blows without losing your balance.

**Condition**: You may enter this Stance as normal.

**Effects**: You ignore any stacks of Harried you have. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 4, Awareness 3

**Raptor's Precision (Level 3) [Stance]**

By taking a moment to adjust your grip, technique, aim, and many other factors, you can deliver attacks that are much more precise and harder to guard against.

**Effects**: You re-flip any cards 4 or lower for weapon attacks. Any weapon attacks or Techniques that would have you to make weapon attacks which take a Main or a Move action instead must be made as a Full action. You may not use any Techniques that would have you to make weapon attacks which take a Full action.

**Prereqs**: (Legerdemain or Intelligence) 4

**Reaping Dervish (Level 3) [Stance]**

A whirlwind of steel on the field of battle, while this aggressive posture leaves you vulnerable to attack at points it also leads into flowing movements that deflect all oncoming attacks.

**Effects**: If one of your weapon attacks Downs a creature, you gain a +2 bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 2, Might 2, Acrobatics 2

**Steel Wind (Level 3) [Stance]**

The experienced two-weapon fighter learns to fight in a constant state of fluidity, brushing aside attacks and delivering counterattacks in their place.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: When you Parry an attack, as a Counter action you may make an attack against the attacker using a weapon you did not Parry the attack with. If this attack hits you may Regain Balance.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Martial Arts, or Melee) 4, Acrobatics 3

**Swashbuckler's Focus (Level 3) [Stance]**

Versed in the art of singling an opponent out and reading their every move, when you are going toe-to-toe with a single foe you are at your peak... assuming there isn't anyone else trying to bash your head in at the same time.

**Effects**: When you enter this Stance, you focus on a hostile creature. You gain a +1 bonus to Dodge and Parry Defense against attacks from that creature but suffer a -1 penalty to Dodge and Parry Defense against all other attacks.

**Prereqs**: (Insight or Awareness) 4, (Acrobatics or Martial Arts) 3

**Earthen Mantle Approach (Level 4) [Stance]**

Solidly planting your feet on the ground, specifically guarding more delicate areas of your body, and applying a number of other small techniques allows you to better stand against bursts of fire, howling blizzards, and other phenomena.

**Effects**: You gain a +2 bonus to Fire, Frost, and Shadow Soak. You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: Athletics 5, Resistance 4

**Iron Vanguard (Level 4) [Stance]**

Adopting a firmly-rooted position and only partially committing to your attacks, you deflect any blows aimed at you or your comrades.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield, and you have not made any weapon attacks this turn. Leave if you are not wearing a shield.

**Effects**: You and all adjacent allies gain a +2 bonus to Parry Defense. You have Bad Luck on weapon attacks.

**Prereqs**: Might 4, Insight 3

**Storm of Blades (Level 4) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding. Leave if you do not meet this condition.

**Effects**: As a Move you may make a weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Martial Arts, or Melee) 5, Acrobatics 4

## Stances
*Source: `stances_1.html` (Part 1)*

Stances

**Lawman's Hand (Level 1) [Stance]**

Bounty hunters, local guards, and spies alike all share the need to bring a target down without killing them. Whether to be delivered in front of some authority or to question them at length, a number of methods exist for taking the fight out of someone without taking their life as well.

**Effects**: You may not gamble on weapon attacks. If one of your attacks would cause a living creature to become Downed or die, instead they are just knocked unconscious for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2, Insight 1

**Breaching Blows (Level 2) [Stance]**

You leverage your large weapon against your foes, swinging with enough force that they find it difficult to avoid injury entirely.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: If you make a weapon attack with a two-handed close-range weapon, you Gamble at least once on the attack, and a creature Parries that attack, they gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Doubleshot (Level 2) [Stance]**

Through much practice you have learned how to handle multiple projectiles at once, throwing handfuls of daggers, firing multiple arrows, and single-handedly pinning foes down with your volleys.

**Condition**: You are wielding a ranged weapon.

**Effects**: Whenever you would make a weapon attack with a ranged weapon, instead you make two of those attacks. If you do, you have Bad Luck on those attacks.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Archery) 3

**Earthen Fist Posture (Level 2) [Stance]**

By standing firmly and leveraging your strength to deliver strikes with as much force as possible, you trade hand-to-hand defense for oppressive offense.

**Effects**: Whenever a creature Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Pushed back 1 meter. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defenses.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Flurry of Strikes (Level 2) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Effects**: If a creature Dodges, Parries, or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Harried once.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Acrobatics 2

**Great Old Oak Style - Branches in the Wind (Level 2) [Stance]**

The tree that bends in the wind can withstand a storm, and no mere mortal can defy this rule. By drifting with the force of an enemy's missiles you too can withstand the onslaught of your foes.

**Condition**: You are unarmed.

**Effects**: You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (a ranged attack is declared against your Parry Defense), and if you do then after the attack flip is made you automatically Parry that attack. You may not Parry attacks made by adjacent creatures or with close-range weapons.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 3, Athletics 1, Meditation 1

**Group Bulwark (Level 2) [Stance]**

You extend your shield out, sacrificing your own defenses to provide cover for one of your friends-in-arms.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: When you enter this Stance and at the start of your turn, you focus on an ally and choose a shield you are wielding. While you are adjacent to that ally, they gain a + [chosen shield's Defense] bonus to Parry Defense and you may not Parry with the chosen shield.

Prereqs: Melee 3, Athletics 2

**Half-Guard (Level 2) [Stance]**

You shift your concentration towards your own defense, strengthening your guard.

**Effects**: You gain a +1 bonus to Parry Defense. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 3, Insight 2

**Oppressive Presence (Level 2) [Stance]**

On the battlefield you cast a long shadow, and no matter the weapons you employ your opponents find it difficult to maneuver around you.

**Effects**: Your enemies consider any space within 2 meters of you to have 1 level of Difficult Terrain. Your Speed is reduced by 1.

**Prereqs**: Presence 3, Awareness 2

**Overchanneling (Level 2) [Stance]**

Many styles of warmage training focus on overwhelming power and raw eldritch offense over all else, and each of those that do have built their practices with this stance as a foundation. With a series of motions that leverage your entire body to gather magical energy and intricate double-handed gestures to keep control over it, you wring every last measure of potency from your incantations.

**Effects**: Your damaging spell attacks deal 1 more damage. You may not Parry attacks.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 3, Might 2

**Swashbuckler's Rhythm (Level 2) [Stance]**

As a practitioner of the duelist's arts you are constantly mindful of both your enemies' and your own movements, and as you flow across a fight you disrupt the cadence of your foes.

**Condition**: You are Fencing.

**Effects**: When you hit a creature with a weapon attack, you gain a +1 bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round. You may only benefit from one bonus for each creature you hit, and if hitting a creature would grant you the bonus again then the newer bonus replaces the older bonus.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 3, Insight 2

**Aura of Death (Level 3) [Stance]**

You emanate a palpable atmosphere of death, decay, and mortality. The longer your enemies fight against you, the more inevitable their demise feels.

**Effects**: At the start of your turn, you and each enemy within [half your Presence Skill Total] meters of you gains a stack of Necrotic which lasts for the scene.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism, Meditation, or Theurgy) 4, Presence 3

**Concussive Swings (Level 3) [Stance]**

Aiming your attacks at limbs and joints rather than vitals, you cause the force of your attacks to unbalance your enemies rather than wound them.

**Effects**: If one of your weapon attacks would cause a creature to lose Health, instead that creature is Harried [twice the Health they would have lost] times.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 4, Might 3

**Crashing Wave Approach (Level 3) [Stance]**

Driving forth with your shield, you descend on your foes like a rushing river of iron.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: Once per turn you may spend 1 AP to make a weapon attack with a shield. If the attack hits or is Parried, the target is Pushed back 1 meter.

**Prereqs**: Melee 4, Athletics 3

**Distancing Draw (Level 3) [Stance]**

Well-trained in launching attacks under duress, you time your steps in careful coordination with your shots so that you create small openings to escape your foes.

**Condition**: You are wielding a ranged weapon.

**Effects**: When you make an attack with a ranged weapon, you may Shift 1 meter. Your Speed is reduced by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Archery) 4

**Immaculate Precision (Level 3) [Stance]**

In the heat of combat your focused fighting style means that every movement you make is perfectly deliberate, landing each stab and strike with the accuracy of an artist painting a masterpiece.

**Condition**: You are Fencing.

**Effects**: Each time you Gamble on a weapon attack it does not increase the attack's damage, and instead if the attack was made as part of a Technique or other ability that ability's Power is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 4, Insight 3

**One-Man Phalanx (Level 3) [Stance]**

Widening your guard to better encompass all of your enemies, although you cannot block weapons at quite the proper angle you stand in such a way that you can knock aside blows without losing your balance.

**Effects**: You ignore any stacks of Harried you have. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 4, Awareness 3

**Reaping Dervish (Level 3) [Stance]**

A whirlwind of steel on the field of battle, while this aggressive posture leaves you vulnerable to attack at points it also leads into flowing movements that deflect all oncoming attacks.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: When one of your weapon attacks with a two-handed close-range weapon Downs a creature, you gain a +2 bonus to Parry Defense for 1 round.

Prereqs: (Brawl or Melee) 4, Might 3

**Sentinel (Level 3) [Stance]**

You focus on enemies around you, matching their every step and following their movements carefully to keep them under control.

**Effects**: Whenever an enemy adjacent to you moves or Shifts, you may Shift into the space they moved from and follow them in this manner as long as they move or Shift. Your Speed is reduced to 0.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 4, Acrobatics 3

**Snake Style - Infinite Coiling (Level 3) [Stance]**

As you coil around your foes, slithering and striking, they unwittingly find themselves trapped as surely as a mouse caught by a python.

**Condition**: You are unarmed.

**Effects**: If a creature Parries or is hit by an attack with your Unarmed weapon, that creature is Slowed once.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 4, Acrobatics 3

**Steel Wind (Level 3) [Stance]**

The experienced two-weapon fighter learns to fight in a constant state of fluidity, brushing aside attacks and delivering counterattacks in their place.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Effects**: You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (after you Parry an attack) to make a weapon attack against the attacker using a weapon you did not Parry the attack with. If this attack hits, remove all stacks of Harried you have.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 4, Acrobatics 3

**Sudden Abjuration (Level 3) [General Spell] [Stance]**

Talented and practiced in the manipulation of energy, by channeling magical power in a certain style you can quickly convert a great deal of it into an extra layer of defense.

**Effects**: When you are hit by an attack you may expend the use of any number of Spell Encounter Techniques, whose combined levels cannot be more than [half your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] . You then gain temporary Health equal to the total levels of the expended spells, which lasts until after the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 4, Insight 3

**Bear Style - Furious Swipes (Level 4) [Stance]**

In an unorthodox method of unarmed fighting you focus the force of your blows in furious, crossing lines of aggression, leaving your prey unable to fully defend themselves against you.

**Condition**: You are unarmed.

**Effects**: If a creature Parries or is hit by an attack with your Unarmed weapon, they gain a stack of Bleeding. You may not Parry with your Unarmed weapon.

**Prereqs**: Brawl 5, Might 4

**Deadeye's Shadow (Level 4) [Stance]**

When you line up your shots, your enemies take notice. Each arrow and dagger rings out with a single message: ignore me at your peril.

**Condition**: You are wielding a ranged weapon.

**Effects**: If a creature Parries or is hit by one of your attacks with a ranged weapon, that creature is Taunted by you for 1 round. If you would take an action that includes making an attack with a ranged weapon that action takes an extra 1 AP.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Archery) 5

**Earthen Mantle Approach (Level 4) [Stance]**

Solidly planting your feet on the ground, specifically guarding more delicate areas of your body, and applying a number of other small techniques allows you to better stand against bursts of fire, howling blizzards, and other phenomena.

**Effects**: You gain a +1 bonus to Fire, Frost, Brilliant, and Shadow Resist. You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 5, Might 4

**Eternal Riposte (Level 4) [Stance]**

"Our pirates boarded the ship to find the crew hiding belowdecks, and a single woman standing before us. She drew a solitary blade, a real modest affair, and simply said 'I always follow when dancing'. We thought she was trying to throw us so we just surrounded her and charged with our weapons, but she swept aside each attack and every poor bastard that took a swing at her got cut in return. By the end of it she was standing perfectly still, and we looked like we had all fallen into a pile of razors."

**Condition**: You are Fencing.

**Effects**: When you Parry an attack, if the attacker is within range of one of your close-range weapons you may make an attack against them with that weapon. If the attack hits it deals no damage, and instead the target gains a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 5, Insight 4

**Follow Through (Level 4) [Stance]**

When your blows fell an enemy you can carry the force from the attack forward, striking another foe down.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: When one of your weapon attacks with a two-handed close-range weapon hits or is Dodged by a creature, until the end of the turn you may spend 1 AP to make an attack with that weapon against another creature that is adjacent to the initial target and who you have not attacked this way during this turn.

Prereqs: (Brawl or Melee) 5, Might 4

**Iron Vanguard (Level 4) [Stance]**

Adopting a firmly-rooted position, you leverage your shield to deflect any attacks made against you.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (you are targeted by an attack against your Parry Defense) to give the attacker Bad Luck on that attack.

**Prereqs**: Melee 5, Athletics 4

**Raptor's Precision (Level 4) [Stance]**

By taking a moment to adjust your grip, technique, aim, and many other factors, you can deliver attacks that are much more precise and harder to guard against.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on weapon attacks. If you would take an action that includes making a weapon attack, that action takes an extra 1 AP.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Athletics 2, Academics 2

**Shifting Ground (Level 4) [Stance]**

Light on your feet and keeping constant momentum, you dance away from your enemies' blows and keep yourself from being pinned down in combat.

**Effects**: Whenever you Parry or Dodge an attack, or whenever you make a weapon or spell attack, you may Shift 1 meter. Your Speed is halved.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 5

**Spell-Rending Arcanology (Level 4) [General Spell] [Stance]**

Practiced mostly by the sort of arcanist who went to a prestigious wizarding school and was top of his class and made sure everyone knew it, sufficient knowledge of the magical arts allows one to recognize the threads of magic another spellcaster is weaving and sunder them by use of overwhelming magical force, dissipating the incantation entirely.

**Effects**: When a creature within [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters begins casting a Spell, you can tell what level of Spell it is.

You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (a creature within [your Elementalism or Theurgy Skill Total] meters begins casting a Spell) and expend the use of a Spell Encounter Technique of a higher level than the creature's Spell. Then you make an Elementalism or Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of that creature, and if the attack hits the target's Spell is Countered. If the target's Spell required Focus to cast then you must spend an equal amount of Focus, otherwise the Spell is not Countered.

**Prereqs**: (Elementalism or Theurgy) 5, Academics 4

**Storm of Blades (Level 4) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Effects**: If you have made a weapon attack this turn, you may spend 1 AP and make a weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Acrobatics 4

---

## Strong
*Source: `strong.html` (Part 2)*

Strong

Strong includes both Body and Agility abilities, as well as a handful of those related to the Meditation Skill. Abilities that represent strong physical prowess, and all that accompanies that, can be found here. For some limited aspects, this can include mental strength as well; usually Integrity-related. Strong abilities feel reliable and solid, easily forming the backbone of a character's power.

Strong Technique Categories

---

## Tactical
*Source: `tactical.html` (Part 1)*

Tactical covers most attack, dodge, and parry related uses of Acrobatics, Archery, Melee, and Brawl. Depending on the fighting style it can cover many different tactics, but always with a deliberate calculation of offense versus defense, power versus precision, with a default emphasis on being well-rounded. Tactical abilities will generally make characters feel as though they are outmaneuvering their opponents, making a tactical choice at each step.

Martial Strikes

Martial Strikes are customized weapon maneuvers you can tailor to your specific fighting style. They come in a range of Levels, with a variety of features to specialize them.

See details on the Martial Strikes section.

**Counterstrike (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You are a whirlwind of action on the battlefield, and no sooner do you avoid one attack than you launch your own.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (after you Dodge or Parry an attack).

**Target**: The creature who attacked you.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against the target. You may use a Technique instead, as long as the Technique has you make a weapon attack, and takes no more than 2 AP to use.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 2, Insight 1

**Feint (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You quickly fake an attack against a foe, throwing them off-balance.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within range of your chosen weapon.

**Effects**: Make an attack with your chosen weapon against the target's Mental Defense, and if you flip a Diamond and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If the attack hits, it applies none of its normal effects, but the target is Harried once plus an additional time for each Extra Success.

**Prereqs**: Insight 2, (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 1

**Flashing Steel (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Stepping into a mixed posture momentarily, you proactively counter your foe's assault.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack is made against your Parry Defense, after the attack flip but before it hits or misses you).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack flip, but instead of a normal attack just note the result. Your Parry Defense against the attack becomes the result instead, and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Parry Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 2, Insight 1

**Lawman's Hand (Level 1) [Stance]**

Bounty hunters, local guards, and spies alike all share the need to bring a target down without killing them. Whether to be delivered in front of some authority or to question them at length, a number of methods exist for taking the fight out of someone without taking their life as well.

**Effects**: You may not Gamble on weapon attacks. If one of your attacks would cause a living creature to become Downed or die, instead they are just knocked unconscious for the scene.

**Prereqs**: Medicine 2, Insight 1

**Lucky Dodge (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You twist out of the path of an attack at the last second, though you probably burn through a bit of your luck in the process.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (an attack is made against your Dodge Defense, after the attack flip but before it hits or misses you).

**Cost**: Discard a card.

**Effects**: You gain a +2 bonus to your Dodge Defense against the attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 2, Insight 1

**Magehunter (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Through multi-disciplinary training in magical practice, you have learned when spellcasters leave gaps in their defenses and how to exploit those weaknesses.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a creature within your weapon's range begins casting a Spell).

**Target**: The creature trying to cast a Spell.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against the target. You may use a Technique instead, as long as the Technique has you make a weapon attack, and takes no more than 2 AP to use.

**Prereqs**: Academics 2, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 1

**Parting Shot (Level 1) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By keeping a keen eye on the flow of battle, you can punish enemies who try to disengage from you.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a creature within range of a close-range weapon you are wielding would move or be Pushed to a space outside of your range, but before they actually move).

**Target**: The creature trying to move or which is being Pushed.

**Effects**: Make an attack with a close-range weapon against the target. You may use a Technique instead, as long as the Technique has you make a weapon attack with a close-range weapon, and takes no more than 2 AP to use.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Insight) 3

**Adaptive Style (Level 2) [Martial]**

Practicing each maneuver tirelessly and endlessly studying how they flow seamlessly into another, the expert fighter can adapt to almost any situation in a heartbeat and abandon strategies in favor of seizing opportunity when it arises.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: Expend the use of any number of Martial Encounter Techniques.

**Effects**: You regain the use of one Martial Encounter Technique. That Technique's Level cannot be greater than half of the total Levels of Techniques you expended as a cost for this.

**Prereqs**: Academics 3

**Battering Blows (Level 2) [Stance]**

You leverage your large weapon against your foes, swinging with enough force that they find it difficult to avoid injury entirely.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: If a creature Parries one of your two-handed close-range weapon attacks, and you Gambled at least once on the attack, they gain a stack of Bleeding.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Disciple of the Flowing Hand (Level 2)**

You have trained in the style of unarmored fighting, granting you increased mobility as you flow like water around your weighed-down opponents.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: While you are wearing no armor, you gain a +1 bonus to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 3

**Duelist's Dance (Level 2) [Stance]**

Well-trained in launching attacks in perfect rhythm, you time your steps in careful coordination so that you create small openings to escape your foes' clumsy strikes.

**Condition**: One of your hands is empty.

**Effects**: When you declare a weapon attack, either before or after the attack is resolved, you may Shift 1 meter.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Insight 2

**Eagle Eyes (Level 2) [Stance]**

Shifting your focus away from your immediate surroundings, you maintain your accuracy even at long range.

**Effects**: You don't have Bad Luck on attacks with a ranged weapon against targets past its range increment. Your Speed is reduced by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Archery) 3

**Evasive Drift (Level 2) [Martial]**

Concentrating your reflexes for a brief moment, you slide away from an attack as it connects to turn an otherwise lethal strike into a grazing blow.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (an attack against your Dodge or Parry Defense hits you, before its effects are resolved).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**:  Make an Acrobatics flip against a difficulty of 9, and if you flip a Spade and succeed you gain an Extra Success. If you succeed you reduce the Health you would lose from the attack by 1, and for each Extra Success you reduce that Health by an additional 1.

**Prereqs**: Acrobatics 3, Insight 2

**Flowing Iron (Level 2) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are wielding two weapons, neither of which is a shield.

**Effects**: If a creature Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Harried once.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Acrobatics 2

**Lend Bulwark (Level 2) [Stance]**

You extend your shield out, sacrificing your own defenses to provide cover for one of your friends-in-arms.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: When you enter this Stance and at the start of your turn, you focus on an ally and choose a shield you are wielding. While you are adjacent to that ally, they gain a + [chosen shield's Defense] bonus to Parry Defense and you may not Parry with the chosen shield.

Prereqs: Melee 3, Athletics 2

**Oppressive Presence (Level 2) [Stance]**

On the battlefield you cast a long shadow, and no matter the weapons you employ your opponents find it difficult to maneuver around you.

**Effects**: Your enemies consider any space within 2 meters of you to have 1 level of Difficult Terrain. Your Speed is reduced by 1.

**Prereqs**: Presence 3, Awareness 2

**Perfect Strike (Level 2) [Martial]**

With a moment of brief concentration, you line up a brilliant attack.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you declare a weapon attack).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You gain Good Luck three times on the weapon attack.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Integrity 1, Meditation 1

**Stone Fist Posture (Level 2) [Stance]**

By standing firmly and leveraging your strength to deliver strikes with as much force as possible, you trade hand-to-hand defense for oppressive offense.

**Effects**: Whenever a creature Parries or is hit by one of your weapon attacks, they are Pushed back 1 meter. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defenses.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 3, Might 2

**Follow Through (Level 3) [Stance]**

When your blows fell an enemy you can carry the force from the attack forward, striking another foe down.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: When one of your weapon attacks with a two-handed close-range weapon hits or is Dodged by a creature, you may immediately spend 1 AP to make an attack with that weapon against another creature that is adjacent to the initial target.

Prereqs: (Brawl or Melee) 4, Might 3

**Iron Vanguard (Level 3) [Stance]**

Adopting a firmly-rooted position, you leverage your shield to deflect any attacks made against you and foil your would-be attacker.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: You may spend 1 AP as an Interrupt (a creature adjacent to you declares an attack against your Parry Defense) to give the attacker Bad Luck on that attack.

**Prereqs**: Melee 4, Athletics 3

**One-Man Phalanx (Level 3) [Stance]**

Widening your guard to better encompass all of your enemies, although you cannot block weapons at quite the proper angle you stand in such a way that you can knock aside blows without losing your balance.

**Effects**: You ignore any stacks of Harried you have. You suffer a -1 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics or Brawl) 4, Awareness 3

**Seize the Moment (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

By constantly monitoring the flow of battle, you are capable of holding your attack for a moment to wait for a perfect opportunity to capitalize on.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (the turn before yours ends, before yours would begin).

**Effects**: Adjust your position in turn order to one of your choice.

**Prereqs**:  (Awareness or Insight) 4

**Sentinel (Level 3) [Stance]**

You focus on enemies around you, matching their every step and following their movements carefully to keep them under control.

**Effects**: Whenever an enemy adjacent to you moves or Shifts, you may Shift into the space they moved from and follow them in this manner as long as they move or Shift. Your Speed is reduced to 0.

**Prereqs**: Awareness 4, Acrobatics 3

**Steel Wind (Level 3) [Stance]**

The experienced two-weapon fighter learns to fight in a constant state of fluidity, simultaneously brushing aside attacks from all angles and lashing out in return.

**Condition**: You are wielding two weapons, neither of which is a shield.

**Effects**: When you Parry an attack from a close-range weapon or an attacker who is adjacent to you, the attacker is Crippled once.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 4, Acrobatics 3

**Swashbuckler's Speed (Level 3) [Stance]**

Your intense focus lets you speed up more and more as a fight goes on, each step taking advantage of motions you made moments before and catapulting you at your foes in a blur of steel.

**Condition**: One of your hands is empty.

**Effects**: At the start of your turn, choose an enemy adjacent to you. That enemy then gains a stack of Harried for each time you have attacked that enemy since the start of your last turn.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 4, Insight 3

**Adrenaline High (Level 4) [Stance]**

Pushing your strength to its limit while hefting your mighty weapon, by pushing that energy inward you revitalize and rejuvenate yourself in the midst of a frenzied fracas.

**Condition**: You are wielding a two-handed close-range weapon.

**Effects**: When you Gamble on an attack with a two-handed close-range weapon, it deals no extra damage. Instead, if it hits, you gain

1 + [times Gambled]

stacks of Protected.

Prereqs: (Brawl or Melee) 5, Might 4

**Cleave Magic (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

Your education in intermediate magical theory has progressed to the point where you are capable of seeing threads of magic being pulled together and sent towards you, and by launching a counterattack at the focal point of the spell you can disperse the energies in a flash of violence.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (a spell attack is declared against you, but no other targets).

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack, the Defense the spell attack targets becomes the result instead, and you ignore any stacks of Harried for your Dodge or Parry Defense against the attack. If the spell attack misses then it instead targets your attacker, is treated as having a flip result of the weapon attack you made, and is resolved accordingly.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Academics 2, (Elementalism or Theurgy) 2

**Cloak and Dagger (Level 4) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"Lord Highcastle! How did you manage to convince the boss of that criminal gang to stop interfering in our trade routes?"

"Well you see, my dear chamberlain, I agreed to meet the lady in her favorite ale house, and when I sat down at the table I projected an aura of firm confidence, never broke eye contact, and greeted her with a cordial yet strong voice. Then, when she was too busy trying to take the measure of my character, I stabbed her in the heart, arranged her cloak so it looked like she was simply napping on the table, and casually strolled out of the establishment."

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you declare a weapon attack).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Make a Stealth attack against the Mental Defense of each target of the weapon attack. Any targets who lose the flip cannot Dodge or Parry the attack.

**Prereqs**: Stealth 5

**Crashing Wave (Level 4) [Stance]**

When foes open themselves up to attack you you counterattack by driving forth with your shield, descending on them like a rushing river of iron.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Effects**: When you Parry an attack from a close-range weapon or an attacker who is adjacent to you, the attacker is Harried once.

**Prereqs**: Melee 5, Athletics 4

**Earthen Mantle Approach (Level 4) [Stance]**

Solidly planting your feet on the ground, specifically guarding more delicate areas of your body, and applying a number of other small techniques allows you to better stand against bursts of fire, howling blizzards, and other phenomena.

**Effects**: You gain a +1 bonus to Fire, Frost, Brilliant, and Shadow Resist. You suffer a -2 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense.

**Prereqs**: Resilience 5, Might 4

**Eternal Riposte (Level 4) [Stance]**

"Our pirates boarded the ship to find the crew hiding belowdecks, and a single woman standing before us. She drew a solitary blade, a real modest affair, and simply said 'I always follow when dancing'. We thought she was trying to throw us so we just surrounded her and charged with our weapons, but she swept aside each attack and every poor bastard that took a swing at her got cut in return. By the end of it she was standing perfectly still, and we looked like we had all fallen into a pile of razors."

**Condition**: One of your hands is empty.

**Effects**: For 1 AP as an Interrupt (you Parry an attack) you may make a weapon attack against the attacker. You may use a Technique instead of the attack, as long as the Technique has you make a weapon attack, and takes no more than 2 AP to use.

**Prereqs**: (Brawl or Melee) 5, Insight 4

**Raptor's Precision (Level 4) [Stance]**

By taking a moment to adjust your grip, technique, aim, and many other factors, you can deliver attacks that are much more precise and harder to guard against.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on weapon attacks. If you would take an action that includes making a weapon attack, that action takes an extra 1 AP.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Athletics 2, Academics 2

**Storm of Blades (Level 4) [Stance]**

As a fighter perfects the art of two-weapon fighting his style takes on a certain beauty. His every step carries one attack into another, his blades dance about in a mesmerizing rhythm, and anyone who stands still to watch is sure to be cut to ribbons.

**Condition**: You are wielding two weapons, neither of which is a shield.

**Effects**: For 1 AP - Interrupt (after you make an attack with one of your weapons), you may make an attack with the other weapon.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Brawl, or Melee) 5, Acrobatics 4

**Final Feint Strikes the Heart (Level 5) [Martial] [Encounter]**

"He was known as a peerless warrior in his homeland, rumored to have never lost a single fight and never failed to slay a single enemy. They said that even in encounters where the man traded blows evenly with his opponents, or even went on the defensive and appeared to be at a disadvantage, regardless of the circumstances he would lash out with a terrible blow of his spear and skewer the hearts of his foes, none of whom could defend against his fury."

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Target**: A creature within range of your weapon.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack against the target's Dodge Defense, and you may treat any card flipped for this attack as a Heart. If the weapon attack hits, for each Extra Success on the attack flip the attack has its damage increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, (Acrobatics or Might) 5, (Awareness or Insight) 5

**Inexhaustible Arms (Level 5) [Martial]**

"As the battle drew to a close, we surveyed the field with the commander. We knew our victory had come at a steep cost, but we expected to see one of our battalions securing a new field camp. Instead, we saw that battalion surrounding a single fighter, calmly dancing between several of our soldiers. As each soldier approached the fighter she would cleave them through with a sweep of a great polearm, each strike quick but precise, weaving between the soldiers' guards to strike a killing blow. Before we knew it, the advisors hurried the commander away, our victory turned to a rout.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Effects**: Make a weapon attack with Good Luck against the target.

**Prereqs**: (Acrobatics, Archery, Brawl, or Melee) 5, (Resilience or Integrity) 5, (Body or Instinct) 5

---

## Techniques
*Source: `techniques.html` (Part 2)*

Techniques are special abilities that let you do great and wondrous things, from unleashing powerful magics to flying into a blood rage. Techniques are grouped into general categories on separate pages for ease of sorting through them, but these groupings are not any kind of limitation. Just because Elemental Strike is listed as a martial Technique (and generally designed for use in combat) doesn't mean you couldn't use it outside of a fight to start a campfire, and similarly there are many creative uses for Techniques!

Encounter Techniques (noted with the [Encounter] tag) are only usable once per encounter. Once you have exhausted the use of an Encounter Technique you may not use it until you have regained the use of it, which happens at the end of the current scene.

Unless a Technique says otherwise you may not learn it more than once. Encounter Techniques are exempt from this however; for each additional time you learn a particular Encounter Technique you gain an extra use of it per encounter.

Using a Technique is relatively obvious to any onlookers and, while only experienced and knowledgeable individuals will be able to identify a Technique by sight, most people will at least notice that you are employing some kind of specific ability. Some Techniques are exempt from this and will be labelled with the [Subtle] tag - those Techniques do not involve any motions, incantations, or anything else that would show onlookers that you are using a Technique, except for any obvious results of using the Technique.

Each Technique has a

name, tags, description, mechanics,

and

prerequisites

.

A Technique's name is just that, and has no special impact on how the Technique works.

A Technique's tags are all of the terms after its name, such as the Technique's Level (1-5), if it's a Spell, a Martial Technique, and so on. These are important for a variety of purposes - the Level determines the Technique's Experience cost, and some Techniques and other abilities have special interactions with Techniques that have certain tags.

A Technique's description is simply a piece of flavor that gives you an idea of how the Technique "works", how it might be interpreted in regards to your character and story. The description doesn't affect how the Technique functions, but it will usually represent aspects of that Technique. It's just a description; no more, no less.

A Technique's mechanics are the parts that explain what the Technique actually does. This includes effects such as if the Technique is "passive" and applies at all times or perhaps what action it takes to use the Technique, who and what the Technique can affect, if the Technique calls for you to make an attack, and a wide range of other possible effects. Read these carefully, as they will lay out exactly what the Technique does.

A Technique's prerequisites list certain requirements you must meet in order to learn the Technique. When these list a certain Skill or Stat at a certain rank you must have at least that many ranks in that Skill or Stat to learn the Technique. If the prerequisites list a specific requirement, such as knowing another Technique, you must also meet that requirement to learn the Technique. If a Technique has prerequisites that include multiple Stats or Skills in parentheses, like "(Acrobatics, Melee, or Perform) 5", this means you must have sufficient points in at least one of the listed Stats or Skills to qualify for the Technique.

---

## Techniques
*Source: `techniques_2.html` (Part 1)*

"Techniques" are special maneuvers and qualities that characters can learn which give them unique powers. These cover a broad range from special weapon attacks to magical spells to the ability to undermine an opponent's poise and composure in the middle of a political debate. As a character grows in experience and improves these they learn more impressive applications of their greatest skills, and with them can stand alone against entire armies, bring wounded allies back from the brink of death with powerful magic, or pull the strings of entire cities through knowledge and favors.

Techniques are broken into 3 groups - Strong, Savvy, and Spells - which are each then divided into 3 smaller groups. Anyone can learn any Technique, so long as they meet the prerequisites, these groups simply help organize them.

---

## the fightspace
*Source: `the-fightspace.html` (Part 2)*

the fightspace

INITIATIVE STUFF

TAUNT / FRIGHTEN STUFF

DEFENSIVE OPTIONS

temp hp boys

dodge boys

SCREAMING OPTIONS

**Riposte**: You may use the Technique as a Counter action when you Parry an attack. If you do, the attack must target the creature that made the Parried attack. You may only do so while wielding a one-handed, close-range weapon and no other weapons, and the attack must use that weapon.

**Prereqs**: Insight 2

**T (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

W

**Condition**: You are dual-wielding.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of both of your weapons.

**Effects**: Make an attack with one of your weapons, then make an attack with your other weapon.

**Prereqs**: Melee 3, Acrobatics 2

**Hammer and Anvil (Level 2) [Martial] [Encounter]**

You stagger an enemy with your shield, driving them back and advancing while creating an opening for an ally to strike.

**Condition**: You are wielding a shield.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature in range of your shield.

**Effects**: Make an attack with your shield against the target. If the target Parries or is hit by the attack, they are Pushed back 3 meters and you Shift forward 3 meters after them. If the target is Pushed this way you may choose an ally; that ally may make a weapon attack against the target as a Counter action.

**Prereqs**: Melee 3, Athletics 2

**Deadeye Shot (Level 3) [Martial] [Encounter]**

It is said by some that true mastery over archery is attained when one perfectly understands the gentle turning of the wind. Archers of this caliber are known to fire through forest thickets, striking a target a mile away directly through the eye.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature you can see.

**Effects**: Choose arm, leg, or head, then make an attack with a ranged weapon against the target; for this attack your weapon is considered to have unlimited range, as long as you can see your target. If the attack hits the target suffers a debuff based on the limb you chose: if you chose arm the target is Crippled 3 times for the scene, if you chose leg the target is Slowed 6 times for the scene, and if you chose head the target is Enervated 3 times for the scene.

Prereqs: (Acrobatics or Archery) 5

**Deadeye Shot (Level 3) [Encounter] [Martial, Style]**

It is said by some that true mastery over archery is attained when one perfectly understands the gentle turning of the wind. Archers of this caliber are known to fire through forest thickets, striking a target a mile away directly through the eye.

Action: This Technique is used as an Action while wielding a ranged weapon.

Target: A creature that you can see.

Effects: Choose to aim at the target's arm, leg, or head, assuming the target possesses the given body part. Make an attack with a ranged weapon against the target, and for this attack your weapon is considered to have unlimited range. If you choose, you may be considered to have flipped a Heart for the attack, and if the attack hits the target suffers the following, based on body part struck:

Arm:  the arm cannot be used to wield anything for the scene, though it can still be moved with severe mobility limitations.

Leg:  the leg cannot bear any meaningful amount of weight, and the target is Snared for the scene.

Head:  the target receives a -4 penalty to Parry and Dodge Defense and has Disadvantage on Cunning flips for the scene.

Prereqs: (Acrobatics or Archery) 5

**Intervene (Level 3) [Encounter] [Martial, Style]**

A shield is truly effective when it protects your allies as well as yourself. To this end you are constantly ready to aid your friends, throwing yourself in harm's way for them and driving back any who would strike them down.

Action: This Technique is used as a Reaction to the target ally being targeted by an attack against Parry or Dodge Defense.

Target: An ally within [your Speed] in meters, and the attack against them.

Effects: You Shift up to [your Movement Speed] meters. If this places you adjacent to both the creature making the target attack and the target ally, or to the direct path the projectile or spell travels along between the creature making the target attack and the target ally, you become the target for the attack instead of your ally. You may not Dodge this attack, but for this attack you ignore all Onslaught penalties to your Defenses and you do not gain an Onslaught penalty for applying your Defense to the attack. If you Parry the attack, you may immediately make a melee attack against the attacker with the weapon you did not Parry with, if they are within its range.

Prereqs: (Martial Arts or Melee) 5, Awareness 3, Insight 2

**Obliterating Smash (Level 3) [Encounter] [Martial, Style]**

You heave the entirety of your being into a great swing, creating a concussive shockwave with your weapon that pulverizes anyone in front of you.

Action: This Technique is used as an Action while you are wielding a two-handed melee weapon.

Target: All creatures in a 3 meter-wide arc in front of you.

Effects: Make an attack with a 2-handed melee weapon against each target, gambling at least 1. If a target Parries this attack they take [your Strength] Physical damage which ignores Armor Soak, and if a target Dodges this attack they take [dice gambled] Physical damage which ignores Armor Soak.

Prereqs: Might 4, (Martial Arts or Melee) 5

**Parry Cover (Level 1) [Encounter] [Martial]**

You reach out and bat away an attack directed at an ally.

Action: This Technique is used as a Reaction to an adjacent ally being targeted by an attack that can be Parried, before the adjacent ally applies a Defense. To use this Technique, you must be adjacent to either the creature making the target attack or the direct path the projectile or spell travels along between the creature making the target attack and the ally.

Target: An adjacent ally and an attack that can be Parried targeting that ally.

Effects: The target ally does not need to apply a Defense against the attack, instead the attack is against your Parry Defense, and you suffer Onslaught penalties as though you were the target of the attack. If you Parry the attack, instead the attack targets you for any effects. If you do not Parry the attack, the target ally is hit by the attack.

Prereqs: (Martial Arts or Melee) 3, Cunning 2

**Shield Slam (Level 2) [Encounter] [Martial, Style]**

You throw your entire weight behind your shield, driving it into your opponent and concussing them.

Action: This Technique is used as an Action while wielding a shield.

Target: A creature in range of your shield.

Effects: Make a Melee attack with a shield. If the attack hits, the target is Stunned for 1 round.

Prereqs: Melee 4, Might 3
