## Theurgy Spells
*Source: `theurgy-spells.html` (Part 1)*

Theurgy Spells

**Alarm Ward (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

Tracing runes of warding and laying lines of salt, you can ward an area to alert you and your allies if outsiders try to sneak up to your location.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Target**: An area up to 50 meters in diameter.

**Effects**: The target area is warded for up to 12 hours, and when you create the ward you can choose any number of people that will not trigger the ward. When any other person enters the warded area, the alarm does any number of the following, chosen when you erect the ward:

Mentally warns you, rousing you from any sleep, but otherwise makes no noise

Mentally warns all people within the ward, rousing them from any sleep, but otherwise making no noise

Emits a loud chiming noise that can be clearly heard within the warded area but nowhere else

Emits a loud chiming noise that can be clearly heard for at least a mile

You may only have one ward at a given time.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Birdsight (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

You enchant a winged beast of nature to temporarily borrow its sight, scouting out the area from above.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: This Spell Summons a

Small Bird

or other small, flying animal to your side for up to one hour. You may only have one such animal Summoned at a time. If the bird is more than [Theurgy Skill Total] kilometers from you, the spell ends.

While Summoned, as an Extra action you may switch from seeing through your eyes to instead see from the eyes of the bird, using its relevant Skills while doing so. As an Extra action you may switch back. The spell also allows you to telepathically communicate with the bird, also allowing you to give it a turn at great distances.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2, Survival 1

**Learning**: You may learn this Technique multiple times. If you already know this Technique, learning it again increases the number of instances of the spell you may have active at any given time by 1.

**From the Brink of Death (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

With small threads of power you stitch together someone's wounds just enough to stabilize them.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals any Health they are missing from their Downed Health level.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Windweaving (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Channeling power out and into the air itself, you cause breezes and gusts to aid your allies in a variety of ways.

**Action**: Main, or Counter - the target begins falling.

**Target**: Yourself or an ally in range. If you spend 1 Soul as you cast this spell, you affect an extra [Theurgy Skill Total] targets.

**Effects**: If cast as a Main action, you create a sphere of clean, fresh air around the head of the target that lasts for the scene. This sphere is constantly full of fresh, uncontaminated, breathable air and will displace any liquids or gases around it, allowing the target to breathe easily in anything from a cloud of poison gas to the bottom of a river.

If cast as a Counter, you slow the target's descent until they land on solid ground, and they take no damage from the fall.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2,  Survival 1

**Beast Venom (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

An ethereal beast's fang strikes a foe within range, driving venom deep into their veins.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Resistance Defense. If the attack hits, the target is affected by a

Basic Poison

of your choice.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Door of Respite (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell]**

You create a "door" that acts as a portal to a portion of the extradimensional realms, giving you a small shelter in which to rest while safe from hazards.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Target**: An open space in front of you.

**Effects**: A "door" that is normally-sized for you and made from translucent light appears in front of you. This door is only visible to you, although you may "hold it open" for other people to allow them to see it and its doorway while held open. The door is a portal to an extradimensional space that resembles a warm cottage, large tent around a campfire, or whatever you would consider safe and comfortable shelter. The shelter has one entrance which leads back to wherever the door was created, and the only way in or out of the shelter is that door. After 8 hours the door closes, and anything inside the shelter is gently deposited on the ground around where the door was created.

Anyone who gets a full night's rest while inside the shelter heals an extra Health.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Fog Cloud (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

By commanding the rain and winds you can cause fog to suddenly roll in across nearby terrain.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A point within

[Theurgy Skill Total] × 10

meters.

**Effects**: Mists begin to swirl around the target point, and at the start of your next turn the mists form into a cloud of fog with a radius of

[Will] × 5

meters which lasts for the scene. Anyone trying to see something or make an attack either into the cloud or from within the cloud has Bad Luck on any related flips.

When you cast this Spell, choose a movement pattern for the cloud to follow:

The cloud stays still and does not move

The cloud moves at a slow pace (1 meter per round) in a direction

The cloud moves at a fast pace (5 meters per round) in a direction

The cloud moves at a fast pace (up to 5 meters per round) and attempts to stay centered on you

As a Move action you may change the movement for the cloud to any other pattern. Particularly strong winds or other forces may divert or disperse the fog cloud.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Gust of Wind (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You create a blast of hurricane-strength wind to scatter your enemies.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: All creatures within a 3-meter-long cone.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Dodge Defense of each target. Each target hit by the attack is Pushed back [your Will] meters, or [twice your Will] meters if you flip a Diamond on the attack. Each target hit must also make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty

7 + [your Theurgy Skill Total]

, or fall Prone.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Land's Bounty (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A staple of druids living in small towns and communes (which is a lot of them), this simple incantation allows a druid to stoke the energy of the land and cause it to bear fruit and other foods in a heartbeat. In a pinch, this can also cause enemy combatants to become bogged down in thick clumps of underbrush and shrubs, making them easy prey for you and your allies.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Up to [Theurgy Skill Total] spaces, each within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters; the same space may be chosen more than once.

**Effects**: All plant life in each target space grows as though for an entire season in the span of a round. Food grown this way will reflect the sorts of fruits and vegetables that are typical for the climate and area.

In most climates this will cause affected terrain to hamper movement, causing the target space to have 1 level of Difficult Terrain for each time it was targeted, and additional affects may apply.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Lifeline (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Extending your life force out to encompass a friend, you protect them from harm while taking the brunt of any attacks made against them.

**Action**:

Extra, or Counter - the target is hit by an attack.

**Target**: An ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target is linked to you until the end of the scene.

While the target is linked to you if they would lose Health instead you lose that much Health and they lose no Health.

The link ends if you become incapacitated or otherwise lose consciousness. You may end the link as a Non-action.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Might 1, Resistance 1

**Mark of Smiting (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You trace a glowing rune in the air and send its power into a comrade, causing their weapon to strike the heart of your foes.

**Action**: Extra.

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent ally.

**Effects**: You mark your target until the end of the scene. The next time the marked target hits with a weapon attack, the attack has up to [your Theurgy Skill Total] Physical damage converted into Spirit damage.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Might 2

**Ring of Entropy (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

By lacing the ground with glowing runes you create an energy vacuum, absorbing power and strength from anyone in the area.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A circular area 2 meters in radius, centered on a point within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You create a runed ring around the target area that lasts for the scene. If a creature starts their turn in, moves into, or is moved into the area, make a Theurgy spell attack against their Resistance Defense. If the attack hits that creature is Weakened once, which lasts until they start their turn outside the ring.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Resistance 1, Integrity 1

**Sculpt Stone (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You manipulate and shape the earth around you, allowing you to quickly break the earth to foil pursuit. Given time, however, you can work stone and dirt into sturdy walls and other formations with nothing other than your magic.

**Action**: Move or 1 minute

(see below)

.

**Target**: Up to [half your Theurgy Skill Total] spaces (square meters) within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters, each of which must be adjacent to dirt, stone, or some form of earth.

**Effects**: The target spaces each gain [half your Theurgy Skill Total] levels of Difficult Terrain.

You may instead spend 1 minute to cast this spell, and you may instead shape the earth into walls, pillars, sculptures, or anything else you can conceive of. Simple structures can be made without any special knowledge, but anything complex or detailed will be limited by your Craft Skill Total as though you were making it with Craft.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Commune With Nature (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell]**

Your connection to the earth is strong enough to open all of its secrets to you, divining many details about the surrounding landscape without such primitive methods as exploration.

**Action**: 1 hour; if you spend 1 Soul you may instead cast this as a Main action.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: Once you finish casting the spell make a Theurgy flip, and on a result of at least 9 you gain information about one of the following subjects:

Existence of water sources

General animal population and prominent species

Earth/terrain type

Any minerals in the earth

Types of vegetation

Presence of humanoids

Powerful unnatural creatures

General state of nature

For every 2 points your result is above 9, you may choose another subject on which to gain information. Any knowledge gained from this Spell applies to all nature within 15 kilometers of your location.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Curse of Mortality (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Summoning deadly magicks of entropy and weakness, you lay a curse on a creature that beckons impending doom.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target gains [Will] stacks of Necrotic which last for 3 rounds. If you flip a Diamond on the attack and it hits, the target loses any temporary Health they have.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Might 1, Resistance 1, Medicine 1

**Dust to Dust (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

With a wave of your arms you focus Nature's power on an area, returning the dead to the earth. However, Nature's idea of what "the dead" is might be more vague than most people's...

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: All dead organic matter in a 5 meter long cone.

**Effects**:  All targeted matter dissolves almost instantly, as though turned to dust and vanished in a gust of wind. Dead plants, fallen trees (that have been toppled long enough to be fully dead), and entire corpses (including bone) will all be disintegrated by this spell. This will affect any objects made out of "dead" wood, bone, leather, and the like, although most enchanted items are immune to the Spell's effects.

For creatures made of any relevant materials, such as a reanimated skeleton or a wooden golem, you must make a Theurgy spell attack against the creature's Dodge Defense. For attended objects, such as armor being worn or a weapon being wielded/carried, you must make a Theurgy spell attack against the Dodge Defense of the creature wearing or carrying the object. Any such targets struck by the attack are dealt

5 + [Will]

Physical damage which ignores Armor Soak.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Entangle (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause a teeming mass of vines and grass to burst from the ground, grasping at the feet of anyone who tries to cross the veritable storm of foliage.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A circular area 2

meters in radius, centered on a point within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You summon thrashing, grasping vines in the target area for the scene, during which time the area has 1 level of Difficult Terrain. Anyone who starts their turn in the area or enters the area must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty

7 + [your Theurgy Skill Total],

or be Snared for 1 round. If a creature succeeds on the flip during their turn, they cannot be Snared by the spell for the rest of their turn.

Note that casting this Spell inside of a building or on top of a floor not made of earth or dirt may result in substantial damage to the flooring.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Heat Metal (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

What is born of the earth is still subject to the earth's influence, and even though iron arms and armor are common among civilized people they can still be conduits for the earth's rage. Using this, you channel power into a work of metal and cause it to immediately heat up and sear anyone touching it.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A metal object, no larger than a large weapon or piece of armor, within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: If the target object is being worn or carried, make a Theurgy spell attack against the Dodge Defense of the creature wearing or carrying the object. If the target is an animate object, make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the target object is not attended or animate, it is considered to be hit automatically. If the attack hits, the object is heated up for 3 rounds. At the end of each of your turns, if the object is heated up then anyone in contact with it takes

1 + [Will]

Fire damage.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Plague Cloud (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

All living things come from the earth, and even though most people associate disease and plague with death they are still part of the natural cycle, and therefore at the command of certain earth magics.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A space within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: You create a visible miasma of a

disease

of your choice in the target space, lasting until the end of the scene.  Anyone who is in the cloud when it is created, starts their turn in the cloud, or enters the cloud is exposed to the chosen disease and must make a Resistance flip against the disease's initial difficulty or become infected with the disease. In addition, any objects in the cloud are exposed to the disease and may potentially carry it if there would normally be that risk, such as food stores or clothing. The disease otherwise works normally, spreading as usual from infected individuals.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Resistance 2, Survival 2

**Step Into the Earth (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

When you commune with the earth you can also become one with it, briefly melding your form into soil and stone.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Yourself and any equipment you are carrying.

**Effects**: You - and any equipment you may be carrying at the time - Shift 1 meter into an area of solid stone, dirt, or other earth; if you cannot, the spell fails.

While in the earth you may take no actions except moving, and your Speed is halved. You cannot see while in the earth although you have a general idea of your surroundings within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters, including terrain layout and any creatures, although you cannot glean any more detailed understanding while in the earth. Your sense of hearing is dulled, although if you are close enough to someone talking you will be able to make out their speech.

Any creatures within the area can make an Awareness flip, difficulty

7 + [your Theurgy Skill total]

, to hear, feel, or otherwise sense you tunneling through the earth. Only those with knowledge of Theurgy will know what the cause is, but others might still notice something going on.

The spell ends when you move out of earth back into an empty space or at the end of the scene, and when it ends you are displaced out of the earth into the nearest empty space.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Cloak of Awe (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Donning a mantle of flaring light over your shoulders, you stagger and shake your enemies.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Crippled [Will] times for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Intimidate 4

**Control Weather (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell]**

Speaking to the earth, wind, and sky, you can wield the power of the weather itself, bringing rain to a parched land or even calming a raging storm.

**Action**: 1 hour; if you spend 1 Soul you may instead cast this as a Main action.

**Target**: An area [Will] kilometers in radius, centered on you.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy flip to determine the potency of the weather change you can make in the target area, lasting for 1 hour. The difficulty of the Theurgy flip refers to the potency of the effect you are creating or ending:

**Minor effect**: A minor change in the weather that has a minor, if any, effect on people in the affected area. This includes such effects as creating a clear sky or causing a light rain in the area. Effects of this caliber have a difficulty of 9 to create or end.

**Moderate effect**: Either creating or ending a significant weather event. Effects of this caliber are heavy fogs that obscure sight, heavy downpours that make travel more difficult, and the like; they have a difficulty of 11 to create or end.

**Major effect**: A significant and destructive force of nature, in the ranks of storms which cause rivers to flood and hurricanes with gale-force winds. Effects of this caliber have a difficulty of 15 to create or end.

For each 2 by which your Theurgy result exceeds the difficulty you may choose one of the following:

Increase the area of effect by an additional [Will] kilometers

Increase the duration by an additional hour

At the end of the spell's effect the normal weather pattern resumes.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Survival 4

**Earthquake (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Reaching into the depths of the earth with your power, you agitate the ground into a state of turmoil and uproar, quaking powerfully in a concentrated area and bringing anything on the surface crashing down.

**Cost**: 1 Soul.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: An area centered on you, roughly

10 × [Theurgy Skill Total]

meters in radius.

**Effects**: An earthquake begins to affect the target area, lasting for the scene. While the effects do not extend beyond this area, anyone in the nearby area will be aware of the earthquake. All terrain affected by this Spell has 1 level of Difficult Terrain in addition to specific affects based on the features of the terrain listed below. Each creature that starts their turn in the area must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty 13, or fall Prone.

A cave, tunnel, cliff, building, or other structure or vertical formation is collapsed by the spell, with the exception of buildings that are particularly reinforced against earthquakes and other disasters. Caves will generally just collapse into themselves, where cliffs and structures that are particularly large will create a landslide of rubble that travels horizontally as far as it falls vertically. Any creature caught in the area of a collapse or landslide must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty 13, or take 8 Physical damage and be pinned beneath the rubble. A creature that succeeds on the Acrobatics flip takes 5 Physical damage and is on top of the rubble. Creatures who are on top of the structure when it collapses must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty 11, or take 5 Physical damage and become pinned beneath the rubble. Any creature pinned beneath rubble loses 1 Health per minute until freed. Any area with rubble in it has 1 level of Difficult Terrain.

Bodies of water are partially drained away, turning all but the largest lakes and oceans into muddy sinkholes for the duration of the Spell and giving them 1 level of Difficult Terrain. Each creature in the mud at the start of their turn must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty 13, or be Snared for 1 round. After the Spell ends the water will slowly fill back in over the course of several minutes.

Terrain in general will be roughly affected by the Spell aside from the explicit effects listed. Trees may very well be toppled, small hills leveled (even if they would not be considered cliffs, as above), and things like cobblestone roads will be thrown into disrepair almost instantly. All effects of the Spell may not happen instantaneously, and instead occur over the course of the spell's duration.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Survival 4

**Interdict (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Wrapping an enemy in strings of power, you tighten your magic around their heart, causing their entire body to seize up.

**Action**: Main.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Bodily Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Off-Balance and Snared for 2 rounds.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, (Might or Integrity) 4

**Wild Shape (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Your connection to nature is so strong that you may borrow the forms of wilderness animals, transforming into a wide variety of animals on a whim.

**Action**: Full.

**Target**: Yourself.

**Effects**: You transform into an animal, although you must be familiar with the species of animal. While transformed:

Your Strength, Dexterity, and Stamina become [half your Theurgy Skill Total] , although if you become an animal notably smaller than a humanoid instead these scores instead become 2.

You may use any natural abilities of your new form such, as water breathing or flight.

You keep access to any Skills or Techniques you know, but you may not cast any spells or effectively wield any weapons except for your Unarmed weapon.

This spell is committed as long as you stay transformed, and you must take a Full action to uncommit it and transform back into your natural form.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Survival 4

---

## Theurgy
*Source: `theurgy.html` (Part 2)*

Theurgy Spells invoke spirits and tap into the metaphysical weave of existence to perform wondrous miracles.

Blessing Magic

Blessing Magic are highly-flexible boons that you can bestow on yourself or your allies. They come in a range of Levels, with a variety of features to .

See details on the Blessing Magic section.

Healing Magic

Healing Magic are Spells that restore Health or otherwise help keep people alive, in a range of Levels with different features. All Healing Magic requires Focus to cast.

See details on the Healing Magic section.

**From the Brink of Death (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

With small threads of power you stitch together someone's wounds just enough to stabilize them.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals any Health they are missing from their Downed Health level.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Glyph of Respite (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell]**

You infuse a small area with a calming power that eases your fatigue and promotes a deep, restful sleep.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Cost**: Discard 1 card.

**Target**: A circular area, with a radius up to 10 meters.

**Effects**: You lay a faint glyph of soothing energy on the ground, which lasts for roughly 8 hours. Any allies who get a full night's rest while inside the affected area heal an extra Health.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Gust of Wind (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You create a blast of hurricane-strength wind to scatter your enemies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: All creatures within a 3-meter-long cone, starting from your space.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Dodge Defense of each target. Each target hit by the attack is Pushed back [Mind] meters, +1 meter if you flipped a Diamond on the spell attack.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Land's Bounty (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A staple of druids living in small towns and communes (which is a lot of them), this simple incantation allows a druid to stoke the energy of the land and cause it to bear fruit and other foods in a heartbeat. In a pinch, this can also cause enemy combatants to become bogged down in thick clumps of underbrush and shrubs, making them easy prey for you and your allies.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A circular area 2 meters in radius, centered on a space within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: All plant life in each target space grows as though for an entire season in the span of a round. Food grown this way will reflect the sorts of fruits and vegetables that are typical for the climate and area.

In most climates this will cause affected terrain to hamper movement, causing the target space to have [Mind] level of Difficult Terrain, and additional affects may apply.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Lifeline (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Extending your life force out to encompass a friend, you protect them from harm while taking the brunt of any attacks made against them.

Action: 1 AP, which may be as an Interrupt (the target is hit by an attack).

**Target**: An ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target is linked to you until the end of the scene. While the target is linked to you if they would lose Health instead you lose that much Health and they lose no Health. The link ends if you become incapacitated or otherwise lose consciousness, and you may end the link at any time.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Might 1, Rapport 1

**Reckoning (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Judging the deeds of an enemy, you force their guilt to weigh heavily on them.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Mental Defense. If it hits they are Crippled 3 times, or 4 if you flipped a Diamond on the spell attack.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Sculpt Stone (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You manipulate and shape the earth around you, allowing you to quickly break the earth to foil pursuit. Given time, however, you can work stone and dirt into sturdy walls and other formations with nothing other than your magic.

**Action**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Up to [twice your Mind] square meters (spaces) of within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters. Each space must be next to or made of earth or stone, although you may stack them next to other target spaces as long as at least one is next to earth or stone.

**Effects**: You shape earth in the target areas, moving earth and stone either into those spaces or out of them. This lets you either create or remove stone barricades and the like. Most of the time, a barricade 1 meter high will provide Light Cover and a barricade 2 meters high will provide Total Cover.

If you spend a full minute casting this Spell, instead you can shape the target earth into walls, pillars, sculptures, or anything else you can conceive of. Simple structures can be made without any special knowledge, but anything complex or detailed will be limited by your Craft Skill Total as though you were making it with Craft.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Weatherworking (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Speaking to the earth, wind, and sky, you can wield the power of the weather itself, bringing rain to a parched land or even calming a raging storm.

**Action**: 1 hour; or 3 AP if you spend 1 Focus to cast the Spell.

**Target**: An area [Mind] kilometers in radius, centered on you.

**Effects**: You change the weather of the target area, starting 3 rounds (~15 seconds) after you finish casting the Spell and lasting for the encounter. Make a Theurgy flip, then choose the power of the weather change you want to make, which will affect the difficulty of the flip.

**Minor effect (Difficulty 9)**: Light rain, wind, cloud cover, and so on. No specific effects.

**Moderate effect (Difficulty 13)**: Fog, thick clouds, generally more substantial versions of minor effects. Can create Light Cover in the affected area, or cause similar effects.

**Major effect (Difficulty 17)**: Effects taken to an extreme - near-hurricane winds, torrential rains, impenetrable fog. Can create Heavy Cover in the affected area, alter the terrain (flooding, etc.), and may distort weather patterns for the near future.

For each Extra Success you get on the flip, you may choose to either increase the area of effect by an additional [Mind] kilometers or increase the duration by an extra hour.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 2

**Cloak of Awe (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Laying a mantle of flaring light over an ally, you stagger and shake enemies, driving them away from your friends.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of each enemy within 3 meters of the target. Each enemy hit is Frightened of the target for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Presence 2, Integrity 1

**Cloak of Command (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Wrapping an ally with bands of compelling power, you draw your enemies' attention away from others and towards them instead.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of each enemy within 3 meters of the target. Each enemy hit is Taunted by the target for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Presence 2, Integrity 1

**Entangle (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause tendrils of vines and grass to burst from the ground, grasping at the feet of your foes and holding them fast to the ground.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters. You may spend 1 Focus to cast the Spell, and if you do you may target any number of additional targets.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Slowed [Mind] times.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Survival 3

**Wild Shape (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell]**

Your connection to nature is so strong that you may borrow the forms of wilderness animals, transforming into a wide variety of animals on a whim.

**Action**: 4 AP.

**Effects**: You transform into an animal; if you're already in an animal form, you may either transform into another or back to your normal form. When you transform you may choose to drop any items carried or worn by you, and all other items meld into your new shape, reappearing when you transform back.

To transform into an animal you must be familiar with it, and it may not have Combat Proficiency higher than [your Theurgy Skill Total] .  You retain your Health Levels and Focus, but otherwise use the statistics of the animal. You may use any abilities of your new form, such as water breathing or flight, as well as any of its attacks. You cannot use any other abilities, although the GM may decide that some abilities can still be used if they relate specifically to knowledge instead of physical ability or spellcasting.

You use the animal's Skill Totals instead of your own, and any unlisted Skill has a total of +0. You may use your own Skill Totals for anything related only to knowledge, such as most uses of Streetwise or Academics.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Survival 3, Insight 3

**Earthquake (Level 5) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Reaching into the depths of the earth with your power, you agitate the ground into a state of turmoil and uproar, quaking powerfully in a concentrated area and bringing anything on the surface crashing down.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Target**: An area 10 meters in radius, centered on a point within 20 meters of you but which you don't need to be able to see.

**Effects**: You create powerful tremors in the target area, lasting for the encounter. All terrain affected by this Spell has 1 level of Difficult Terrain in addition to specific effects based on the features of the terrain. If a creature is in the area when the Spell begins, moves or is Pushed into the area, or ends their turn in the area, they are Crippled twice and Harried twice.

At the end of the encounter, the affected area may suffer permanent damage as a result of the tremors. A cave, tunnel, cliff, building, or other structure or vertical formation is damaged and potentially collapsed by the spell, with the exception of extremely sturdy structures. Trees may be toppled, small hills leveled, and things like cobblestone roads will be thrown into disrepair.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Nature Lore 5, Spirit 5

**Spiritual Intervention (Level 5) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

As a sturdy boulder in the frantic storm of the world, you extend your magic to bend the fabric of existence itself.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: When you cast this Spell, choose another Theurgy Spell no higher than level 4 that you know. This Spell then works just like the chosen Spell, although you must pay any costs the chosen Spell has in addition to this Spell's.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Spirit 5, Integrity 5

**Learning**: You may only learn this Spell once.

## Theurgy
*Source: `theurgy_1.html` (Part 1)*

**Alarm Ward (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

Tracing runes of warding and laying lines of salt, you can ward an area to alert you and your allies if outsiders try to sneak up to your location.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Target**: A spherical area up to 25 meters in radius.

**Effects**: The target area is warded for up to 8 hours, and when you create the ward you can choose any number of people that will not trigger the ward. When any other person enters the warded area, the alarm does any number of the following, chosen when you create the ward:

Mentally warns you, rousing you from any sleep, but otherwise makes no noise

Mentally warns all people within the ward, rousing them from any sleep, but otherwise making no noise

Emits a loud chiming noise that can be clearly heard within the warded area but nowhere else

Emits a loud chiming noise that can be clearly heard for at least a mile

You may only have one ward at a given time.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2, Academics 1

**Birdsight (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell] [Re-Learning]**

You enchant a winged beast of nature to temporarily borrow its sight, scouting out the area from above.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Effects**: This Spell Summons a

Small Bird

or other small, flying animal to your side for up to one hour. You may have one such animal Summoned at a time for each time you know this Spell. If the bird is more than [your Theurgy Skill Total] kilometers from you, the spell ends.

While Summoned, as a Quick action you may switch from seeing through your eyes to instead see from the eyes of the bird, using its relevant Skills while doing so. As a Quick action you may switch back. The spell also allows you to telepathically communicate with the bird, also allowing you to give it a turn at great distances.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2, Nature Lore 1

**From the Brink of Death (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell]**

With small threads of power you stitch together someone's wounds just enough to stabilize them.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: An adjacent ally.

**Effects**: Your target heals any Health they are missing from their Downed Health level.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2

**Windweaving (Level 1) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Channeling power out and into the air itself, you cause breezes and gusts to aid your allies in a variety of ways.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: Yourself or an ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters. You may spend 1 Focus to cast the Spell, and if you do you may affect up to [Theurgy Skill Total] additional targets.

**Effects**: You create a sphere of clean, fresh air around the head of the target that lasts for the scene. This sphere is constantly full of fresh, uncontaminated, breathable air and will displace any liquids or gases around it, allowing the target to breathe easily in anything from a cloud of poison gas to the bottom of a river.

You may spend 1 AP to cast the Spell as an Interrupt (the target begins falling), and if you do instead of its normal effects you slow the target's descent until they land on solid ground, and they take no damage from the fall.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 2,  Survival 1

**Beast Venom (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

An ethereal beast's fang strikes a foe within range, driving venom deep into their veins.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Resistance Defense. If the attack hits, choose either

Crippling Poison

,

Deadly Poison

, or

Weakening Poison

, and the target is affected by the chosen poison.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Nature Lore 2

**Door of Respite (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell]**

You create a "door" that acts as a portal to a portion of the extradimensional realms, giving you a small shelter in which to rest while safe from hazards.

**Action**: 10 minutes.

**Effects**: A "door" that is normally-sized for you and made from translucent light appears in front of you. This door is only visible to you, although you may "hold it open" for other people to allow them to see it and its doorway while held open. The door is a portal to an extradimensional space that resembles a warm cottage, large tent around a campfire, or whatever you would consider safe and comfortable shelter. The shelter has one entrance which leads back to wherever the door was created, and the only way in or out of the shelter is that door. After 8 hours the door closes, and anything inside the shelter is gently deposited on the ground around where the door was created.

Anyone who gets a full night's rest while inside the shelter heals an extra Health.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Fog Cloud (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

By commanding the rain and winds you can cause fog to suddenly roll in across nearby terrain.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A point within

[Theurgy Skill Total] × 10

meters.

**Effects**: Mists begin to swirl around the target point, and at the start of your next turn the mists form into a cloud of fog with a radius of

[Mind] × 5

meters which lasts for the scene. Anyone trying to see something or make an attack either into the cloud or from within the cloud has Bad Luck on any related flips.

When you cast this Spell, choose a movement pattern for the cloud to follow:

The cloud stays still and does not move

The cloud moves at a slow pace (1 meter per round) in a direction

The cloud moves at a fast pace (5 meters per round) in a direction

The cloud moves at a fast pace (up to 5 meters per round) and attempts to stay centered on you

As a Move action you may change the movement for the cloud to any other pattern. Particularly strong winds or other forces may divert or disperse the fog cloud.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Nature Lore 2

**Gust of Wind (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You create a blast of hurricane-strength wind to scatter your enemies.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: All creatures within a 3-meter-long cone, starting from your space.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of [your Mind] .

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Dodge Defense of each target, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack the Spell's Power is increased by 1. Each target hit by the attack is Pushed back [Power] meters.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Nature Lore 2

**Land's Bounty (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

A staple of druids living in small towns and communes (which is a lot of them), this simple incantation allows a druid to stoke the energy of the land and cause it to bear fruit and other foods in a heartbeat. In a pinch, this can also cause enemy combatants to become bogged down in thick clumps of underbrush and shrubs, making them easy prey for you and your allies.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A circular area 2 meters in radius, centered on a space within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: All plant life in each target space grows as though for an entire season in the span of a round. Food grown this way will reflect the sorts of fruits and vegetables that are typical for the climate and area.

In most climates this will cause affected terrain to hamper movement, causing the target space to have [Power] level of Difficult Terrain, and additional affects may apply.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Nature Lore 2

**Lifeline (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Extending your life force out to encompass a friend, you protect them from harm while taking the brunt of any attacks made against them.

Action: 1 AP, which may be as an Interrupt (the target is hit by an attack).

**Target**: An ally within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: The target is linked to you until the end of the scene.

While the target is linked to you if they would lose Health instead you lose that much Health and they lose no Health.

The link ends if you become incapacitated or otherwise lose consciousness, and you may end the link as a Non-action.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Might 1, Rapport 1

**Ring of Entropy (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

By lacing the ground with glowing runes you create an energy vacuum, absorbing power and strength from anyone in the area.

**Action Points**: 2 AP.

**Target**: A circular area 2 meters in radius, centered on a space within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: You create a runed ring around the target area that lasts for the scene. If a creature starts their turn in, moves into, or is moved into the area, make a Theurgy spell attack against their Resistance Defense, and if you flip a Diamond on the attack the Spell's Power is increased by 1 for the attack. If the attack hits that creature is Crippled and Weakened [Power] times each.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3

**Sculpt Stone (Level 2) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You manipulate and shape the earth around you, allowing you to quickly break the earth to foil pursuit. Given time, however, you can work stone and dirt into sturdy walls and other formations with nothing other than your magic.

**Action**: 1 minute; if you spend 1 Focus you may instead spend 1 AP to cast the Spell.

**Target**: Up t

o [Power] square meters (spaces) of earth or stone within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of [your Mind] .

**Effects**: You shape the target earth into walls, pillars, sculptures, or anything else you can conceive of. Simple structures can be made without any special knowledge, but anything complex or detailed will be limited by your Craft Skill Total as though you were making it with Craft. When shaped, the target earth may be moved up to [Power] meters away, although this may not be used to make attacks of any kind or move the earth into an occupied space.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 3, Nature Lore 2

**Cloak of Awe (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Donning a mantle of flaring light over your shoulders, you stagger and shake your enemies.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Frightened of you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Presence 2, Integrity 2

**Cloak of Command (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Donning a mantle of compelling power, you draw your enemies' attention away from others and towards yourself.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: Any number of creatures within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the Mental Defense of each target. Each target hit is Taunted by you for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Presence 2, Integrity 2

**Commune With Nature (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell]**

Your connection to the earth is strong enough to open all of its secrets to you, divining many details about the surrounding landscape without such primitive methods as exploration.

**Action**: 1 hour; if you spend 1 Focus you may instead spend 1 AP to cast the Spell.

**Effects**: Once you finish casting the spell make a Theurgy flip, difficulty 9. If you succeed you gain information about one of the following subjects:

Existence of water sources

General animal population and prominent species

Earth/terrain type

Any minerals in the earth

Types of vegetation

Presence of humanoids

Powerful unnatural creatures

General state of nature

For each Extra Success on the flip, you may choose another subject on which to gain information.

Any knowledge gained from this Spell applies to all nature within 15 kilometers of your location.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Nature Lore 3

**Entangle (Level 3) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

You cause tendrils of vines and grass to burst from the ground, grasping at the feet of your foes and holding them fast to the ground.

**Action Points**: 1 AP.

**Target**: A creature within [Theurgy Skill Total] meters. You may spend 1 Focus to cast the Spell, and if you do you may target any number of additional targets.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy spell attack against the target's Dodge Defense. If the attack hits, the target is Snared for 1 round.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 4, Nature Lore 3

**Control Weather (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell]**

Speaking to the earth, wind, and sky, you can wield the power of the weather itself, bringing rain to a parched land or even calming a raging storm.

**Action**: 1 hour; if you spend 1 Focus you may instead spend 1 AP to cast the Spell.

**Target**: An area [Mind] kilometers in radius, centered on you.

**Effects**: Make a Theurgy flip to determine the potency of the weather change you can make in the target area, lasting for the scene. The difficulty of the Theurgy flip depends on the potency of the effect you are creating or ending:

**Minor effect**: A minor change in the weather that has a minor, if any, effect on people in the affected area. This includes such effects as creating a clear sky or causing a light rain in the area. Effects of this caliber have a difficulty of 9 to create or end.

**Moderate effect**: Either creating or ending a significant weather event. Effects of this caliber are heavy fogs that obscure sight, heavy downpours that make travel more difficult, and the like; they have a difficulty of 11 to create or end.

**Major effect**: A significant and destructive force of nature, in the ranks of storms which cause rivers to flood and hurricanes with gale-force winds. Effects of this caliber have a difficulty of 15 to create or end.

For each Extra Success you get on the flip, you may choose one of the following:

Increase the area of effect by an additional [Mind] kilometers

Increase the duration by an extra scene (or 5 minutes, whichever is longer)

At the start of your next turn the weather change begins. At the end of the spell's effect the normal weather pattern resumes.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Nature Lore 4

**Earthquake (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Reaching into the depths of the earth with your power, you agitate the ground into a state of turmoil and uproar, quaking powerfully in a concentrated area and bringing anything on the surface crashing down.

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Target**: An area centered on you, roughly

10 × [Theurgy Skill Total]

meters in radius.

**Power**: This Spell has a Power of 1.

**Effects**: You create powerful tremors in the target area, lasting for the scene. All terrain affected by this Spell has [Power] levels of Difficult Terrain in addition to specific effects based on the features of the terrain.

A cave, tunnel, cliff, building, or other structure or vertical formation is collapsed by the spell, with the exception of extremely sturdy structures. Anything collapsed will create rubble underneath it and away from it a distance roughly equal to the height of the structure. Any creature caught in the rubble must make an Acrobatics flip, difficulty 13, or be stuck in the rubble. A creature that begins its turn stuck in the rubble gains a stack of Bleeding, and as a Main action may make an Acrobatics or Might flip, difficulty 13, to escape the rubble.

Bodies of water are partially drained away, turning all but the largest lakes and oceans into muddy sinkholes for the duration of the Spell. After the Spell ends the water will slowly fill back in over the course of several minutes.

Terrain in general will be roughly affected by the Spell aside from the explicit effects listed. Trees may very well be toppled, small hills leveled (even if they would not be considered cliffs, as above), and things like cobblestone roads will be thrown into disrepair almost instantly. All effects of the Spell may not happen instantaneously, and instead occur over the course of the spell's duration.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Nature Lore 4

**Wild Shape (Level 4) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

Your connection to nature is so strong that you may borrow the forms of wilderness animals, transforming into a wide variety of animals on a whim.

**Action Points**: 3 AP.

**Effects**: You transform into an animal, and you must commit this Spell as long as you stay transformed. You may only uncommit this Spell by spending 3 AP, causing you to transform back into your natural form.

You may only transform into an animal that you are familiar with, and it may not have any Stats or Skills higher than [your Mind] . You may use any natural abilities of your new form such, as water breathing or flight. You keep access to any Skills or Techniques you know, but you cannot effectively wear or wield items in any item slot except for Body slot items designed specifically for your new form.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Nature Lore 4

**Spiritual Intervention (Level 5) [Theurgy Spell] [Encounter]**

As a sturdy boulder in the frantic storm of the world, you extend your magic to bend the fabric of existence itself.

**Action**: 1 AP - Interrupt (whenever you want).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: When you cast this Spell, choose another Theurgy Spell no higher than level 4 that you know. This Spell functions as the chosen Spell, except as noted for this Spell, although you must pay all costs of the chosen Spell.

**Prereqs**: Theurgy 5, Spirit 5, Integrity 5

**Learning**: You may only learn this Spell once.

---

## Willful
*Source: `willful.html` (Part 1)*

Willful abilities represent a character's strength of will, sharp mind, and discipline that lets them push themselves beyond normal limits. As they strive and shape themselves, they can shape the world around them as well.

**Follower (Level 1)**

You have gained the trust and loyalty of a squire, apprentice, or other follower who helps you on your adventures. They aren't capable of holding their own in combat or other situations, but you can always rely on them to help where they can.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You gain a follower, an independent character (controlled by the GM) but who will generally listen to you, follow your orders, is trustworthy, and thinks well of you. They have an effective Skill Total of 0 for all actions, can carry a small pack, and may perform simple tasks with no chance of failure. Your follower cannot directly participate in combat, and will hang back or hide if combat breaks out around them. They generally will not be in any mortal danger, and will usually end up fleeing if put into a situation where they would otherwise die.

If for any reason you and your follower part ways, you may gain the same follower, a similar follower, or a new follower, depending on the situation and the GM's discretion.

**Prereqs**: (Presence or Rapport) 3

**Follower: Knack (Level 1) [Re-Learning]**

Your heroics and expertise have pushed your follower to follow in your footsteps, developing their own abilities.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Choose one of these knacks for your follower to learn:

**Contraband Handler**: Using a large jacket, heavy cloak, or similar garment your follower is good at hiding small objects. They can hide an object with a weight of up to 1 on their person, and it will only be found by a thorough and invasive search (the sort that is only given when one is arrested by the authorities, for example).

**Strong Shoulders**: Your follower can carry almost as much equipment as a mule or ox could, including multiple bedrolls and backpacks.

**Scavenger**: While traveling or going about business, your follower can spend their time scavenging for raw materials. During this time they cannot do anything except scavenge. After a full day of this, they may turn up a Level 1 material.

**Grifter's Eye**: Your follower knows how to run some con games, grifts, and/or fortune tellings. In addition your follower can spend most of a day grifting in an area, returning to you at the end. They will share some of their spoils, which will often be things other than money, and never more than 1 Gold.

**Medical Training**: Your follower can tend to a creature while it rests, and that creature heals an extra 1 Health if it gets a full night's rest while being tended to.

**Guild Member**: If you are a member of a guild or organization, your follower is authorized and trained to do official business on your behalf and can represent you in your absence.

**Squire**: Your follower carries a set of weaponry, ready at a moment's notice. For 1 AP, you command your follower to bring a weapon to you or an ally within 5 meters of you. Your follower can only lend out one weapon at a time.

**Elemental Adept**: Your follower knows minor elemental magic, enough to boil water and ignite items such as tinder or cloth, or chill items as though kept in an ice chest.

(If you and your follower part ways and you acquire a new follower, your GM may allow you to change which ability you have chosen for this Technique to better suit the new follower.)

**Prereqs**: Follower

**Soulblade (Level 1) [Re-Learning]**

Through mystical training, force of will, or some other mental regimen you have unlocked the ability to forge your spiritual force into a solid weapon.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique choose a specific type of weapon, such as One-Handed Power Melee.

For 0 AP at any time you create a weapon of the chosen type, which appears in your hand(s). If the weapon leaves your hands, it disappears, although if thrown it will complete the action first.

**Prereqs**: (Integrity or Meditation) 3

Temper Soulblade (Level [Varies]) [Re-Learning]

As you learn lessons they are etched into your soul, and as you express your spiritual weaponry its form changes to reflect your newfound wisdom.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique choose a Masterwork power as though you were creating a new item. This Technique's Level is equal to the Level of that Masterwork power.

When you create a weapon with Soulblade, you may choose one of the Masterwork powers chosen for this Technique which could apply to the Soulblade's weapon. That weapon is created with the chosen Masterwork power. The copy of enhancement is shared across any and all weapons created with it, and any limits on its use apply to all of them.

****Prereqs**: (Integrity or Meditation) [this Technique's Level] Vow of Poverty (Level 1)**

You have forsworn all forms of material excess and wealth, and vowed to give all you can to the needy. As time goes on, your generosity and dedication teach you many lessons.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You may not own or use any luxury items without breaking this vow. Luxury items are generally any items aside from weapons, armor, or goods required for your profession, self-defense, or daily life. This also includes items with a Level of 2 or higher. The GM might include or exclude other items as appropriate. You may not keep any amount of money above 3 Gold for an extended period of time - including jewelry, precious metals, or similar valuables - unless it is for the specific purpose of being donated.

You may donate money or goods to a worthy cause. When you do, for every 4 Gold worth of donated items you gain 1 Experience. A worthy cause must be one that needs your donation, such as a house of religion or orphanage, and you cannot receive any other reward for the donation.

If you willingly break your vow, until you atone you have Bad Luck on all Presence, Persuasion, and Rapport flips, and may not donate to gain Experience. To atone, you must donate 20 gold worth of money or goods to your worthy cause.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 2, Academics 1

**Grim Resolve (Level 2)**

When you get backed into a corner, you push yourself to your absolute limits to do what needs to be done.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 3 Health.

**Effects**: You draw a card.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 3, Might 2

**Lucky (Level 2)**

Even in the strangest of times, things just seem to go your way.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Look at the top card of your deck, then you may discard it. Then draw a card.

**Prereqs**: (Insight, Streetwise, Survival, or Academics) 3, (Acrobatics, Athletics, or Might) 2

**Roll the Dice (Level 2)**

You keep your wits about you, no matter when. If the situation starts to go south, you're already halfway out the figurative (or literal) door with a brand-new plan.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus, discard a card.

**Effects**: You draw 2 cards.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3, Streetwise 2

**Solemn Pact (Level 2) [Encounter]**

You have sworn a pact - a pledge of fealty to your god, a promise to exchange favors with a demon of the Abyss, or a dedication to a set of beliefs and ideals. It might be that this pact guides your life and encourages you towards a more virtuous existence, or perhaps that your patron grants you a measure of power in exchange for your service to their ends.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you make a flip of the chosen type, with the chosen Skill).

**Effects**: When you learn this Technique, choose a Skill and either attack flips or non-attack flips.

You gain Good Luck on the chosen flip.

**Pact**:

When you learn this Technique choose the details of your pact, such as if the pact is to a specific patron (such as a deity, devil, or something else) or a set of ideals. Regardless of the specifics, there must be certain values or duties for you to uphold. At the GM's discretion, this Technique can be revoked if you break your vows or if your patron no longer considers you a worthy servant. If this happens you lose the benefits of this Technique (although you still keep the Technique) until you properly atone and show forgiveness for your misdeeds, or until you find a new patron or pact to swear.

**Prereqs**: (Integrity or Academics) 3

**Take the Initiative (Level 2)**

By training your instinct and reflexes you have attained a reaction speed that ensures you are rarely caught off-guard.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: You have Good Luck on Reflex flips.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3

**Morning's Determination (Level 3)**

When you wake up each day, you always remember what needs doing. Every new day reminds you of your goals and duties, and this gives you the drive to persevere in the face of any hardship.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: After you get a full night's rest, when you would discard your cards, instead you may shuffle any of those cards back into your deck, then discard all but one of your remaining cards.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 3, (Might or Resilience) 2

**Primal Intuition (Level 3)**

You spit in the face of fate, playing by your own rules.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (any time).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: Look at the top [Insight Skill Total] cards of your deck, discard any number of them, then shuffle your deck.

**Special**: Whenever you get a full night's rest, after you discard your cards but before you draw new cards for the day, you must shuffle your discard pile into your deck.

**Prereqs**: Insight 3, Integrity 2, Resilience 2

**Solemn Perseverance (Level 3)**

"I am your loyal servant, my master. Failure is not an option; I live to do your bidding, and no mortal agents can stay my course. Lend me your power and I shall see your will executed upon this world."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: The first time you lose Health each scene, you gain the following:

You are Bolstered once.

You are Protected 3 times.

Remove up to 2 total stacks of Bleeding, Crippled, Frightened, Slowed, Taunted, or Vulnerable from yourself.

**Pact**:

If you break your pact, you lose the benefits of this Technique (although you still keep the Technique) until you properly atone and show forgiveness for your misdeeds, or until you find a new patron or pact to swear.

**Prereqs**: Solemn Pact, (Resilience or Presence) 3

**Lay On Hands (Level 4)**

Those with sufficient courage and will are capable of stoking their own life's flame to burn bright and use that to comfort their allies and burn away any impurities.

**Action**: 1 AP.

**Cost**: 1 charge of this Technique (see below).

**Target**: Yourself or an adjacent willing creature.

**Effects**: Choose one of the following:

The target heals 1 Health

Remove up to 2 Bleeds from the target

Reduce the effects of each poison afflicting the target as though an hour had passed.

**Special**: After you get a full night's rest, you may spend any amount of Focus. For each Focus spent you gain [half your Integrity Skill total] charges for this Technique, which last until you get a full night's rest.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 3, Rapport 3, Presence 3

**Mind and Body Unbroken (Level 4)**

Through sheer force of will you can force your body to carry on despite what would ordinarily be crippling wounds.

**Action**: 0 AP - Interrupt (you would become Downed).

**Cost**: 1 Focus.

**Effects**: You are not Downed, and instead your total Health becomes 3 in your 'Maimed (-3)' Health level.

**Prereqs**: Integrity 5, Resilience 4

**Solemn Covenant (Level 4)**

"As witnessed by the divine host, I solemnly pledge with all my heart -

To be kind: that I might love my fellow man thoroughly and completely, that I will stand before those in danger and defend them, that I shall show mercy and forgiveness to all men, even those with evil in their hearts.

To be strong: that the fires of conviction might burn bright within me, that I might fight for what I believe, that I can stay my hand and forgive an enemy, that I will hold firm and correct an ally's misdeed.

To be good: that I know this world is full of darkness, that all men including myself hold wickedness in their hearts, and that I might through my actions be a flame that burns brightly enough to hold evil at bay.

I pledge my life to the cause. My arm is sworn to protect what is most important. Lend me your strength, and I will fight to my last breath to uphold my vow."

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: The first time each scene you use a Level 3 or lower Encounter Technique, do not expend it.

**Pact**:

If you break your pact, you lose the benefits of this Technique (although you still keep the Technique) until you properly atone and show forgiveness for your misdeeds, or until you find a new patron or pact to swear.

**Prereqs**: Solemn Perseverance, (Any Skill) 5

**Mastermind (Level 5)**

A strong natural intelligence tempered by carefully cultivated willpower has given you a superior ability to balance many acts that would require the entire concentration of a lesser mind.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: Your maximum Focus is increased by 1.

**Prereqs**: (Academics, Craft, Medicine, or Mixology) 5, Integrity 5, Mind 5

**Preternatural Reflexes (Level 5)**

You keep your razor sharp wits about you at all times, and you react to danger the instant it presents itself. It is impossible to catch you off-guard, and as soon as any conflict breaks out you are leaping into action while everyone else is still drawing their arms.

**Passive**: This Technique applies to you at all times.

**Effects**: When a combat begins, if either there would not normally be a surprise round, or if there would be but you would not be included in it, instead there is a surprise round and you are part of it. You only get 2 AP at the start of combat for such a round.

You are always aware of attacks against your Dodge or Parry Defense. This means that you cannot be prevented from applying those Defenses against an attack

**Prereqs**: Awareness 5, Insight 5, Integrity 5
